# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — 00C: HISTORICAL INTELLIGENCE REGISTRY (ENTRIES #1–#38), 9-YEAR CYCLE LAWS & EXECUTION INVARIANTS
**Provenance:** Consolidated & deduplicated from Crypto & Macro Master Intelligence Log (Sources 00 & 04) plus live Q4 2026 empirical telemetry ledger (`Entry #38`) to preserve foundational historical lessons without duplicating `MOD 01–60`.

---

#### 1. Foundational Cycle & Structural Laws (Entries #1–#34 Distilled)
1. **9-Year Empirical Cycle Top Architecture (Entry #1):**
   * Derived from 3,341 daily candles and 107 All-Time Highs: secular bull peaks require multi-pillar saturation across on-chain unrealized profit (MVRV), long-term holder distribution velocity, and macro liquidity peaks (Cluster B).
2. **Primacy of Horizontal Liquidity Pools Over Lagging Geometric Charting (Entries #10 & #31):**
   * Diagonal trendlines and lagging oscillators have zero causal force; institutional execution targets **horizontal resting liquidity pools** (equal highs/lows, Volume Profile VAH/VAL/POC, and liquidation clusters).
3. **Q4 2026 vs. Q4 2018 Structural Macro Analogue (Entry #25):**
   * Cost-push energy/tariff inflation shocks combined with restrictive central-bank policy force prolonged multiple compression and high-beta drawdowns until a clear Federal Reserve liquidity pivot occurs.
4. **The 72-Hour Macro Trinity Gate (Entry #33):**
   * Major scheduled macro prints (**FOMC, CPI, PCE, NFP**) create a 72-hour volatility distortion window (`T-24h` pre-positioning chop, `T-0` algorithmic whip, `T+24h` true institutional repricing — codified in `MOD 58`).
5. **Permanent VPIN Exclusion Directive:**
   * The Volume-Synchronized Probability of Toxicity (**VPIN**) model is permanently excluded from all scoring and execution engines in favor of **Rough Path Signatures (Order N <= 2 Lévy Signed Areas)** and **Domestic USD vs. Offshore USDT Spot CVD**.

---

#### 2. Exact Microstructure Bar-Count & Asymmetry Invariants (From Source 04)
To prevent premature entries and poor reward-to-risk setups during `MOD 11` (Volume Profile VAL/VAH sweeps), `MOD 54` (Algorithmic Market Structure BOS), and `MOD 18` execution:

##### A. Timeframe Bar-Count Scaling Matrix (1H & 4H Spot Swing Calibration)
| Execution Phase | 1H Spot Swing Calibration | 4H Spot Swing Calibration | Structural Disqualification (Mandatory Abort) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Phase 1: Sweep & Reclaim Duration** | **1 to 3 completed 1H bars** below support before closing firmly back inside the range. | **1 to 2 completed 4H bars** (single long lower-wick hammer or 2-bar reversal) closing back inside range. | **True Breakdown Acceptance:** Price closes and holds below support for **4+ consecutive 1H bars** or **2+ consecutive 4H bars** (Lock 100% Cash). |
| **Phase 2: Low-Volume Retest Window** | **2 to 6 completed 1H bars** after the reclaim candle closes (contracting sell volume + narrowing spreads). | **1 to 3 completed 4H bars** after the reclaim candle closes. | **Runaway V-Bottom:** Price rallies straight to mid-range POC without retesting, degrading R:R below 3:1 (Pass — Do Not Chase). |

##### B. Minimum 3:1 Asymmetry Filter (`Net R >= 3.0` Law — `MOD 18` & `MOD 37`)
* Before authorizing any unleveraged spot entry (`0.75%` or `0.375%` risk ceiling):
  * `Stop_Distance = Entry_Price - Hard_Invalidation_Wick_Low`
  * `Target_1 = Range Point of Control (POC)` | `Target_2 = Value Area High (VAH)`
  * **Hard Gate:** If `(Target_2 - Entry_Price) / Stop_Distance < 3.0` post-slippage, **PASS UNCONDITIONALLY** (insufficient asymmetric payoff).

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC: THE SOVEREIGN BITCOIN BRAIN MATRIX
**Architectural Charter, Mission Manifesto & Operational Blueprint (v1.0)**

## 1. Opening Message & Governing Doctrine
Most market participants fail not because they lack charts or news feeds, but because they rely on discretionary human emotion at the exact moment the market is engineered to break them. When volatility strikes, the unassisted human mind falls prey to FOMO, panic, narrative rationalization, premature candle-close entries, and the fatal hope that a losing trade will magically recover. At the same time, asking an unconstrained AI to "predict Bitcoin's price" produces generic guesses and hallucinated opinions.

**Project CORTEX-BTC (The Sovereign Bitcoin Brain Matrix)** is a Neuro-Symbolic Deterministic Decision Engine—a "Second Brain" that encodes validated, causal market mechanics during times of calm so that three, six, or twelve months from now, any live daily or on-demand Bitcoin telemetry snapshot can be scanned against the Matrix to derive a rigorous, emotionless **Probable Structural Verdict** and **Non-Negotiable Execution Protocol**.

> **The Governing Doctrine:** *Build in Peace. Scan in War. Never re-learn a market lesson at a financial loss.*

---

## 2. System Identity & Nomenclature
* **Full System Name:** **Project CORTEX-BTC: The Sovereign Bitcoin Brain Matrix** (*Causal Order-Flow, Rough-Path & Telemetry Execution Matrix for BTC*)
* **Core Knowledge Units:** **Conditional Signal Units (`CSU`)** — Standardized `"If Condition A -> Then Observed Outcome B -> Enforce Execution Rule C"` state blocks.
* **Dynamic State Trackers:** **Regime Mutation Matrices (`M1–M8`)** — Pre-mapped transition rules that detect in real time when a setup is strengthening, stalling, or failing.
* **Capital Defense Layer:** **The 0.75% Sovereign Shield** — The mathematical risk-sizing law ensuring spot-only preservation of capital with zero direct leverage.

## 3. Mission & Vision
* **Mission:** Eliminate human emotional interference and AI hallucination from Bitcoin spot swing trading and sovereign wealth management by converting verified macro, order-flow, path-geometry, derivatives, and on-chain mechanics into an auditable state machine—where every live market condition maps deterministically to either a **100% Cash Lock**, a **0.75% risk-capped unleveraged spot execution**, or a **sovereign cold-storage posture**.
* **Vision:** Establish an evergreen, self-compounding institutional intelligence engine—operable from anywhere in the world, even from a mobile smartphone without a laptop—where every historical market trap, liquidity shock, and structural anomaly is permanently codified into an interconnected Brain Matrix managed jointly by **Gemini AI**, **Google NotebookLM**, **Hermes AI Agent**, and **GitHub**.

## 4. The Three Quantitative Laws of the Matrix
1. **Causal Orthogonality (Zero Noise):** Rejects lagging retail indicators and stores only validated, causal mechanics:
   * Who is forced to buy or sell (derivatives liquidations, open interest, and basis).
   * Who is absorbing on the tape (domestic fiat USD vs. offshore USDT spot order flow).
   * Where value is accepted (Volume Profile VAH/VAL/POC and on-chain realized cost basis).
   * How multi-dimensional paths curve (Rough Path Signatures, Order N <= 2 Lévy Signed Areas).
2. **Rolling Percentile & Z-Score Normalization (Zero Decay):** Thresholds are anchored to rolling historical percentiles and Z-scores so rules remain calibrated whether Bitcoin trades at $60,000 or $150,000, using raw historical figures only as empirical benchmarks.
3. **Probable Outcome + Mandatory Mutation Watch:** Every scan outputs a 3-Part Verdict:
   * **Part 1 — Probable Structural Trajectory (`Outcome B`):** Highest-probability path based on trapped counterparty liquidity.
   * **Part 2 — Non-Negotiable Action (`Rule C`):** Either **100% Cash Standby** or an **Unleveraged Spot Entry** sized strictly to the **0.75% risk formula**:
     `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Hard Structural Invalidation Price)`
   * **Part 3 — Active Mutation Trigger (`M1–M8`):** Exact real-time telemetry shift that warns when the probable outcome is mutating or failing.

## 5. The 5-Pillar Decision Waterfall & Anti-Trap Circuit Breakers

### The 5-Pillar Hierarchy
1. **Pillar 1 (Macro Permissions & Regimes):** Sovereign bond yields, DXY trajectory, global liquidity impulse, stablecoin dry powder, and 200D/200W secular moving averages dictate whether risk is permitted or 100% Cash must be locked.
2. **Pillar 2 (Microstructure, Order Flow & Path Geometry):** Domestic Fiat (USD) vs. Offshore (USDT) Spot CVD, Perpetual CVD, 15m/1H Volume Profile (VAH, VAL, POC), Limit Order-Book Absorption, and Rough Path Signatures (Order N <= 2 Lévy Signed Areas).
3. **Pillar 3 (Derivatives & Market Fragility):** Open Interest 7D expansion/contraction, Perpetual Basis discounts/premiums, 72H Funding Rate Z-scores, and liquidation clusters.
4. **Pillar 4 (Portfolio Engineering & Risk Shield):** The 0.75% Maximum Portfolio Risk Ceiling, Garman-Klass volatility stop calibration, Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocols, and Cross-Pillar Gate Degradation breakers.
5. **Pillar 5 (On-Chain Cohorts & Sovereign Defense):** Short-Term Holder (STH) & Long-Term Holder (LTH) Realized Price, MVRV bands, SOPR 1.0 resets, Spot ETF net creation/redemption flows, and multi-vendor hardware multi-sig custody.

### Mandatory Anti-Trap Circuit Breakers
* **CB-01 (No Premature Candle Closes):** Never buy a daily or 4H close until resting limit-order absorption and footprint reclaim (VAH/VAL) are verified.
* **CB-02 (Venue Purity Split):** Never mistake positive offshore USDT spot churn for institutional accumulation if domestic USD gateways (Coinbase/Kraken/ETFs) are distributing.
* **CB-03 (Forced-Flow Stand-Down):** Automatically lock 100% Cash during passive index rebalancings or active cascading margin liquidations until Open Interest stabilizes.
* **CB-04 (Signal Half-Life Filter):** Never allow short-lived terminal noise to override higher-timeframe Pillar 1 and Pillar 5 structural regimes.

Verify that **Domestic Fiat Gateways (Coinbase/Kraken BTC-USD)** exhibit positive Spot CVD or a rising positive Coinbase Premium alongside net spot taker buying, and verify that CME Open Interest is not collapsing against offshore leverage expansion.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Order-Book Absorption Verification:** Do not buy a candle close simply because basis is in the bottom 1% of history. Limit-order absorption must be confirmed on the 15-minute/1-hour footprint (selling volume failing to displace price downward at Value Area Low or high-volume support nodes).
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Forced-Liquidation Stand-Down:** If Open Interest is actively puking downward on red candles (long liquidation cascade in progress), all spot buy orders are frozen until Open Interest contraction decelerates and funding/basis volatility stabilizes.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-01A: The Spot-Backed Short Squeeze ("The August 19 Precedent")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. 7-day Open Interest expands significantly (`>= +5%` or `+1.5 Z-score`; benchmark `+22,000 BTC`) while Perpetual Basis remains in the bottom 1st–5th historical percentile (`<= -0.025%` to `-0.06%`) for `>= 72 hours`.
  2. **Perpetual CVD** slopes steeply negative (7-day futures net taker flow deeply negative, e.g., `-$1.1B`), confirming aggressive leveraged shorting.
  3. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** and net spot taker flow remain positive (spot absorbing futures selling), and price holds flat or prints higher lows above the 15m/1H Volume Profile Value Area Low (VAL).
  4. **Rough Path Signature (Verdict 0):** Price–Volume and Spot CVD–OI Lévy Signed Area confirms spot flow is leading contract creation with positive upward path curvature.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Unleveraged institutional spot buyers are absorbing every market-sell order fired by leveraged perpetual short sellers. Because every perpetual contract requires a 1:1 counterparty, the Open Interest surge consists of trapped leveraged shorts sitting underwater against a rising spot floor.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Any upward spot catalyst forces sequential short margin breaches and automated market-buy liquidations (mirroring the August 19 precedent at `$69,335` that drove a `+14%` 7-day rally).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon a verified 15-minute/1-hour footprint **Value Area High (VAH) reclaim** (or Developing POC breakout) accompanied by positive Domestic USD Spot CVD.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop immediately below the absorption swing low / Volume Profile VAL where limit bid absorption was verified.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% into the short-squeeze vertical impulse when **Perpetual Basis crosses above 0.00% into positive territory** AND **Open Interest drops by 5% to 8%** (confirming trapped shorts have been forcibly covered). Move stop on remaining 50% to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind the 4H Volume Profile VAL or close fully if Domestic USD Spot CVD diverges negatively while Perp Basis spikes into overheated positive territory (`> +0.03%`).

### CSU-01B: The Passive Long-Trap Cascade ("Adler Correction Risk")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. 7-day Open Interest expands (`>= +5%` or `+1.5 Z-score`) with Perpetual Basis negative (`<= -0.025%`) for `>= 72 hours`.
  2. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** slopes **negative or flat** (both spot and futures show aligned net taker outflows, e.g., 24H futures `-$1.2B` and spot `-$83.3M`, or CME OI drops while offshore OI rises).
  3. Price fails to reclaim the 1H Developing POC and breaks below the multi-day Volume Profile Value Area Low (VAL) on expanding volume.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional spot buyers have stepped aside. The 1:1 long side of the Open Interest build-up is held by **offshore leveraged passive futures limit-buyers** borrowing into a falling market to catch a falling knife.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** With no genuine domestic spot bid floor beneath the market, aggressive perp selling pushes price below the margin maintenance threshold of passive futures longs, triggering a cascading **long-liquidation flush**.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH (Zero Spot Long Exposure).** If holding an existing spot swing position, execute an immediate protective exit upon the loss of the 1H VAL accompanied by negative Domestic Spot CVD.
  * **Knife-Catching Ban:** Do not place resting spot limit bids during the breakdown. Wait for **Module 03 (Deleveraging Flush & OI Reset)** conditions—specifically an Open Interest wipeout of `> 8% to 12%` and verified post-flush Domestic USD Spot CVD step-in.

### CSU-01C: Institutional Delta-Neutral Basis Compression (Synthetic Chop)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Open Interest rises steadily while Perpetual Basis sits moderately negative (`-0.01%` to `-0.035%`), while dated quarterly/CME futures maintain a standard money-market contango premium (`+5.3%` to `+5.7%` annualized).
  2. Spot ETF netflows or OTC spot purchases match the notional increase in short futures Open Interest.
  3. Garman-Klass (OHLC) volatility compresses into the bottom 20th percentile; price oscillates tightly around the Volume Profile Point of Control (POC).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  The Open Interest expansion is driven by delta-neutral institutional hedging or cross-venue basis arbitrage rather than directional speculation. Upward price ticks will not trigger panic short-covering cascades because short futures legs are hedged by spot holdings.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY / 100% CASH PRESERVATION.** Do not deploy swing capital inside a delta-neutral POC chop zone. Wait for a Garman-Klass volatility expansion paired with a directional Rough Path Signature breakout above VAH or below VAL.

### CSU-01D: Orderly Deleveraging & Mean Reversion (Signal Reset)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Perpetual Basis rises from its extreme discount back to `0.00%` or slightly positive.
  2. Total Open Interest contracts gradually by `5% to 8%+` over 48–96 hours **without** a violent price spike or crash.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Both leveraged longs and shorts have voluntarily closed their positions into neutral liquidity. The coiled spring has been disarmed without a liquidation cascade.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **DEACTIVATE MODULE 01 WATCH.** Cancel all squeeze-dependent pending triggers and transition the scanner to **Module 05 (Spot vs. Perp CVD Matrix)** and **Module 11 (Volume Profile Topology)** for standard trend evaluation.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 02 can authorize a spot action, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Pillar 1 Macro Permission Gate:** Verify that secular macro permissions (200-Day Moving Average, sovereign yields, DXY, and stablecoin supply impulse) permit risk exposure. If offshore retail traders are paying 30%+ annualized funding while US spot venues show net selling, the rally is paper-driven and fragile.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Passive Ask-Wall & Price-Displacement Audit:** If Open Interest and Perpetual CVD are surging upward (`+Delta`) while price stalls below a High-Volume Node (HVN) or Value Area High (VAH), institutional spot holders are using passive limit asks to distribute into retail FOMO longs.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Leverage Crowding Ceiling:** When 8-hour funding exceeds the **95th rolling percentile** without a corresponding Spot CVD surge, new spot entries are strictly prohibited; focus shifts exclusively to **protecting open swing gains**.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-02A: Spot-Backed Leveraged Breakout ("Healthy Trend Expansion")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. 7-day Open Interest expands (`>= +5%` or `+1.5 Z-score`) with Perpetual Basis elevated (`+0.025% to +0.050%`, 85th–92nd percentile), reflecting strong directional demand.
  2. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** and US Spot ETF netflows slope **steeply positive**, outpacing or matching Perpetual CVD slope.
  3. **Rough Path Signature (Verdict 0):** Price–Volume and Spot CVD–OI Lévy Signed Area exhibits positive curvature (`Spot leading OI`), and price holds cleanly above the reclaimed 1H/4H **Value Area High (VAH)**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Genuine unleveraged institutional capital is bidding spot higher, pulling perpetual futures into a healthy contango premium. Because spot buyers own the underlying float outright, they are immune to margin liquidations on minor pullbacks.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sustained multi-day trend expansion toward the next higher-timeframe Low-Volume Node (LVN) or liquidity target, punctuated by brief shallow dips that flush excess leverage without breaking structural spot support.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** only on a verified **15m/1H pullback retest of the reclaimed VAH or Developing POC** where Domestic USD Spot CVD remains positive and initial weak longs have been shaken out. (Never buy the vertical top wick).
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop immediately below the reclaimed 4H Value Area High (VAH) / breakout origin shelf.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% when 8-hour funding reaches extreme euphoria (`>= 95th percentile` or `> +0.045%`) and price hits the upper Garman-Klass volatility fan band. Move stop on remaining 50% to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind the 4H Volume Profile VAL or exit immediately if Domestic USD Spot CVD rolls over negative while Open Interest continues climbing.

### CSU-02B: Exhaustion Long Top at Resistance ("Euphoric Leverage Crowding")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Open Interest surges (`>= +5%` 7D) and Perpetual Basis spikes above the **90th historical percentile** (`>= +0.035%`, funding `> +0.025%/8h`).
  2. Price stalls against a major HTF resistance / VAH ceiling—failing to make further upward displacement despite heavy market-buy volume (`Perp CVD rising, Price flat`).
  3. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** flattens or turns **negative**, revealing that unleveraged spot holders are selling into the leveraged futures bid.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Late retail and momentum traders are aggressively opening high-leverage longs at resistance, paying exorbitant funding rates while institutional spot holders distribute inventory to them.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Because marginal leveraged buyers are exhausted and spot bids are absent underneath, a minor dip triggers a cascading **long-liquidation flush** back through the Volume Profile Value Area.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL NEW LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH ON SIDELINES.**
  * **Existing Position Management:** If already holding an open Spot Swing Long from a lower entry, immediately execute **Stage 2 Full Profit Exit** (or tighten trailing stop to the 1H Value Area Low) to lock in gains ahead of the forced long flush.

### CSU-02C: Institutional Cash-and-Carry Basis Arbitrage ("Synthetic OI Inflation")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Total Open Interest (especially CME and dated quarterly futures) climbs steadily while annualized futures basis sits in the **+12% to +35% APR** arbitrage zone.
  2. **US Spot ETF Net Inflows** or OTC spot purchases rise in lockstep with short futures Open Interest, while perpetual funding remains moderate (`+0.008% to +0.015%/8h`).
  3. Garman-Klass volatility contracts into the bottom quartile; price remains range-bound near the POC.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Institutional desks are buying spot BTC (or Spot ETFs) and simultaneously shorting dated/perpetual futures to harvest risk-free basis yield. This inflates headline Open Interest without creating directional liquidation fuel.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY / 100% CASH PRESERVATION** for swing capital. Do not mistake arbitrage-driven Open Interest for an imminent directional breakout. Wait for basis compression and a verified directional Volume Profile breakout.

### CSU-02D: Pre-Cascade Spot Bid Pull ("Thin Book Liquidation Setup")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Open Interest sits at extreme highs (`>= 85th percentile`) with elevated positive funding (`>= +0.020%/8h`).
  2. Order-book depth within `-1% to -2%` below spot thins out sharply (`Spot bid liquidity pulled`), and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** breaks down into negative territory (`aligned spot + futures outflow`).
  3. Price loses the 1H Developing POC on rising sell volume.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  With resting spot bids removed beneath an over-leveraged long book, market makers step down their quotes. Once price breaches the first cluster of leveraged long stop-losses, forced market-sell liquidations cascade rapidly downward.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE 100% CASH LOCK.** Exit any remaining spot exposure immediately. Transition scanner to **Module 03 (Deleveraging Flushes & OI Reset Mechanics)** and wait for a verified > 8%–12% OI wipeout before evaluating re-entry.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 03 can authorize a spot entry after a flush, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Active Cascade Freeze (`CB-03`):** Never catch a falling knife while Open Interest is still contracting sharply (`> -1.5% per hour`) and forced liquidations are accelerating. You only act **after** the liquidation engine finishes clearing weak hands.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Partial vs. Full Reset Audit:** A shallow `-2% to -4%` OI dip leaves 95% of crowded leverage intact and frequently leads to a **second, deeper liquidation wave**. High-conviction post-flush entries require either a rapid `-8% to -12%+` cascade wipeout or a multi-week `-15%+` structural OI reset.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Post-Flush Domestic Spot Verification (`CB-02`):** After a long liquidation flush, observe *who* buys the bounce. If Open Interest immediately surges back up while **Domestic USD Spot CVD** stays negative, offshore gamblers are reloading leverage—a classic dead-cat bounce trap.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Regime Check:** In a confirmed bear-market macro contraction (below 200D MA with contracting global liquidity), post-flush bounces are short-lived relief rallies rather than multi-week trend reversals.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-03A: Full Capitulation Flush & V-Bottom Absorption ("Complete Leverage Reset")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Open Interest suffers a deep, decisive wipeout (`>= -8% to -15%` rapid cascade or `>= -15% to -20%` structural contraction, e.g., Binance OI dropping `-17%` from `$10.6B` to `$8.8B`), accompanied by `>= 85%` long liquidation dominance (`>= +2.5 Z-score`).
  2. Perpetual funding and basis reset completely from overheated levels back to **neutral (`0.00%`) or slightly negative**.
  3. **OI Contraction Halts:** Open Interest flattens out for `>= 2 to 4 hours` (proving forced selling is finished), while **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** turns sharply **positive** at a higher-timeframe support or Volume Profile VAL.
  4. **Price Reclaim:** Price sweeps a major liquidity low (capitulation wick) and reclaims the 15m/1H **Value Area Low (VAL)** or Developing POC.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The derivatives market has been purged of weak, over-leveraged speculators, transferring coins from forced sellers into unleveraged institutional spot hands at discounted valuations (mirroring the June 2026 reset below `$60,000` prior to the `+45%` rally).
  * **Probable Trajectory:** With overhead forced-selling supply exhausted and clean order books above, modest spot accumulation drives a sustained, low-resistance structural recovery.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon the confirmed **15m/1H VAL reclaim** backed by rising Domestic USD Spot CVD and flat/low Open Interest.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop strictly below the **capitulation liquidation wick low**. (If the wick is wide, reduce BTC position size proportionally so maximum dollar loss never exceeds `0.75%` of portfolio equity).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% at the prior breakdown origin / mid-range Volume Profile POC (`+1.5R to +2.5R`). Move stop on remaining 50% to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind the 4H Volume Profile VAL or exit if Open Interest rebuilds too rapidly with negative USD Spot CVD.

### CSU-03B: Partial Flush Dead-Cat Bounce ("Unfinished Deleveraging Trap")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price drops sharply into support, triggering a **small/partial OI contraction (`only -2% to -4.5%`)**, leaving total Open Interest still above the **75th rolling percentile**.
  2. Price bounces 1%–3%, and **Open Interest immediately spikes back upward** (`new leveraged longs jumping back in`) while **Perpetual Basis remains elevated** (`> +0.015%`).
  3. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** continues sloping **negative** during the bounce (`Aligned Outflow` across 1H/4H/24H windows).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The initial drop failed to clear out the crowded leverage; instead, retail dip-buyers immediately added fresh leverage on top of an un-reset market while spot holders continued distributing.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High probability of a **secondary, deeper liquidation leg** that takes out the initial wick low to finish the deleveraging process.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH (Zero Spot Long Exposure).** Do not buy the first bounce. Wait for the secondary flush to complete and satisfy `CSU-03A` full reset criteria.

### CSU-03C: Short-Cover Blow-Off Climax ("Upside Deleveraging Exhaustion")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price spikes vertically upward while **Open Interest drops sharply (`-5% to -12%`)** and **Short Liquidations exceed 85%–95%** of total liquidations (`e.g., 4H liquidations 97% Shorts at $29.44M vs. $954K Longs`).
  2. Perpetual basis surges from deep negative into positive territory (`> +0.020%`), while **Domestic USD Spot CVD flattens or turns negative** at the top of the spike.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  The vertical rally was fueled almost entirely by **forced short covering** rather than organic new spot demand. Once the last trapped short is liquidated, the mechanical bid vanishes abruptly.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **DO NOT CHASE / EXECUTE PROFIT TAKING.** If holding an existing Spot Swing Long from a squeeze setup (`MOD 01`), execute **Stage 1 or Stage 2 Profit Exit** directly into the short-liquidation climax. Lock Cash and wait for a post-squeeze pullback retest.

### CSU-03D: Quiet Time-Correction Leverage Bleed ("Voluntary Position Unwind")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Over 7 to 21 days of sideways price consolidation, Open Interest drifts steadily downward by **`-10% to -18%`** *without* single-day liquidation spikes (`liquidations < 50th percentile`).
  2. Funding rates normalize to baseline (`+0.005% to +0.010%/8h`), Garman-Klass volatility compresses into the bottom decile, and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** slowly accumulates higher lows.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Impatient leveraged speculators are voluntarily closing positions due to boredom and funding decay, while quiet institutional spot accumulation absorbs the supply without moving price. This creates the cleanest, lowest-fragility launchpad for a new macro impulse.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Prepare **Unleveraged Spot Long** entry upon a Garman-Klass volatility squeeze breakout above the multi-week Volume Profile **VAH** confirmed by positive Rough Path Signature (`Verdict 0`) and Domestic USD Spot CVD. Size strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling**.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 04 can authorize a spot decision, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — OI-Weighted Normalization (`CB-02`):** Different exchanges settle funding on different schedules (Binance/Bybit/OKX every 8 hours; Coinbase CDE/Hyperliquid every 1 hour). Always normalize all rates to an **8-hour OI-weighted composite** (`Rate_8h = Rate_1h * 8`) and track the **spread between offshore retail venues and domestic USD venues**.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Negative Funding Fallacy Filter (`CB-01`):** A common retail error is assuming negative funding is automatically bullish. Negative funding is only bullish when **Price is holding support or rising** and **Domestic USD Spot CVD is positive** (`Disbelief Rally`). If Price and Domestic Spot CVD are both falling, negative funding simply confirms an active downtrend.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Pillar 1 Macro Permission Gate:** Even a textbook Disbelief Rally divergence is capped in duration if Pillar 1 (Macro Liquidity & 200D MA) is in a **100% Cash Lock**.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Velocity Exhaustion Filter:** When funding velocity spikes vertically (`> +2.5 Z-score` in `< 24 hours`) without matching Spot CVD acceleration, the move is driven by synthetic leverage chasing and is prone to sharp mean reversion.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-04A: The Disbelief Rally ("Price Rising While Funding Compresses or Stays Negative")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Price Slope (`Delta_P`):** Spot price trends higher over 48–168 hours, printing higher highs and higher lows above the 4H Developing POC.
  2. **Funding Velocity (`Delta_F`):** OI-weighted 8-hour funding **falls or remains compressed/negative** (`<= +0.0030%` down to `-0.020%`, rolling 72h Price-Funding correlation `<= -0.50`).
  3. **Spot Confirmation:** **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** slopes steadily **positive** while Perpetual CVD lags or trends negative (traders are shorting every bounce in futures while unleveraged spot buyers absorb).
  4. **Rough Path Signature (Verdict 0):** Price–Volume Lévy Signed Area confirms volume-backed upward path curvature.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** This is the highest-quality, most sustainable bullish structure in Bitcoin markets. Real spot capital is driving price higher while skeptical futures traders continuously short the rally—creating a self-fueling ladder of trapped short liquidity without any long-side funding overhang.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Persistent upward grind punctuated by sharp, sudden short-squeeze candles whenever resistance levels break, continuing until skepticism finally turns into FOMO (`funding flips steeply positive`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on any minor pullback to the 1H Volume Profile **POC or VAL** where funding dips further negative and Domestic USD Spot CVD holds firm.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop below the prior 4H swing higher-low / Volume Profile VAL.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Hold through the disbelief grind until funding finally capitulates upward (crosses above `+0.020%/8h` with a sharp short-liquidation spike); close 50% and move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Exit remaining 50% when funding hits the `>= 90th percentile` (`> +0.035%/8h`) and Domestic USD Spot CVD flattens.

### CSU-04B: Disbelief Dip-Buying ("Price Falling While Funding Spikes Higher")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Price Slope (`Delta_P`):** Spot price declines steadily over 24–96 hours, losing key 1H/4H Volume Profile support nodes.
  2. **Funding Velocity (`Delta_F`):** OI-weighted 8-hour funding **rises sharply** (`e.g., from +0.005% to +0.020%+`, especially on offshore retail venues like OKX/Binance), with rolling 72h Price-Funding correlation `<= -0.50`.
  3. **Spot Confirmation:** **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** and US Spot ETF netflows are **negative** (spot distribution into leveraged retail dip-buyers).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** As price drops, retail futures traders aggressively buy the dip with high leverage, bidding perpetual futures *above* the falling spot price and paying high funding rates to institutional spot sellers who are offloading inventory.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Extremely fragile structure. Because dip-buyers are using margin rather than unleveraged spot, continued spot selling forces those leveraged dip-buyers into margin calls—accelerating the downtrend via cascading long liquidations.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH (Strict Ban on Dip-Buying).** Do not deploy spot capital while retail funding is rising into a falling price tape. Wait until funding breaks downward (`capitulation flip`) and Module 03 (`CSU-03A`) confirms leveraged dip-buyers have been liquidated.

### CSU-04C: Contango Carry Bleed ("Stagnant Price With Sustained High Positive Funding")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Price Slope (`Delta_P`):** Price is trapped in a tight horizontal range (`Garman-Klass volatility < 25th percentile`) for `>= 72 hours`.
  2. **Funding Level:** OI-weighted 8-hour funding stays elevated at **`+0.015% to +0.035%+` (`16%–38% APR`)** with elevated Open Interest.
  3. **Cross-Venue Spread:** Offshore retail funding (Binance/OKX/Bybit) trades `>= +6 to +10 bps` higher per 8h than domestic/institutional venues (Coinbase/CME).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Leveraged longs are bleeding heavy funding payments every 8 hours without receiving any upward price appreciation. Eventually, funding fatigue forces leveraged longs to close at market, triggering a sharp downward stop-hunt wick through the bottom of the range.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY / 100% CASH PRESERVATION.** Avoid holding unhedged tactical longs inside high-funding range chop. Wait for the inevitable downward funding-fatigue wick (`Mutation M4`) to sweep range lows and reset funding before evaluating entry.

### CSU-04D: Backwardation Panic Inflection ("Funding Velocity Crash Into Deep Negative Territory")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Funding Velocity (`Delta_F`):** OI-weighted 8-hour funding plunges rapidly (`<= -2.5 Z-score`, crashing from positive into **`-0.015% to -0.050%`** within 12–36 hours) following a sharp price drop.
  2. **Order-Book & Spot Audit:** Price hits a major higher-timeframe Volume Profile VAL or On-Chain STH Realized Price band, and **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** shows immediate passive limit-bid absorption (`price stops falling despite heavy market selling`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Panic hedging and late-bottom shorting have reached a crescendo at structural support. Market makers and spot institutions are absorbing the panic futures selling at a discount.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Authorize **Unleveraged Spot Long** only once 15m/1H price reclaims the local Developing POC with positive Domestic USD Spot CVD (`confirming panic sellers are trapped`). Size strictly via the **0.75% Risk Formula** with hard stop beneath the panic wick low.

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 05: THE 4-QUADRANT SPOT CVD VS. PERPETUAL CVD MATRIX

**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** November 2026 Multi-Window Order Flow — Distinguishing 3D/7D "Spot Absorbing" regimes (Spot net taker inflow `+$32.5M` to `+$269.4M` vs. Futures net taker outflow `-$1.1B` to `-$1.2B`) from 1H–24H "Aligned Outflow" breakdown regimes (Futures `-$722.5M to -$1.2B` + Spot `-$83.3M to -$141.5M`).

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Perp-Led Pump Ban:** Perpetual futures volume is 5x to 10x larger than spot volume, but perpetual buyers use borrowed margin that must eventually be closed. Unleveraged **Domestic USD Spot CVD** is the only capital that permanently removes Bitcoin supply from the float. Never buy a rally driven solely by Perp CVD (`Quad 3`).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Multi-Timeframe Window Cascade Check (`1H / 4H / 24H` vs. `3D / 7D`):** Higher-timeframe absorption (`3D/7D Spot CVD > 0`) can fail if short-term windows (`1H/4H/24H`) flip into **Aligned Outflow**. Both the tactical (`1H/4H`) and structural (`24H/3D`) Domestic Spot CVD slopes must be positive before authorizing an entry.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Price Displacement Confirmation:** Positive Spot CVD must eventually produce upward price displacement (reclaiming 15m/1H VAH). If Spot CVD rises steeply while price falls, passive limit sellers are overwhelming spot market buyers (see Module 06).

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units: The 4 Quadrants (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-05A (Quadrant 1): Spot CVD Rising (+) / Perp CVD Falling (–) — "Spot Absorbing Perp Shorts"
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** slopes positively (`Z-score >= +1.0`) across the 1H, 4H, and 24H windows.
  2. **Perpetual CVD** slopes negatively (`Z-score <= -1.0`), accompanied by rising Open Interest and negative Perp Basis.
  3. Price holds flat above a 1H/4H Volume Profile VAL or prints higher lows despite heavy perpetual market-selling.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional unleveraged spot buyers are accumulating directly into the panic/aggression of leveraged perpetual short sellers.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Once perpetual selling exhausts, the absence of spot sellers combined with trapped perp shorts drives an asymmetric upward expansion (`Module 01 / CSU-01A` confluence).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon a verified 15m/1H **Value Area High (VAH) reclaim** under the **0.75% Risk Ceiling**:
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Absorption VAL Stop)`
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit:** Close 50% when Perp CVD spikes positive on short covering (`Basis > 0`); trail the remaining 50% behind the 4H VAL.

### CSU-05B (Quadrant 2): Spot CVD Rising (+) / Perp CVD Rising (+) — "Aligned Institutional Markup"
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Both **Domestic USD Spot CVD** and **Perpetual CVD** slope positively across 1H, 4H, and 24H windows.
  2. **Rough Path Signature (`A_SpotCVD,PerpCVD`)** confirms that Domestic Spot CVD initiated the upward turn *before* Perp CVD followed.
  3.Perpetual Basis remains moderate (`< +0.02%`, below the 85th percentile overheating threshold).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Broad-based institutional and momentum trend expansion where spot demand leads and derivatives follow in healthy alignment.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sustained directional trend through Low-Volume Nodes (LVNs) toward higher HTF valuation bands.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Hold existing spot swing positions or enter on 15m/1H pullbacks to Developing POC/VAH at **0.75% maximum portfolio risk**. Monitor for **Mutation M2** (when Perp CVD accelerates far ahead of Spot CVD at resistance).

### CSU-05C (Quadrant 3): Spot CVD Falling (–) / Perp CVD Rising (+) — "Perp-Led Bull Trap / Spot Distribution"
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Perpetual CVD** slopes strongly positive (leveraged takers aggressively buying breakouts or dips) and Open Interest rises.
  2. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** slopes **negative** (Coinbase/Kraken net taker selling and/or Spot ETF outflows).
  3. Price struggles to hold above 1H VAH or prints bearish CVD divergences at local highs.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The classic retail leverage trap. Unleveraged spot whales and institutions are dumping physical Bitcoin into the bids created by leveraged perpetual futures buyers.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** As soon as perpetual buying slows down, the weight of spot distribution breaks the floor and triggers a long-liquidation cascade downward.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STRICT 100% CASH LOCK (CB-01 Tripped).** If holding an open spot swing position, execute an immediate protective exit. Never hold spot when institutions are selling spot into perpetual leverage.

### CSU-05D (Quadrant 4): Spot CVD Falling (–) / Perp CVD Falling (–) — "Aligned Structural Markdown"
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Both **Domestic USD Spot CVD** and **Perpetual CVD** slope negatively across the 1H, 4H, 12H, and 24H windows (e.g., 24H Futures `-$722.5M to -$1.2B` alongside 24H Spot `-$83.3M to -$141.5M`).
  2. Price breaks below 1H/4H Volume Profile VAL and trades below the 1H/4H Developing POC.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Synchronized capital exit across both unleveraged spot holders and leveraged futures traders. There is no active buyer of last resort on the tape.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sustained markdown and support failure until a major HTF liquidity pool is swept and Quadrant 1 (`Spot CVD + / Perp CVD -`) re-emerges.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH.** Zero dip-buying is permitted while all tactical windows (`1H/4H/24H`) remain in Quadrant 4 Aligned Outflow.

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 06: PASSIVE LIMIT ABSORPTION VS. MARKET TAKER EXHAUSTION
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 2 (Microstructure, Order Flow & Path Geometry) cross-verified with Pillar 4 (Portfolio Engineering).
**Core Thesis:** Resolves the fundamental order-book paradox where Cumulative Volume Delta (CVD) only measures aggressive market takers. Quantifies price displacement per unit of net delta ($\Delta P / |\Delta \text{CVD}|$) to detect hidden institutional limit-order walls before price reverses.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_06"
entry_type: "CONDITIONAL_SIGNAL_UNIT"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_2_MICROSTRUCTURE_ORDER_FLOW"
title: "Passive Limit Absorption vs. Market Taker Exhaustion"

# PILLAR 1 MASTER PERMISSION PRE-CHECK

cash_lock_override: "If Pillar 1 Macro Regime == RISK_OFF_100_CASH, block all Long executions."

# PRIMARY TELEMETRY TRIGGERS (EFFORT VS. RESULT RATIO)
base_regime_triggers:
  displacement_efficiency_ratio: "E_d = abs(Delta Price %) / abs(CVD Z-score) over 15m/1H/4H window"
  absorption_threshold: "E_d <= 15th rolling percentile (Massive net CVD effort with near-zero price displacement)"
  vacuum_threshold: "E_d >= 85th rolling percentile (Large price displacement on minimal volume/CVD)"
  limit_book_depth_imbalance: "Bid-to-Ask resting liquidity ratio within 1.0%–2.5% of mid-price"

# ANTI-TRAP CIRCUIT BREAKERS
circuit_breakers:
  CB_01_BLIND_LIMIT_WALL_BAN: "NEVER front-run a visible limit bid wall on the DOM/heatmap before taker exhaustion and an upward Absorption-to-Initiation candle print (spoof walls can be pulled in milliseconds)."
  CB_02_PREMATURE_CLOSE_FILTER: "Verify that absorption occurs at a structural Volume Profile node (VAL, VAH, POC, or STH Realized Price), not in the middle of a random Low-Volume Node."
  CB_03_WALL_BREACH_ABORT: "If price slices through an absorption zone on expanding volume, exit immediately—the passive limit wall has been completely filled and exhausted."

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  leverage_permitted: 1.0
  exit_protocol: "TWO_STAGE_STRUCTURAL"
```

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Anti-Spoofing Verification (Wait for the Initiation Flip):** Large resting limit orders on an order-book heatmap can be spoofed and pulled milliseconds before price touches them. Never buy simply because a resting bid wall appears on a screen. You must wait for **realized absorption** (heavy market selling actually hitting the bid with $\Delta P \approx 0$) followed by an **Absorption-to-Initiation Flip** (market buyers stepping up to reclaim the 15m/1H footprint high).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Structural Location Filter:** True institutional limit absorption occurs at pre-defined valuation boundaries (Volume Profile VAL/VAH, 200D MA, or Short-Term Holder Realized Price). Ignore minor 5-minute absorption inside noisy mid-range chop.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Hard Invalidation on Wall Depletion:** Even genuine institutional limit walls have a finite BTC budget. If sellers exhaust the entire resting bid wall and push price cleanly below the absorption cluster low, exit immediately with zero hesitation.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-06A: Passive Limit Bid-Wall Absorption (Bullish Floor Verification)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price tests a major structural support zone (4H/1D Volume Profile VAL, HTF swing low, or STH cost basis).
  2. Net Taker CVD plunges sharply negative (`1H/4H CVD Z-score <= -2.0`), yet **Price Displacement Efficiency ($E_d = |\Delta P| / |\Delta \text{CVD}|$) drops into the bottom 15th percentile** (candles print tight bodies or long lower wicks that refuse to close lower).
  3. **Initiation Trigger:** Following the absorption cluster, a 15m/1H candle closes above the high of the highest-volume absorption bar alongside a positive uptick in **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional whales executing passive TWAP/iceberg limit buy orders have absorbed 100% of aggressive market-taker selling without letting price drop.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Market sellers are now exhausted (out of inventory or maxed out on short margin). As soon as passive buyers switch to market-buying the ask, price springs upward rapidly.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon the 15m/1H close above the absorption cluster high (and VAL reclaim).
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Absorption Cluster Low Stop)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop `0.15% to 0.25%` below the lowest wick of the verified absorption cluster (providing ultra-tight risk geometry and high R-multiples).

### CSU-06B: Passive Limit Ask-Wall Capping (Bearish Ceiling Distribution)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price rallies into a major structural resistance zone (4H/1D Volume Profile VAH or prior distribution HVN).
  2. Net Taker CVD surges strongly positive (`1H/4H CVD Z-score >= +2.0`), yet **Price Displacement Efficiency ($E_d$) compresses into the bottom 15th percentile** (price flattens and prints repeated upper wicks).
  3. Price subsequently breaks below the low of the high-volume capping cluster as Domestic USD Spot CVD turns negative.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional holders are passively unloading huge spot inventory via iceberg limit asks, using aggressive retail market-buyers as liquidity without crashing the price prematurely.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Once aggressive buyers exhaust their purchasing power against the invisible ask ceiling, price drops sharply back toward the Volume Profile POC and VAL.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Existing Spot Longs:** **Execute Immediate Profit Exit (50% to 100%)** when the 15m/1H candle closes below the ask-wall absorption cluster low.
  * **New Entries:** **Enforce 100% Cash Lock.** Never buy into an active passive ask-wall ceiling.

### CSU-06C: Order-Book Liquidity Vacuum Impulse (Thin-Book Acceleration)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Price Displacement Efficiency ($E_d = |\Delta P| / |\Delta \text{CVD}|$) spikes into the top 85th+ percentile** (price moves rapidly on moderate volume/delta).
  2. Price is traversing a Volume Profile **Low-Volume Node (LVN)** where resting limit-book depth is thin.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Because resting limit liquidity has been pulled from the book, even small market orders displace price across wide ranges until the next High-Volume Node (HVN) is reached.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **If Bullish Vacuum (Price ↑, Domestic Spot CVD +):** Hold existing spot swing positions untouched until price reaches the next HVN magnet; do not chase mid-LVN with wide stops.
  * **If Bearish Vacuum (Price ↓, Spot/Perp CVD –):** Remain **100% Cash**; do not attempt to bid inside an LVN air pocket.

### CSU-06D: Taker Capitulation Climax (Exhaustion Spike Reversal)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. A single 15m/1H bar prints a `>= +3.5 Z-score` volume and delta climax at the end of a multi-day trend extension.
  2. Over 60% of the candle's volume occurs in the outer wick tip (footprint node trapped at the extreme), and the candle closes back inside the prior bar's range.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  The final wave of forced liquidations and late emotional market orders has been completely absorbed at the wick extreme, leaving zero follow-through takers remaining.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **At Climax Highs:** Close 100% of tactical spot longs immediately.
  * **At Climax Lows:** Prepare **CSU-06A** spot long entry upon confirmation of the next 1H candle holding above the trapped-seller footprint node, sized strictly to **0.75% max equity risk**.

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 07: DOMESTIC FIAT GATEWAYS (USD) & SPOT ETFS VS. OFFSHORE PERP/USDT FLOWS
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 2 (Microstructure & Order Flow) integrated with Pillar 5 (Institutional & On-Chain Flows).

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Sovereign Venue Hierarchy Law:** Not all exchanges are equal. **Domestic Fiat Gateways (Coinbase/Kraken BTC-USD + US Spot ETFs IBIT/FBTC)** represent regulated fiat capital entering or exiting the Bitcoin ecosystem. **Offshore Venues (Binance/OKX/Bybit USDT + Perps)** represent internal crypto-collateralized rotation and high-turnover leverage. Whenever Domestic USD flows conflict with Offshore USDT flows, **Domestic USD is treated as ground truth.**
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — The Wall Street Cash-and-Carry Filter:** When US Spot ETFs print a `+$300M` daily inflow, do not automatically assume `+$300M` of directional bullish buying. Cross-check **CME Open Interest** and **Annualized CME Basis**: if CME OI expands simultaneously while price stays flat, hedge funds are buying the ETF and shorting CME futures (delta-neutral arbitrage).
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — T+1 ETF Flow Lag Protection:** Daily ETF netflow summary tables are published *after* the US market close. During live market hours, use **intraday IBIT volume + Coinbase BTC-USD Spot CVD + Coinbase Premium Index** as the real-time proxy for institutional ETF creation/redemption.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-07A: Pure Directional Institutional Accumulation (Fiat Gateway Lead)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Coinbase Premium Index** holds consistently positive (`>= +0.05% to +0.20%`) during US trading hours, and **Coinbase/Kraken USD Spot CVD** slopes steeply upward.
  2. **US Spot BTC ETFs** record sustained net inflows (`>= +$150M to +$500M+/day` on a 3D rolling basis), led by BlackRock (`IBIT`) and Fidelity (`FBTC`).
  3. **CME Open Interest** either grows *less* than ETF inflows (confirming unhedged directional spot buying) or rises alongside expanding positive CME basis as institutions take outright long exposure.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Genuine fiat liquidity from sovereign, RIA, and corporate allocators is permanently absorbing circulating BTC into cold-storage ETF custodians.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Structural multi-week uptrend where every offshore perpetual flush is rapidly bought up during the New York cash session.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Full-Allocation Unleveraged Spot Swing** under the **0.75% Risk Ceiling** upon a 1H Volume Profile VAH reclaim during or immediately following the NY cash open:
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - 1H/4H VAL Stop)`
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit:** Hold core position across multi-day swings; take Stage 1 (50%) at HTF valuation targets (`STH-MVRV >= 1.30` or 1D VAH) and trail Stage 2 behind the 4H/1D VAL.

### CSU-07B: Offshore Leverage Bull Trap Against Domestic Fiat Distribution
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Offshore perpetual Open Interest (Binance, OKX, Bybit, Hyperliquid) expands rapidly (`+10,000 to +25,000+ BTC`) and offshore USDT Spot CVD appears flat or slightly positive.
  2. Simultaneously, **Coinbase Premium Index** is persistently negative (`<= -0.05%`), **Coinbase USD Spot CVD** slopes downward, **CME Open Interest drops (`-10% to -15%`)**, and **US Spot ETFs print multi-day net outflows** (e.g., `-$299.8M/day`, `-$1.177B` over 3 weeks).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** US institutions and fiat holders are actively redeeming ETFs and selling physical BTC on Coinbase, while offshore retail traders are trying to prop up the market with leveraged perpetual contracts and USDT churn.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Fiat supply eventually overwhelms offshore paper leverage, triggering a violent multi-leg breakdown that liquidates the offshore dip-buyers (`November 2026 Empirical Precedent`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LOCK 100% CASH (CB-01 Tripped).** Completely ignore bullish offshore perpetual or USDT signals until Coinbase Premium and 3-day rolling US Spot ETF netflows flip back to positive.

### CSU-07C: Delta-Neutral Wall Street Basis Absorption (High Inflow / Flat Price)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. US Spot BTC ETFs print large positive inflows (`+$250M to +$600M/day`), yet BTC spot price remains range-bound (`< 1.5%` net progress).
  2. **CME Futures Open Interest** increases by nearly the exact same dollar notional as the cumulative ETF inflows, while CME annualized basis sits at an attractive `+10% to +16%`.
  3. Directional Coinbase USD Spot Premium outside the TWAP settlement windows remains flat (`~0.00%`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Arbitrage hedge funds are capturing the CME basis spread by buying ETF shares and shorting CME futures 1:1. Net market delta is zero—neither bullish nor bearish until the basis compresses or directional spot buyers arrive.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY / DO NOT CHASE HEADLINE ETF INFLOWS.** Treat the market as range-bound (`Module 11` Value Area rotation) until directional Coinbase USD Spot CVD breaks price above 4H VAH.

### CSU-07D: Exhaustion of ETF Outflow Capitulation (Contrarian Institutional Floor)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. After 2 to 4 weeks of heavy US Spot ETF outflows (`>= -$1.0B` cumulative) and a `-15% to -30%` BTC price correction, a single day prints peak ETF outflow headlines.
  2. Despite the ugly T+1 outflow headline, **intraday Coinbase BTC-USD Spot CVD and the Coinbase Premium Index flip sharply positive** (`>= +0.08%`) and price reclaims the 1H/4H VAL.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  ETF flow headlines report *yesterday's* panic redemptions, whereas intraday Coinbase USD Spot CVD reflects *today's* real-time institutional re-accumulation stepping in at the bottom.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Prioritize **real-time intraday Coinbase USD Spot CVD + 1H VAH/VAL reclaim** over lagging T+1 ETF outflow headlines; authorize a **0.75% risk-capped Spot Long** with hard invalidation below the intraday absorption low.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 08 can authorize a spot order, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Thin-Book Illusion Filter (`CB-01`):** Standard candle charts treat a +3% rally on $72M thin book depth identically to a +3% rally backed by institutional volume. Level-2 Lévy Signed Area (`A_P,V`) mathematically exposes the chronological order: if price rises *before* volume steps in (`A_P,V < -1.0 Z-score`), the rally is a fragile liquidity vacuum trap.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Wash-Volume Normalization (`CB-02`):** Normalize volume increments `S^(2)` using rolling median absolute deviation (MAD) across verified USD/institutional spot exchanges so offshore zero-fee maker-rebate churn cannot spoof positive signed area.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Liquidation Cascade Freeze (`CB-03`):** During forced margin flushes, price gaps downward before liquidation volume prints (`extreme negative A_P,V`). Freeze all spot entries until signed area curvature inflects back toward zero.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Gate:** Even strong volume-led upward curvature is restricted to tactical profit-taking if Pillar 1 is locked in `RISK_OFF_100_CASH`.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-08A: Volume-Led Markup Curvature ("Institutional Impulse Birth")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. First-order price increment `S^(1) > 0` (price breaking above 1H/4H Volume Profile POC or VAH).
  2. **Level-2 Lévy Signed Area `A_P,V >= +1.5 Z-score` (`>= 85th rolling percentile`)**, proving mathematically that **volume expansion preceded and drove price displacement** chronologically within the path window.
  3. **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** confirms positive net taker buying and Pillar 1 grants risk permission.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Real capital committed size at the bid *before* price cleared overhead liquidity, absorbing resting supply rather than chasing thin air.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-persistence directional trend leg with strong structural support at the impulse origin, as institutional buyers defend their volume-weighted entry zone.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon a verified 15m/1H pullback retest of the reclaimed **VAH or impulse POC** while `A_P,V` remains positive (`> +0.5 Z-score`).
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop immediately beneath the volume-led impulse origin / 1H VAL.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% when `A_P,V` begins rolling over from peak (`> +2.5 Z-score` decelerating below `+1.0 Z-score`) at upper Garman-Klass volatility bands; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H Volume Profile VAL or exit immediately if `A_P,V` flips negative (`< -1.0 Z-score`) while price makes marginal new highs.

### CSU-08B: Price-Led Thin Vacuum Rally ("Bull Trap Curvature")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. First-order price increment `S^(1) > 0` (price pushes into resistance or sweeps a prior swing high).
  2. **Level-2 Lévy Signed Area `A_P,V <= -1.5 Z-score` (`<= 15th rolling percentile`)**, proving that **price jumped first on thin order-book liquidity and volume only appeared at the top** (late retail chasing / passive institutional ask absorption).
  3. Domestic USD Spot CVD is flat or negative.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market makers pulled offers in a thin book (such as $72M depth regimes), allowing price to float up effortlessly before institutional sellers unloaded into late momentum buyers at the peak.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Rapid mean-reversion failure back through the entire thin-volume gap ("look-above-and-fail").
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH.** If holding an existing Spot Swing Long from lower levels, immediately execute **Stage 2 Profit Exit** into the thin-book spike.

### CSU-08C: Volume-Backed Accumulation Loop ("Counter-Clockwise Bottom Curvature")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Net price change over the window is near zero (`S^(1) ~ 0`), oscillating inside a tight Volume Profile Value Area at higher-timeframe support.
  2. **Level-2 Lévy Signed Area `A_P,V` cycles strongly positive (`>= +1.25 Z-score`)** across repeated intraday sweeps of VAL—showing heavy volume surges on dips and quiet, low-volume rebounds.
  3. Domestic USD Spot CVD prints higher lows while price prints equal or slightly lower lows.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  The joint `(Price, Volume)` path is tracing an institutional absorption loop: large passive buyers are soaking up every wave of market selling at the bottom of the range without letting price break down.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Authorize **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon a verified 15m/1H **VAH or Developing POC reclaim** exiting the accumulation loop, sized strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling** with stop beneath the loop's lowest absorption wick.

### CSU-08D: Climax Distribution & Vacuum Breakdown ("Markdown Acceleration")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. First-order price increment `S^(1) < 0` (breaking below 1H/4H VAL) while **`A_P,V <= -2.0 Z-score`** on red candles (price dropping rapidly through air pockets *before* panicked liquidation volume hits at the lows).
  2. Domestic USD Spot CVD and Perpetual CVD are both negative (`Aligned Outflow`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Bid liquidity has vanished beneath price; sellers are hitting air pockets, triggering delayed stop-losses and forced margin liquidations lower down the book.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE 100% CASH LOCK.** Stand down completely until `A_P,V` stops accelerating negatively and `CSU-08C` accumulation curvature reappears at lower HTF support.

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 09: ROUGH PATH SIGNATURES II — SPOT CVD–OPEN INTEREST LÉVY SIGNED AREA (A_CVD,OI)
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 2 & Pillar 3 Bridge (Joint Path Geometry of Unleveraged Spot Flow vs. Leveraged Derivatives Creation) cross-verified with Pillar 4 (Portfolio Engineering).
**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** November 2026 Joint Flow-Leverage Telemetry — During the November 6–12 window, Perpetual Open Interest surged by **+22,000 BTC** (to **367.4K BTC**) *before* brief 3-day spot buying (**+$269.4M**) appeared, followed by domestic spot outflows (**-$83.3M to -$141.5M** 24H) while offshore perps kept adding leverage—tracing a negative `A_CVD,OI` curvature (`Leverage leading Spot`) that preceded the **$1.41B** long liquidation flush below **$69,000**.

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 09 can authorize a spot order, all four circuit breakers must pass:
* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Lead-Lag Causal Order (`CB-01`):** Standard correlation treats `(Spot CVD up, OI up)` as one single state. Rough Path Signed Area `A_CVD,OI` measures **which moved first**: did unleveraged spot buyers accumulate *first* and pull futures OI up behind them (`A_CVD,OI > 0`), or did leveraged gamblers pile into futures *first* hoping spot would rescue them (`A_CVD,OI < 0`)?
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Pure USD Spot Coordinate (`CB-02`):** `X_t` must be built from verified domestic USD spot flows. If offshore USDT spot rises while USD spot falls, `X_t` must reflect the domestic outflow.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Dual-Collapse Freeze (`CB-03`):** When both Spot CVD and Open Interest collapse together (`S^(CVD) < 0` and `S^(OI) < 0`), forced liquidations and spot redemptions are compounding. Lock 100% Cash.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Permission Gate:** Verify secular macro permissions before deploying risk on positive `A_CVD,OI` setups.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-09A: Spot-Led Leverage Expansion ("Self-Sustaining Bull Curvature")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Both Domestic USD Spot CVD (`S^(CVD) > 0`) and Open Interest (`S^(OI) > 0`) are expanding over the 24H–72H window.
  2. **Joint Lévy Signed Area `A_CVD,OI >= +1.5 Z-score` (`>= 85th rolling percentile`)**, confirming chronologically that **unleveraged domestic spot accumulation initiated the move** and derivatives Open Interest expanded afterward as trend followers joined.
  3. Price holds cleanly above the 1H/4H Volume Profile POC/VAH.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional spot buyers own the underlying coins outright and set the structural floor; derivatives leverage is riding *behind* real capital inflows rather than front-running thin liquidity.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Durable multi-day trend expansion where shallow leverage shakeouts are rapidly bought up by the underlying spot bid.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on a 15m/1H pullback to the Volume Profile **VAH or Developing POC** while `A_CVD,OI` remains positive (`>= +0.75 Z-score`).
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop beneath the prior 4H swing low / VAL where domestic spot accumulation began.
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% if `A_CVD,OI` crosses below `0.0` into negative territory (meaning leverage has overtaken spot leadership); move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind 4H VAL or exit completely if `A_CVD,OI` drops below `-1.5 Z-score`.

### CSU-09B: Leverage-Led Speculative Overextension ("Fragile Paper Tower")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price is rising or holding near resistance while Open Interest surges (`S^(OI) >> 0`).
  2. **Joint Lévy Signed Area `A_CVD,OI <= -1.5 Z-score` (`<= 15th rolling percentile`)**, proving mathematically that **leveraged futures contracts expanded first** while Domestic USD Spot CVD lagged, stayed flat, or turned negative.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The rally is built on borrowed margin rather than sovereign spot demand (precisely matching the November 2026 setup where offshore OI jumped +22K BTC while spot demand failed to sustain follow-through).
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High vulnerability to an abrupt **long-liquidation air pocket** the moment momentum stalls.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL NEW LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH.** If holding an existing Spot Long from lower prices, execute **Stage 2 Full Profit Exit** immediately.

### CSU-09C: Spot Buying Into Open Interest Unwind ("Cleanest Structural Reversal")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Open Interest contracts steadily (`S^(OI) < 0`, deleveraging flush or voluntary unwind) while **Domestic USD Spot CVD rises sharply (`S^(CVD) > 0`)**.
  2. **Joint Lévy Signed Area `A_CVD,OI >= +1.5 Z-score`**, showing spot accumulation stepping in aggressively as leveraged weak hands are flushed out of the system.
  3. Price sweeps a higher-timeframe liquidity low and reclaims the 15m/1H Volume Profile VAL.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Coins are transferring directly from liquidated margin gamblers to unleveraged institutional spot wallets. Overhead liquidation risk is purged while a genuine spot floor forms.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon 15m/1H **VAL or Developing POC reclaim**, sized strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling** with hard stop below the flush wick low.

### CSU-09D: Spot Selling Into Open Interest Build-Up ("Institutional Hedging / Distribution Markdown")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Domestic USD Spot CVD plunges (`S^(CVD) << 0`)** while **Open Interest expands (`S^(OI) > 0`)**, generating a steep negative signed area **`A_CVD,OI <= -2.0 Z-score`** on a downward price trajectory.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**

Holding a 1H tactical trade into a multi-week swing without 4H/1D signature confirmation violates path-decay physics and exposes capital to higher-timeframe trend resumption.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Hierarchy of Drift:** Higher timeframes govern structural drift (`1D > 4H > 1H`), while lower timeframes govern entry precision (`1H / 15m`). Never fight a negative 4H + 1D structural vector with full 0.75% risk size.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Counter-Trend Half-Risk Rule:** If taking a high-confluence 1H reversal inflection (`CSU-10C`) at a major HTF support while 4H/1D remain negative, position risk is automatically cut in half (**0.375% maximum portfolio equity risk**) until 4H flips positive.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-11A: Look-Below-and-Fail Spring ("VAL Liquidity Sweep & Reclaim")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price breaks downward below the multi-day or session **Value Area Low (VAL)**, sweeping resting retail stop-losses sitting beneath support.
  2. **Rejection & Reclaim:** Within 1 to 4 bars (15m/1H), price fails to build volume below VAL, snaps back **inside the Value Area**, and closes firmly above **VAL**.
  3. **Order-Flow Verification:** **Domestic Fiat (USD) Spot CVD** prints a clear bullish absorption divergence on the sweep (`passive limit bids absorbing market sells`), and Rough Path `A_P,V` inflects positive.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional buyers used the stop-loss liquidity pool below VAL to fill large spot bids at a discount without pushing price up prematurely.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Under the **80% Auction Rotation Rule**, once price re-enters and accepts inside a Value Area after a failed breakdown, it rotates across the entire distribution—first to the **POC** and then to the **Value Area High (VAH)**.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon the confirmed **15m/1H close back above VAL** (or immediate retest of VAL from above) with positive Domestic USD Spot CVD.
  * **Position Sizing Formula (0.75% Risk Ceiling):**
    `Spot Position (BTC) = (0.0075 * Total Portfolio Equity) / (Entry Price - Invalidation Stop Price)`
  * **Hard Structural Invalidation Stop:** Place hard stop strictly beneath the **failed breakdown swing wick low** (typically `0.5%–1.5%` below VAL).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% at the composite **Point of Control (POC)** (the primary high-volume magnet); move stop on remaining 50% to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Close remaining 50% at the **Value Area High (VAH)** unless `CSU-11B` breakout acceptance triggers.

### CSU-11B: Low-Volume Node (LVN) Fast-Traverse Breakout ("Initiation Away From Value")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price breaks above the **Value Area High (VAH)** and enters a **Low-Volume Node (LVN)** air pocket (`volume density <= 20th percentile`).
  2. **Acceptance Verification:** Price holds above VAH for `>= 2 consecutive 15m closes` (or 1H close), Developing POC (`dPOC`) migrates upward toward the breakout zone, and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** + Rough Path `A_P,V` (`>= +1.5 Z`) surge upward.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Market participants have rejected the prior lower value range and are aggressively repricing Bitcoin upward to establish a new equilibrium.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Because LVNs contain minimal historical resting liquidity, price traverses the LVN rapidly until it reaches the next overhead **High-Volume Node (HVN)** or HTF Value Area.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on a shallow 15m pullback to the **reclaimed VAH / LVN entry lip** where sellers fail to push price back into the old Value Area. Size strictly via the **0.75% Risk Formula** with hard stop just inside the old Value Area (`below VAH`).
  * **Two-Stage Profit Exit Protocol:**
    * **Stage 1 Exit (50% of Position):** Close 50% upon reaching the base of the next overhead **High-Volume Node (HVN)**; move stop to breakeven.
    * **Stage 2 Exit (Remaining 50%):** Trail behind the rising 1H Developing POC (`dPOC`).

### CSU-11C: Look-Above-and-Fail ("VAH Sweep Rejection & Downward Rotation")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price pokes above the **Value Area High (VAH)**, sweeping breakout buyers and short stops.
  2. Price fails to hold above VAH, closes back **below VAH** on the 15m/1H chart, while **Domestic USD Spot CVD** diverges negatively (`Perp CVD positive, USD Spot CVD negative`) and `A_P,V <= -1.25 Z-score`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional spot sellers used the breakout liquidity above VAH to distribute inventory into late breakout chasers.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-probability rotation downward through the **POC** toward the **Value Area Low (VAL)**.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH.** If holding an existing Spot Long from lower inside the range, immediately execute **Stage 2 Full Profit Exit** upon the close back below VAH.

### CSU-11D: High-Volume Node (HVN) Equilibrium Chop ("POC Balance Lock")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price oscillates tightly within `+/- 0.75%` of a multi-day **High-Volume Node / Point of Control (POC)**.
  2. Developing POC (`dPOC`) remains stationary, Garman-Klass volatility compresses below the 20th percentile, and Spot/Perp CVD slopes are flat.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  Two-sided institutional liquidity is in complete equilibrium; market makers are harvesting bid-ask spread while directional traders get chopped to pieces.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STANDBY / 100% CASH PRESERVATION.** Zero trades allowed inside an HVN magnet zone. Wait for price to reach an edge (`VAL` or `VAH`) and trigger `CSU-11A` or `CSU-11B`.
