# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 36: TELEMETRY TRUST, STALENESS SLA & DETERMINISM ATTESTATION
**Waterfall Tier:** Tier D Supreme Control-Plane Gate (Sits *above* `MOD 33` Master Regime Radar and `MOD 18` Sizing Engine to enforce fail-closed data integrity, cross-venue price quorum, cron heartbeat verification, and bit-for-bit replay determinism).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT, Qwen, Perplexity) — addressing GitHub Actions `schedule` best-effort top-of-hour queue delays/drops, stale API feeds, cross-venue price outliers, and strict separation between NotebookLM qualitative research and the deterministic pure-Python compiler (`cortex_matrix_engine.py`).

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_36"
entry_type: "CONTROL_PLANE_TRUST_ATTESTATION"
waterfall_pillar: "TIER_D_SUPREME_TELEMETRY_TRUST_GATE"
title: "Telemetry Trust, Staleness SLA & Determinism Attestation"

# SUPREME HIERARCHY POSITION
governance_priority: 1 # Overrides MOD 33 Regime Permission and MOD 18 Entry Sizing
icdm_contribution_mode: "BINARY_FAIL_CLOSED_GATE" # Does not add ICDM points; emits GREEN / AMBER / RED / UNKNOWN

# MANDATORY MODULE OUTPUT CONTRACT (APPLIES TO ALL MODULES)
common_module_contract:
  required_timestamps: ["event_timestamp_utc", "publication_timestamp_utc", "ingestion_timestamp_utc", "as_of_timestamp_utc"]
  required_hashes: ["ruleset_yaml_sha256", "input_snapshot_sha256", "decision_replay_sha256"]
  allowed_states: ["GREEN", "AMBER", "RED", "UNKNOWN"]
  zero_hallucination_rule: "UNKNOWN (missing/stale/unverified) MUST NEVER be coerced to 0 or GREEN."

# TELEMETRY SLA & QUORUM THRESHOLDS
sla_and_quorum_thresholds:
  feed_staleness_multiplier_max: 2.0 # Feed age > 2.0x declared cadence -> UNKNOWN / TRUST_DEGRADED
  cron_heartbeat_max_gap_minutes: 90 # GitHub Actions hourly schedule dead-man switch
  venue_quorum_min_sources: 3 # Minimum independent spot venues (e.g., Coinbase, Binance, Kraken/Bitstamp)
  venue_quorum_max_dispersion_bps: 35.0 # Max mid-price spread across quorum venues before outlier freeze
  ntp_clock_skew_max_ms: 2000 # Max local clock drift vs UTC NTP reference
  rearm_clean_cycles_required: 2 # Consecutive clean cycles required to lift TRUST_DEGRADED lock

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  degraded_trust_max_risk_pct: 0.00 # Strict Fail-Closed Cash / Freeze
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Control-Plane Data Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Fail-Closed `UNKNOWN` Law (`CB-01`):** Missing, stale, or unverified telemetry must be tagged `UNKNOWN`—never defaulted to `0.0` or `GREEN`. While `RED` logs a verified adverse market event and `UNKNOWN` logs an unverified telemetry state, **both block all new spot entries**.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — GitHub Actions Cron Dead-Man Switch (`CB-02`):** Because GitHub Actions `schedule` triggers (`cron: "5 * * * *"`) are best-effort and subject to top-of-hour queue delays or drops under load, an independent watchdog checks `ingestion_timestamp_utc`. If no attested pipeline heartbeat arrives within **`90 minutes`**, new entries lock automatically until **2 consecutive clean cycles** pass.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Three-Venue Price Quorum (`CB-03`):** Spot reference price and stop-loss triggers must be validated across at least **3 independent spot venues** (e.g., Coinbase USD, Binance USDT, Kraken/Bitstamp USD). If dispersion exceeds **`35 bps`**, freeze execution to prevent single-venue API glitches or flash-wick anomalies from triggering false entries or premature stops.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Bit-for-Bit Compiler Determinism (`CB-04`):** NotebookLM and LLM summaries are strictly research/audit layers and **never** enter the live execution path. `cortex_matrix_engine.py` must log `ruleset_yaml_sha256` + `input_snapshot_sha256` so any decision can be replayed bit-for-bit during `MOD 28` Live Autopsies.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-36A: Critical Feed Staleness or Cron Heartbeat Lapse (`TRUST_DEGRADED — Fail-Closed`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Any critical input feed (`spot price`, `USD/Perp CVD`, `OI/funding`, or `order-book depth`) exceeds **`2.0x` its declared cadence SLA**, **OR** `as_of_timestamp_utc` lags current UTC time by **`> 90 minutes`** (missed/delayed cron heartbeat).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** The engine is blind to current market state; computing entries on stale data creates unpriced tail exposure.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **SET `MOD_36_STATE = UNKNOWN (TRUST_DEGRADED)`.** Override `MOD 33` to block all new spot entries; manage any existing open swing strictly from the last attested pre-computed stop/target levels; send `CRITICAL` Telegram HUD warning.

### CSU-36B: Cross-Venue Quorum Dispersion Breach ("Single-Venue Feed Anomaly")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Fewer than **`3` independent spot venues** respond within timeout, **OR** the max-minus-min mid-price spread across active quorum venues exceeds **`35 bps`** (`0.35%`) outside a verified `MOD 32` exchange solvency event.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** One venue is experiencing an API freeze, order-book outage, or localized stablecoin de-peg distortion.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **FREEZE NEW ENTRIES & SUPPRESS VENUE-SPECIFIC STOP TRIGGERS** until cross-venue dispersion compresses back below `35 bps` across `>= 3` venues.

### CSU-36C: Determinism Replay or Rule-Hash Drift Violation ("Governance Halt")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `ruleset_yaml_sha256` does not match the version-controlled GitHub configuration hash, **OR** deterministic replay of `input_snapshot_sha256` fails to reproduce the logged `MOD 18 / MOD 33` verdict bit-for-bit.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** Unlogged manual override, schema-adapter mismatch, or race condition compromised audit reproducibility.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **IMMEDIATE EXECUTION HALT (`0.00%` New Risk).** Route snapshot to `MOD 28` Live Autopsy and require manual operator hash reconciliation before re-arming.

### CSU-36D: Full Telemetry Attestation Passed (`TRUST_VERIFIED = GREEN`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. All critical feeds fresh (`<= 2.0x` SLA), `>= 3` venues within `<= 35 bps` quorum, NTP clock skew `< 2000 ms`, and `ruleset_yaml_sha256` verified.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Control-Plane Lens:** Input state is verified, reproducible, and safe for downstream `MOD 33` regime evaluation.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **EMIT `MOD_36_STATE = GREEN` (`TRUST_VERIFIED`).** Pass control downstream to `MOD 33` Master Radar (`GREEN` does not raise risk above `0.75%`).

---

## 4. Dynamic Regime Mutation Lookup Matrix (`M1` through `M8`)

| Mutation ID | Initial State | Telemetry Mutation Observed (`Condition A'`) | Mechanical Meaning (`Outcome B'`) | Mandatory Execution Override (`Rule C'`) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **M1: Top-of-Hour GitHub Cron Delay** | `GREEN` (Hourly Cron) | Scheduled `:05` UTC GitHub Actions job queues for `> 45 min` during peak load, pushing heartbeat gap past `90 min`. | Pipeline running on stale prior-hour snapshot. | **Flip to `UNKNOWN` at `90 min` mark**; require `2` consecutive clean hourly cycles to re-arm. |
| **M2: Secondary Feed Missing, Core Fresh** | `GREEN` | Core price/CVD/depth feeds are fresh (`< 5 min`), **but a slow macro/on-chain feed (`e.g., H.4.1` or `Farside`) is delayed**. | Core execution tape is valid; slow background feed is pending publication. | Keep core state `AMBER` (do not freeze exits), apply `MOD 31 M7` 50% caution penalty to the missing macro/on-chain sub-metric. |
| **M3: Stablecoin De-Peg Quorum Split** | `GREEN` (3-Venue Quorum) | Coinbase USD and Kraken USD agree within `8 bps`, **but Binance USDT trades `+45 bps` higher** due to USDT de-peg pressure. | Cross-venue dispersion caused by quote-currency (`USDT` vs `USD`) divergence, not API failure. | Exclude USDT venue from USD reference price; anchor execution exclusively to **Domestic USD quorum (`MOD 07`)**. |
| **M4: Post-Outage Re-Arm Verification** | `UNKNOWN` (Frozen) | Outage resolves and fresh telemetry arrives on Cycle 1. | Single packet could still contain catch-up backlog spikes. | **Hold Freeze Through Cycle 1;** lift `UNKNOWN` lock only after **Cycle 2** passes all SLA & quorum checks. |
| **M5: Manual Operator Override Logged** | Any Active State | Operator manually overrides a `MOD 33` or `MOD 18` rule during live trading. | Discretionary intervention bypassed deterministic ruleset. | **Force `override_audit_flag = TRUE`** in JSON log; mandate `MOD 28` post-trade autopsy regardless of P&L outcome. |
| **M6: Local VPS / Mobile Clock Drift** | `GREEN` | System clock drifts `> 2000 ms` from UTC NTP reference. | Candle-close timestamps (`1H / 4H` bar-count rules in `00C`) could misalign across exchanges. | Trigger `AMBER` clock-sync warning; block bar-close entries until NTP sync restores `< 500 ms`. |
| **M7: Schema Adapter Unmapped Key Spike** | `GREEN` | `cortex_schema_adapter.py` reports `> 5` newly unmapped critical keys after an upstream API schema change. | Upstream vendor changed JSON field names silently. | **Flip to `UNKNOWN` (`Schema Drift Halt`)** until `KEY_ALIAS_MAP` is updated and unit-tested. |
| **M8: Clean Audit Trail Attestation** | Routine Cycle | Every cycle writes immutable `(input_hash, ruleset_hash, verdict_hash)` to Git history. | Guarantees 100% walk-forward backtest fidelity without look-ahead bias. | Archive `as_of_timestamp_utc` alongside `publication_timestamp_utc` for all delayed macro/ETF prints. |

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 37: EXECUTABLE LIQUIDITY, NET-3.0R SLIPPAGE & EQUITY-CURVE GOVERNOR
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 4 & Tier D Control-Plane Execution & Portfolio-Heat Governor (Verifies L2 executable depth, post-cost `Net R >= 3.0`, stressed stop slippage, and temporal drawdown caps).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT `MOD 40` + Qwen `MOD 40` + Perplexity `MOD 38`) — ensuring theoretical chart setups survive real-world order-book impact, trading fees, stressed stop-loss slippage, and multi-trade temporal loss limits without ever exceeding the **0.75% Sovereign Risk Shield**.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_37"
entry_type: "EXECUTABLE_LIQUIDITY_AND_EQUITY_GOVERNOR"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_4_TIER_D_EXECUTION_AND_HEAT_GOVERNOR"
title: "Executable Liquidity, Net-3.0R Slippage & Equity-Curve Governor"

# SUPREME GOVERNANCE PRIORITY
governance_priority: 2 # Sits directly beneath MOD 36 Trust Gate and above MOD 18 Order Entry
advisory_invariant: "Advisory & hard-veto decision governor only; never initiates autonomous orders or raises risk above 0.75%."

# EXECUTABLE LIQUIDITY & POST-COST 3.0R FORMULAS
execution_cost_equations:
  r_planned_usd: "(Position_Size_BTC * Stop_Distance_USD) + Estimated_Roundtrip_Fees_and_Entry_Slippage_USD"
  r_stressed_usd: "(Position_Size_BTC * Stop_Distance_USD) + Stressed_Exit_Slippage_USD + Roundtrip_Fees_USD"
  net_reward_to_risk: "(Target_T2_USD - Expected_Avg_Fill_USD - Roundtrip_Costs_Per_BTC) / (Expected_Avg_Fill_USD - Hard_Stop_USD + Stressed_Slippage_Per_BTC)"

# L2 ORDER BOOK & VENUE HEALTH THRESHOLDS
venue_liquidity_thresholds:
  max_top_of_book_spread_bps: 15.0 # Sustained > 15 bps for 30 min -> DEGRADED / BLOCK
  min_depth_vs_30d_median_pct: 60.0 # Depth down > 40% vs 30D median -> DEGRADED
  max_order_impact_of_10bps_depth_pct: 25.0 # Intended spot order must not consume > 25% of +/-10 bps L2 book depth
  min_net_r_multiple: 3.0 # Hard post-cost asymmetry floor (00C + MOD 18)

# TEMPORAL EQUITY-CURVE & PORTFOLIO HEAT INVARIANTS
equity_governor_limits:
  max_portfolio_heat_pct: 0.75 # Sum of open risk + pending entry risk across ALL positions <= 0.75%
  daily_loss_budget_pct: -1.50 # Day realized + open P&L <= -1.50% (2 full stops) -> 24H FLAT-LOCK
  weekly_loss_budget_pct: -3.00 # Rolling 7D P&L <= -3.00% -> 7-DAY COOLDOWN + MOD 28 Autopsy
  trailing_20_trade_min_avg_winner_r: 2.50 # Below 2.50R over 20 trades -> Strategy Drift Half-Risk (0.375%)

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  amber_reduced_risk_pct: 0.375
  red_lock_risk_pct: 0.00
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Execution & Drawdown Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Post-Cost `Net R >= 3.0` Law (`CB-01`):** A setup showing `3.3R` on a clean chart that degrades to `2.6R` after bid-ask spread, L2 book walk, maker/taker fees, and stressed stop slippage is **mathematically negative-expectancy** under your system. `MOD 37` blocks any entry where `Net_R < 3.0`.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Portfolio-Wide Heat Ceiling (`CB-02`):** Multiple staggered or re-entered tranches (`e.g., T1 + T2`) can never sum to more than **`0.75%` total portfolio equity at risk** (`0.375%` in Amber regimes). If an existing tranche still carries `0.40%` open risk, a secondary add is capped at `0.35%`.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Temporal Loss Budgets (`CB-03`):** Per-trade caps do not stop clustering losses during hostile regime transitions. Hitting **`-1.50%` daily P&L** triggers a mandatory **24-hour `FLAT-LOCK`**, and hitting **`-3.00%` rolling 7-day P&L** enforces a **7-day cooldown + `MOD 28` Autopsy**.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Cold Reserve vs. Execution Float Isolation (`CB-04`):** Operational trading capital is strictly bounded to a prefunded spot execution float; `MOD 37` never assumes instant access to 2-of-3 multi-sig cold reserves (`72-hour operational wall`, `MOD 32`).

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-37A: Executable Depth Degradation or Net-R Collapse (`NEW_ENTRY = BLOCKED`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Top-of-book spread widens above **`15 bps`** for `30+ minutes`, **OR** `1%` L2 book depth drops **`> 40%` below its 30-day median**, **OR** intended spot order size exceeds **`25%` of `+/-10 bps` L2 depth**.
  2. Recomputing entry/stop/target with realistic L2 book walk and fees yields **`Net_R < 3.0`** or **`R_stressed > 0.75%` (`0.375%` Amber)**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Execution Lens:** Thin book and adverse selection will eat the theoretical chart edge via entry/exit slippage.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK NEW ENTRY (`MOD_37_STATE = RED`)** or scale down order size / switch strictly to passive post-only limit ladder (`MOD 21`) until `Net_R >= 3.0` and `R_stressed <= approved_risk_budget`.

### CSU-37B: Daily or Weekly Loss Budget Exhausted ("Temporal Drawdown Lock")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Intraday realized + unrealized P&L reaches **`<= -1.50%`** of portfolio equity (`two full 0.75% stops`), **OR** rolling 7-day P&L reaches **`<= -3.00%`**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Governance Lens:** Strategy is out of phase with market microstructure or experiencing a regime transition; continuing to trade risks compounding tilt/whipsaw losses.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE `FLAT-LOCK` (Overrides all `MOD 33` entry permissions):**
    * If Daily `-1.50%` breached: **24-hour hard lock** (`0.00%` new risk).
    * If Weekly `-3.00%` breached: **7-day hard cooldown** + mandatory `MOD 28` Live Autopsy before re-arming.

### CSU-37C: Portfolio Heat Invariant Breach ("Stacked Tranche Cap")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Proposed new order risk plus existing open-position risk (`measured to hard stops`) exceeds **`0.75%` of total equity** (`or 0.375%` under Amber regimes).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Governance Lens:** Stacked re-entries would breach the single-event Sovereign Risk Shield if a sudden gap hits all stops simultaneously.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **REJECT OR TRIM INCREMENTAL ORDER** so `Total_Open_Portfolio_Heat <= Max_Approved_Risk_Pct` (`0.75%` or `0.375%`).

### CSU-37D: Realized Expectancy / CUSUM Drift Warning ("Exit-Engine Recalibration")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Over a trailing window of `20` completed trades, realized average winner drops below **`2.50R`** (design target `>= 3.0R`) or cumulative sum (`CUSUM`) of `(R_i - E[R])` breaches control limit `h`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Governance Lens:** Either stops are too tight for current Garman-Klass volatility (`MOD 12`) or Stage 1/Stage 2 exits are cutting winners prematurely.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **THROTTLE MAX RISK TO `0.375%` (`AMBER`)** and trigger `MOD 18` exit-parameter calibration review until `20` validated trades restore expectancy.

---

## 4. Dynamic Regime Mutation Lookup Matrix (`M1` through `M8`)

| Mutation ID | Initial State | Telemetry Mutation Observed (`Condition A'`) | Mechanical Meaning (`Outcome B'`) | Mandatory Execution Override (`Rule C'`) |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **M1: Pre-Event Depth Evaporation** | `GREEN` (Valid Setup) | 15 minutes before a macro print, L2 `1%` bid depth drops `-45%` (`like Nov 13's thin $72M book`), pushing `R_stressed` to `1.15%`. | Stop-loss slippage risk jumped 50% above the `0.75%` ceiling. | **Automatically Scale Down BTC Size by 35%** (or abort) so `R_stressed <= 0.75%`. |
| **M2: Passive Limit Slice Rescue** | `AMBER` (Size > 25% of 10bps book) | Single market/aggressive limit order would walk 18 bps of book, dropping `Net_R` from `3.2R` to `2.7R`. | Market impact destroys `3.0R` asymmetry if executed in one clip. | **Route to `MOD 21` TWAP / Post-Only Ladder** (`3` child slices); approve only if blended `Net_R >= 3.0`. |
| **M3: Breakeven Stop Frees Portfolio Heat** | `CSU-37C` (Heat at `0.75%`) | Open Trade 1 hits Stage 1 (`50%` banked) and its trailing stop moves strictly **above entry price (`risk-free / locked profit`)**. | Open downside risk on Trade 1 is now `0.00%` of equity. | **Reset Trade 1 Open Heat to `0.00%`**, unlocking fresh `0.75%` risk budget for a new setup. |
| **M4: Daily Lock Override Attempt** | `CSU-37B` (`-1.50%` Daily Lock) | A textbook `CONFIG_J` Bear Trap prints 4 hours after hitting `-1.50%` daily loss limit. | High-conviction setup appears during mandatory cooling-off period. | **Hard Veto Preserved:** Never bypass the `24h` daily lock; log missed setup for `MOD 28` review. |
| **M5: Exchange API Rate-Limit / Reject Spike** | `GREEN` | Primary execution venue returns HTTP `429/5xx` errors or order-ack latency exceeds `1500 ms`. | Venue execution reliability degraded; stop/exit updates could fail. | **Set `MOD_37_STATE = RED` (`Venue Degraded`)**; halt new entries until venue API latency normalizes `< 300 ms`. |
| **M6: Stressed Stop Gap Adjustment** | Active Open Swing | Weekend or macro volatility doubles Garman-Klass APR (`MOD 12 M5`), increasing expected stop-out slippage. | Historical 20 bps stop-slippage buffer is now too narrow for live volatility. | **Recompute `R_stressed` with `2.0x` slippage buffer**; trim partial exposure if `R_stressed` exceeds `0.90%`. |
| **M7: Fee-Tier / Zero-Fee Pair Shift** | Routine Audit | Execution venue changes maker/taker fee schedule on `BTC/USD` or `BTC/USDT`. | Roundtrip transaction cost changes `Net_R` threshold calculations. | Update `roundtrip_fee_bps` constant in `btc_config.json` before next live entry evaluation. |
| **M8: Post-Cooldown Half-Risk Ramp** | Exiting `7-Day` Weekly Lock | 7-day cooldown and `MOD 28` autopsy complete cleanly. | Re-entering live markets after a `-3.0%` drawdown requires controlled re-acclimation. | **Cap First 5 Post-Cooldown Trades at `0.375%` Risk** (`Half-Risk`) before restoring full `0.75%` ceiling. |

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 38: ETF PRIMARY-MARKET SETTLEMENT & BRRNY FIX-WINDOW ENGINE
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 2 & Pillar 5 Institutional Settlement Mechanics Engine (Extends `MOD 07` by separating intraday creation/redemption arbitrage during the `15:00–16:00 NY` BRRNY fix window from `T+1` lagged flow tables and in-kind vs. cash AP delivery).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT `MOD 39` + Qwen `MOD 36` + Meta `MOD 38`) — incorporating the SEC's **July 29, 2025 in-kind ETP creation/redemption approval**, **T+1 settlement mechanics**, and the **CME CF BRRNY (`15:00–16:00 ET`)** daily NAV benchmark window.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_38"
entry_type: "INSTITUTIONAL_ETF_SETTLEMENT_ENGINE"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_2_5_ETF_PRIMARY_MARKET_SETTLEMENT"
title: "ETF Primary-Market Settlement & BRRNY Fix-Window Engine"

# PILLAR 1 MASTER PERMISSION PRE-CHECK
pillar_1_permission_required: true
icdm_integration: "Adjusts MOD 31 Vector 2 Institutional Flow quality (capped +/- 8 pts; never double-counts Farside prints)."

# PRIMARY TELEMETRY INPUTS (INTRADAY NOWCAST VS T+1 SETTLEMENT)
etf_settlement_telemetry:
  tracked_spot_etfs: ["IBIT", "FBTC", "BITB", "ARKB", "GBTC", "BTC"]
  nav_fix_benchmark: "CME CF BRRNY (15:00-16:00 ET / 20:00-21:00 UTC calculation window)"
  intraday_inav_premium_bps: "((ETF_Market_Price / (BTC_Per_Share * Live_BRRNY_Composite_BTC)) - 1.0) * 10000"
  creation_redemption_route: ["CASH_SETTLEMENT", "IN_KIND_DELIVERY", "UNKNOWN"]
  settlement_lag_convention: "T+1 (Trade date order recorded T+0; BTC/cash settled T+1)"

# DETERMINISTIC THRESHOLDS (INITIAL CALIBRATION PARAMETERS)
settlement_thresholds:
  fix_window_premium_bull_bps: 15.0 # >= +15 bps across >= 4 of 6 ETFs during 15:00-16:00 ET
  fix_window_discount_bear_bps: -15.0 # <= -15 bps across >= 4 of 6 ETFs during 15:00-16:00 ET
  session_discount_persistence_pct: 60.0 # >= 60% of US session trading at discount -> AP redemption pressure

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  redemption_headwind_max_risk_pct: 0.375
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (ETF Primary-Market Anti-Trap Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — In-Kind vs. Cash Creation Distinction (`CB-01`):** Following the SEC's **July 29, 2025 approval of in-kind creations and redemptions** for spot Bitcoin ETPs, Authorized Participants (APs) can deliver or receive existing BTC inventory directly without executing a public spot-market buy/sell order. Never assume an in-kind share creation equals fresh public-market USD buying unless confirmed by **Domestic USD Spot CVD (`MOD 05 / MOD 19`)**.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — `T+1` Flow Lag Awareness (`CB-02`):** Public ETF flow tables (e.g., Farside Investors) publish after evening share-count updates and reflect **prior-session (`T+0`) orders settling `T+1`**. Never treat an evening Farside print as a live intraday momentum trigger; use **intraday iNAV premium/discount breadth** as the contemporaneous nowcast and Farside prints strictly as `T+1` settlement confirmation.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — The `15:00–16:00 ET` (`20:00–21:00 UTC`) BRRNY Fix-Window Guard (`CB-03`):** Major US spot ETFs (including IBIT) strike daily NAV against the **CME CF BRRNY** window (`15:00–16:00 ET`). Persistent `<= -15 bps` ETF discounts entering `15:00 ET` signal AP redemption arbitrage (selling spot into the fix)—block new spot entries until the `16:00 ET` (`21:00 UTC`) fix completes.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Never Infer Flow From Share Price Alone (`CB-04`):** High ETF share volume with zero iNAV premium/discount represents secondary-market share rotation between equity traders, **not** primary-market BTC creation or redemption.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-38A: BRRNY Fix-Window Creation Arbitrage ("Mechanically-Backed Spot Bid")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. During **`15:00–16:00 ET` (`20:00–21:00 UTC`)**, at least **`4 of 6` major spot ETFs** (`IBIT, FBTC, BITB, ARKB, GBTC, BTC`) trade at a persistent intraday premium **`>= +15 bps`** above indicative NAV (`BTC_per_share * BRRNY_composite`).
  2. Confirmed by **positive Domestic USD Spot CVD (`MOD 05`)** and **Coinbase Premium `>= +0.03%` (`MOD 19`)**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** APs are capturing the ETF premium by buying spot BTC across BRRNY constituent venues (Coinbase, Kraken, Bitstamp, Gemini, LMAX) to hedge cash creations into the `16:00 ET` fix.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **AWARD CONFLUENCE CREDIT TO `MOD 18` (`-6 pts` ICDM fragility)**; defer `Stage 1` profit-taking until after the `16:00 ET` (`21:00 UTC`) fix completes, and authorize `0.75%` full-risk long setups if `MOD 33` permits.

### CSU-38B: Persistent Intraday Discount & AP Redemption Nowcast ("Pre-Print Distribution")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. At least **`4 of 6` spot ETFs** trade at an intraday discount **`<= -15 bps`** for **`>= 60%` of the US session** (or into the `15:00–16:00 ET` fix window), **AND** prior `3-day` settled flows are net negative (`< -$250M`).
  2. Confirmed by negative Coinbase Premium (`< -0.05%`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** APs are redeeming ETF shares and unloading spot BTC across constituent venues ahead of tonight's `T+1` outflow report (`MOD 17 M8` NY-close dump).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK ALL NEW SPOT LONGS (`+8 pts` ICDM fragility)**; if holding an active swing long, tighten trailing stop before `20:00 UTC` (`15:00 ET`) and accelerate `Stage 1` exit to VAL loss.

### CSU-38C: In-Kind Creation / Redemption Re-Classification ("Zero Public-Book Impact")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Issuer daily holdings file reports a large share creation or redemption (`>= $200M`), **but intraday iNAV premium/discount stayed flat (`-5 bps to +5 bps`) AND Domestic USD Spot CVD showed zero corresponding impulse**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** Institutional holder executed an **in-kind basket transfer** (`SEC July 2025 rule`), moving existing BTC into/out of the trust wrapper without hitting public spot order books.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **DO NOT CHASE OR PANIC-SELL ON HEADLINE FLOW NUMBER.** Tag flow event as `IN_KIND_NEUTRAL` and keep execution anchored to live `MOD 05 / MOD 30` spot order flow.

### CSU-38D: Post-Redemption Settlement Exhaustion ("T+1 Overhang Cleared")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following `3+` days of heavy redemptions, intraday ETF discounts compress from `-25 bps` back to **`>= 0 bps`**, Domestic USD Spot CVD turns positive, and price reclaims 4H VAL (`MOD 11A`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Settlement Lens:** AP redemption selling pressure has subsided; secondary demand is absorbing remaining supply.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LIFT REDEMPTION VETO;** permit `0.375%–0.75%` spot reclaim entry subject to `MOD 33` Master Regime and `R >= 3.0`.

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 39: EXCHANGE SUPPLY ACTIVATION & AGED-UTXO DORMANCY ENGINE
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 5 On-Chain Supply-Activation Engine (Tracks dormant/aged UTXO spending and entity-adjusted BTC migration onto exchange-controlled infrastructure *before* realized spot selling prints).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT `MOD 37` + Qwen `MOD 38` + Meta `MOD 39`) — separating *cohort valuation* (`MOD 13–15`) from *exchange-accessible supply activation* (`Supply-Adjusted CDD`, `Dormancy`, and `Point-in-Time Entity-Adjusted Exchange Net Position Change`) with a strict anti-double-counting cap against `MOD 15`.

---

# DETERMINISTIC THRESHOLDS (INITIAL CALIBRATION PARAMETERS)

  supply_shock_outflow_30d_pct: -2.0 # Exchange balances down > -2.0% over 30D -> Structural supply sink

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (On-Chain Supply Anti-Noise Rules)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Exchange Deposit $\neq$ Immediate Market Sell (`CB-01`):** Coins moving into exchange wallets may be intended for OTC settlement, derivatives collateral, or institutional custody rather than immediate spot liquidation. An exchange inflow spike **never** triggers a standalone market exit unless corroborated by deteriorating spot structure (`Spot < STH Realized Price`, `USD Spot CVD < 0`, or `4H POC/VAL rejection`).
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Entity-Adjustment Mandatory (`CB-02`):** Raw on-chain transfer volume and unadjusted Coin Days Destroyed (`CDD`) are heavily distorted by internal exchange cold-to-hot wallet consolidations. Only **entity-adjusted** exchange flows and **supply-adjusted CDD** may enter `MOD 39`.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Anti-Double-Counting Cap vs. `MOD 15` (`CB-03`):** Because `MOD 15` already tracks LTH-to-STH wealth transfer, `MOD 31` ICDM takes **`max(MOD_15_points, MOD_39_points)`**—preventing the same old-coin distribution wave from being penalized twice.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Low Label Confidence Quarantine (`CB-04`):** If an on-chain provider flags `exchange_label_confidence == LOW` (unidentified cluster migration), tag `MOD_39_STATE = UNKNOWN` (`MOD 36`) rather than triggering a false distribution alert.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-39A: Aged-UTXO Awakening & Exchange Supply Overhang ("Pre-Distribution Warning")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Supply-Adjusted CDD `>= +3.0 sigma`** (dormant `1Y+` UTXOs awakening) **AND** **72-hour entity-adjusted net exchange inflow `> +12,000 BTC`** (`HIGH` label confidence).
  2. **Structural Confirmation:** Price trades below `STH Realized Price` (`MOD 13`, `STH-MVRV < 1.00`) OR fails to hold above `4H POC / VAH` with weakening `USD Spot CVD` (`MOD 05`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Dormant holders have moved physical inventory onto exchange order books into weak spot demand—creating overhead supply overhang.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK NEW SPOT LONGS (`+7 pts` ICDM fragility, subject to `max(MOD 15, MOD 39)` cap)**; if holding an active swing long, **accelerate `Stage 1 (50%)` profit exit** on the first 4H close below POC.

### CSU-39B: Structural Supply Sink & Re-Dormancy ("Illiquid Squeeze Tailwind")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **30-day exchange reserves drop `<= -2.0%`** (persistent entity-adjusted net exchange outflows for `>= 7 consecutive days`) **AND** **illiquid supply increases** over 30 days.
  2. **Supply-Adjusted CDD & Dormancy** remain at or below baseline (`Z < +1.0`), proving long-term holders are locking coins away rather than spending.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Available exchange float is contracting; shallow pullbacks get absorbed quickly once `MOD 33` permits trend entries.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **AWARD CONFLUENCE CREDIT TO `MOD 18` (`-6 pts` ICDM fragility)**; allow `Stage 2` trailing runners extra room (`hold through first pullback above 4H POC`) when `MOD 33` is in `REGIME_1` or `REGIME_2`.

### CSU-39C: Internal Custodian / Exchange Consolidation ("False Inflow Filter")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Raw CDD or gross transfer volume spikes, **but entity-adjusted net exchange inflow stays `~0 BTC`** (coins moved within the same custodian or exchange entity).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Routine operational wallet maintenance—zero net increase in sellable exchange inventory.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **SUPPRESS SUPPLY ALERT (`MOD_39_STATE = GREEN / NEUTRAL`).** Zero change to active risk or exit posture.

### CSU-39D: Post-Distribution Inflow Exhaustion ("Overhang Absorbed")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following a `CSU-39A` inflow spike, 72H entity-adjusted net exchange flows flip back negative (`net outflows`), `Supply-Adjusted CDD` drops `< +1.0 sigma`, and price reclaims 4H VAL/POC with positive `USD Spot CVD`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **On-Chain Supply Lens:** Deposited supply has been absorbed by buyers without breaking structural support.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **LIFT SUPPLY OVERHANG VETO;** re-enable standard `MOD 18 / MOD 33` entry evaluation (`R >= 3.0`).

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 40: MINER TREASURY STRESS & HASHPRICE ECONOMICS ENGINE
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 5 Structural Supply-Side Economics Engine (Detects deteriorating miner unit revenue via `Hashprice`, network difficulty retarget pressure, fee-share compression, and corroborated miner-to-exchange transfers).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Synthesized from the November 13, 2026 Cross-Model Architecture Audit (ChatGPT `MOD 38` + Qwen `MOD 37` + Meta `MOD 36` + Perplexity `MOD 37`) — isolating obligated miner supply economics from investor cohorts (`MOD 13–15`) while strictly distinguishing *gross hashprice compression* from *confirmed miner-to-exchange selling*.

---

## 1. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block (Python / Hermes / NotebookLM Index)

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_40"
entry_type: "MINER_ECONOMICS_AND_SUPPLY_STRESS_ENGINE"
waterfall_pillar: "PILLAR_5_MINER_TREASURY_STRESS"
title: "Miner Treasury Stress & Hashprice Economics Engine"

# PILLAR 1 & ANTI-DOUBLE-COUNTING GOVERNANCE
pillar_1_permission_required: true
anti_double_count_rule: "Miner-to-exchange transfers scored here are excluded from MOD 39 non-miner exchange inflows."
epistemic_disclosure: "Hashprice measures gross expected revenue per PH/day — NEVER assume an unverified electricity breakeven cost."

# PRIMARY TELEMETRY INPUTS (HASHRATE INDEX / MEMPOOL.SPACE / ON-CHAIN)
miner_economics_telemetry:
  btc_hashprice_per_ph_day: "Expected daily BTC revenue per PH/s (subsidy + fees / network difficulty)"
  usd_hashprice_per_ph_day: "Expected daily USD revenue per PH/s (BTC_Hashprice * Spot_USD)"
  hashprice_365d_percentile: "Rolling 365-day percentile rank of USD & BTC hashprice"
  next_difficulty_retarget_pct: "Projected difficulty adjustment % from mempool.space block-pace telemetry"
  fee_share_of_block_reward_pct: "Transaction fees / (Block subsidy + Transaction fees) * 100"
  network_hashrate_7d_vs_30d_pct: "7-day vs 30-day estimated network hashrate divergence (capitulation proxy)"
  miner_to_exchange_flow_zscore: "Z-score of BTC moving from identified mining pools to exchange wallets"

# DETERMINISTIC THRESHOLDS (INITIAL CALIBRATION PARAMETERS)
miner_stress_thresholds:
  hashprice_stress_percentile_max: 15.0 # Rolling 365D <= 15th percentile -> Revenue compression
  difficulty_squeeze_retarget_min_pct: 4.0 # Difficulty rising >= +4.0% while hashprice depressed
  hashrate_capitulation_drop_pct: -5.0 # 7D hashrate >= 5% below 30D average -> Unprofitable rigs shutting off
  miner_exchange_inflow_zscore_min: 2.0 # Corroborated miner-to-exchange transfer burst

# RISK & SIZING CONSTANTS (PILLAR 4)
risk_parameters:
  max_portfolio_risk_pct: 0.75
  miner_distribution_max_risk_pct: 0.375
  leverage_permitted: 1.0 # Strict Unleveraged Spot Only
```

---

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Miner Telemetry Anti-Fallacy Rules)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Gross Revenue $\neq$ Net Profit (`CB-01`):** `Hashprice` (`USD` or `BTC` per `PH/day`) measures gross expected mining revenue—it does **not** know private fleet energy contracts (`$/kWh`), ASIC efficiency (`J/TH`), or debt service. Never invent a hardcoded "miner breakeven price"; rely strictly on rolling `365D` hashprice percentiles and observable on-chain behavior.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Revenue Stress Alone Is Not a Sell Signal (`CB-02`):** Low hashprice alone indicates tight margins, not automatic selling (well-capitalized miners often hold BTC or fund opex via equity/credit). `MOD 40` only restricts long risk when **Hashprice Stress** is corroborated by **elevated Miner-to-Exchange transfers (`Z >= 2.0`)**.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Pool Attribution Caveat (`CB-03`):** Public mining-pool payout addresses aggregate hashpower from thousands of independent sub-miners; treat `miner_to_exchange_flow_zscore` as a directional supply proxy requiring `MOD 05` Spot CVD confirmation, never a standalone trigger.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Weekly Pool Share Cadence (`CB-04`):** Mining pool distribution averages (`e.g., mempool.space` 1W pool stats) update on multi-day windows—never treat weekly pool shares as intraday signals.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-40A: Corroborated Miner Treasury Distribution ("Obligated Supply Overhang")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Hashprice Compression:** Rolling 365D `USD Hashprice` falls **`<= 15th percentile`** while transaction fees contribute **`< 3%` of block rewards** (or next difficulty retarget projects **`>= +4.0%`**, squeezing margins further).
  2. **Corroborated Supply Movement:** `miner_to_exchange_flow_zscore >= +2.0` over a 72H window while `USD Spot CVD` weakens below resistance.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Supply-Side Lens:** Margin-compressed miners are actively transferring treasury BTC to exchanges to cover operating/power obligations—creating persistent overhead sell pressure on rallies.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **CAP RISK AT `0.375%` (`AMBER` | `+8 pts` ICDM fragility)**; block breakout chasing (`MOD 30 CONFIG_A`) and take `Stage 1 (50%)` profits at the first structural resistance (`POC / VAH`).

### CSU-40B: Hashrate Capitulation & Post-Flush Miner Reset ("Generational Bottoming Signal")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Rig Shut-Off & Difficulty Relief:** 7D network hashrate drops **`>= -5.0%` below 30D average** (triggering a negative difficulty adjustment projection `<= -3.0%`), **AND** `miner_to_exchange_flow_zscore` spikes `>= +3.0` and then **decays back `< +1.0` within 48–72 hours**.
  2. **Structural Reclaim:** Price reclaims swept 4H VAL (`MOD 11A`) with positive `USD Spot CVD` and `Hashprice` 5D slope turning upward.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Supply-Side Lens:** High-cost rigs have unplugged, forced miner treasury liquidation has exhausted itself, and upcoming downward difficulty retarget restores margins for surviving miners.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **RE-ARM FULL ENTRY PERMISSION (`-5 pts` ICDM fragility)**; authorize `0.375%–0.75%` spot reversal entry (`MOD 30 CONFIG_F / CONFIG_J`, `R >= 3.0`).

### CSU-40C: Uncorroborated Margin Squeeze ("Watch-Only Caution")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `USD Hashprice` drops `<= 15th percentile`, **but `miner_to_exchange_flow_zscore` remains `< +1.0`** (miners are holding reserves or funding opex off-chain).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Supply-Side Lens:** Economic stress exists, but zero incremental BTC is hitting exchange order books.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **EMIT `MOD_40_STATE = AMBER` (Advisory Warning Only).** Do not restrict `MOD 33` entries unless `miner_to_exchange_flow_zscore` crosses `>= +2.0`.

### CSU-40D: Fee-Market Surge & Hashprice Expansion ("Healthy Network Tailwind")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. `USD Hashprice` trends above its 50th percentile with rising on-chain fee share (`> 8%` of block rewards) and `miner_to_exchange_flow_zscore <= 0.0` (miners accumulating).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Supply-Side Lens:** Strong network block-space demand and healthy miner margins eliminate forced treasury selling.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **SET `MOD_40_STATE = GREEN`.** Full `0.75%` risk ceiling permitted when `MOD 33` is in `REGIME_1` or `REGIME_2`.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Secular Cycle Filters)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Week-1 Leverage Killer Timer (`CB-01`):** Whenever Bitcoin sets a new All-Time High with record Open Interest and positive funding, the first violent flush is **days** away, not months away. Start a **10-day countdown timer** on Day 0 (`ATH high + ATH close`). Watch daily closes (`-10%` in `3–8` days) rather than intraday hourly noise.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Partial Laddering Over All-In/All-Out Guesses (`CB-02`):** Tops are rounded multi-week processes, not single prints. Never dump 100% of sovereign holdings on a single target price; scale out in **laddered tranches** (e.g., `4 x 5%` tranches) when marginal highs stall, and let `Rule 1` (`-10%` daily close) confirm whether the distribution is fatal.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Descriptive Filter vs. Tradable Trigger (`CB-03`):** In 2024–2025, Bitcoin printed `33` new ATHs—`32` resolved higher because the overshoot above prior peaks was wide (`+13%` to `+50%+`), and only the `33rd` crashed because the overshoot was flat (`+1.4%` to `+2.4%`). Never short or exit a secular bull market prematurely during wide `+13%+` continuations.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Diminishing Cycle Multiples Discipline (`CB-04`):** Cycle multiples compressed from `3.49x` (2017 -> 2021) to `1.83x` (2021 -> 2025). Calibrate future cycle harvest bands conservatively (`$172k–$187k` base / `$230k–$265k` bull stretch) rather than extrapolating parabolic 2017 multiples.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-41A: Rule 1 — First-Week Post-ATH Leverage Flush ("The 10-Day Countdown")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. **Day 0 Ignition:** BTC prints a new All-Time High while aggregate Open Interest sits at record highs (`>= 90th percentile`) and perpetual funding is positive (`MOD 02`).
  2. **Days 1–10 Countdown:** Monitor daily UTC closes against the Day 0 ATH high.
  3. **Flush Trigger:** Daily close drops **`<= -10.0%` below ATH within Days 3–8** (and/or intraday liquidation wick reaches **`-17%` to `-44%` within Days 4–9**).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Record post-ATH leverage triggers an immediate mechanical margin-wipeout wave (`4/4` historical cycle hit rate: 2017 `-44.6%`, 2021 `-19.4%`, 2024 `-17.6%`, 2025 `-19.2%` / `$19B` liquidation).
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sharp Week-1 liquidation flush clearing over-leveraged breakout chasers; true cycle bottom takes `100+` days if it is a final top, or forms a mid-cycle shakeout base if overshoot was wide.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Days 0–3 (Pre-Flush):** Tighten trailing stops on tactical swing positions (`MOD 18`) and take Stage 1 profits (`50%`) into record funding (`MOD 02B`) before the flush strikes.
  * **Days 3–9 (Active Flush):** Enforce **100% Cash Lock** on tactical swing capital (`MOD 03B`). Do not catch the falling knife until daily close stabilizes and `MOD 03A` + `MOD 05A` Domestic USD Spot CVD confirm absorption.

### CSU-41B: Rule 2 & Rule 3 — Flat Double-Top Exhaustion ("+1% to +6% Second-Peak Harvest")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following an initial ATH cluster (`Peak 1`) and a `>= 12%` correction trough (typically `-13%` to `-55%` over `32` to `210` days), Bitcoin rallies back to print a second ATH cluster (`Peak 2`).
  2. **Flat Overshoot Measurement:** `Peak 2` exceeds `Peak 1` by only **`+1.0%` to `+6.0%`** (`1.01x` to `1.06x`, average `+3%` to `+4%`) and begins rounding over with flattening/negative Domestic USD Spot CVD (`MOD 05C` / `MOD 19C`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Marginal demand is exhausted (`Flat = Fatal`): institutional holders are distributing into a rounded double-top ceiling (`71%` historical probability of `> -20%` drop in 90 days, median `-36.1%`; `57%` probability of `-50%` to `-85%` bear market within 500 days).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Step 1 (`+4%` Peak 2 Partial Harvest):** Execute **Tranche 2 Laddered Profit-Taking** (sell partial sovereign/swing tranches into the `+1%` to `+6%` overshoot zone) and immediately arm the `Rule 1` 10-Day Timer.
  * **Step 2 (`Rule 1` Confirmation Exit):** If `-10%` daily close triggers within `3–8` days of `Peak 2`, confirm Fatal Flat Double-Top—exit remaining tactical swing exposure into **100% Cash Lock** and transition to `CSU-41D` Bear Drawdown Defense.

### CSU-41C: Healthy Wide Continuation ("+13% to +50%+ Breakout Expansion")

* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Retain core long exposure and trail stops behind `4H/1D` Volume Profile VALs (`MOD 11/18`), applying only tactical `Rule 1` stop-tightening during Week-1 post-ATH leverage flushes.

Price remains below STH Realized Price (`$75,600`) and 200D MA (`MOD 13B` / `MOD 17`).

# MOD 41.1 — Secular ATH Operational Field Runbook & Countdown Checklist (Pillar 1 & 5)

*Source File: `MOD_41.1_Secular_ATH_Operational_Field_Runbook_and_Countdown_Checklist.md`*

---

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 41.1: SECULAR ATH OPERATIONAL FIELD RUNBOOK & COUNTDOWN CHECKLIST
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 1 and Pillar 5 Operational Execution Protocol (Sitting directly below Module 41 as the active, single-page field-ready cockpit for live cycle distribution, post-ATH leverage flushes, and bear drawdown defense).
**Empirical Foundation:** Codified from `3,341` daily candles across 2017–2026, `107` All-Time High prints, and `39` major Order-25 pivots.

---

## 1. Operating Charter and Live Telemetry Tripwires

This operational runbook provides a real-time execution cockpit when Bitcoin enters price discovery at or above prior All-Time Highs (ATHs). Its objective is to eliminate subjective hesitation, enforce systematic capital harvesting during peak market euphoria, and protect capital from post-ATH leverage flushes and secular bear drawdowns.

### Day 0 Ignition Criteria
A new Secular ATH Cycle event is officially declared when the following telemetry conditions occur simultaneously:
* **Price Discovery Breakout:** Bitcoin prints a new All-Time High on daily and intraday timeframes.
* **Open Interest Overheating:** Aggregate derivatives Open Interest sits at or above the 90th percentile of its 90-day rolling baseline, indicating peak leverage.
* **Perpetual Funding Friction:** Perpetual swap funding rates are positive across major offshore venues, indicating aggressive long taker positioning.
* **System Action:** Start the **10-Day Rule 1 Countdown Timer** on Day 0 at `00:00 UTC`. Record the exact Day 0 ATH high price and Day 0 daily UTC close price.

---

## 2. Chronological 10-Day Countdown Execution Protocol

The first post-ATH leverage flush strikes within days, not months. Historical cycles confirm a 4-for-4 major top hit rate: 2017 exhibited a `44.6%` flush within `5` days; 2021 experienced a `19.4%` drop within `9` days; 2024 saw a `17.6%` drawdown within `6` days; and 2025 suffered a `19.2%` liquidation flush within `4` days.

The following chronological sequence governs active risk parameters across the 10-day window:

* **Days 0 to 2 (Pre-Flush Distribution Window):**
  * **Telemetry Monitoring:** Monitor Coinbase Premium Index Z-scores and United States Spot ETF net flows. Track whether Domestic USD Spot Cumulative Volume Delta (CVD) is expanding alongside price or diverging negatively.
  * **Action:** Tighten trailing stop-losses on tactical swing exposure. Harvest Stage 1 profits by scaling out **50%** of tactical swing positions directly into record positive funding and retail breakout chasing. Core sovereign cold-storage holdings remain untouched.
* **Days 3 to 8 (The Rule 1 Daily Close Tripwire):**
  * **Telemetry Monitoring:** Track daily UTC closes relative to the Day 0 ATH high price. Disregard intraday hourly noise and flash wicks; focus exclusively on the `23:59:59 UTC` closing candle.
  * **Action:** If any daily UTC close finishes at or below **`-10.0%`** from the Day 0 ATH high, **Rule 1 is officially confirmed**. Immediately execute a **100% Cash Lock** on all remaining tactical swing capital. Halt all new long entries. Do not attempt to catch falling knives during the flush.
* **Days 4 to 9 (Liquidation Wick Zone):**
  * **Telemetry Monitoring:** Watch for cascading liquidation wicks dropping between **`-17.0%` and `-44.0%`** intraday.
  * **Action:** Maintain cash preservation. The liquidation engine must fully exhaust before any tactical re-entry is evaluated. Tactical re-entry requires verified passive absorption and positive Domestic USD Spot CVD divergence.
* **Day 10 (Timer Expiration and Overshoot Evaluation):**
  * **Telemetry Monitoring:** If Bitcoin traverses the full 10-day window without triggering a daily close at or below `-10.0%`, the immediate leverage flush threat has subsided.
  * **Action:** Transition immediately to Section 3 to classify the structural overshoot metric.

---

## 3. Structural Overshoot Diagnostic Matrix (Flat vs. Wide)

Whenever Bitcoin returns to retest or exceed an All-Time High following a correction trough of `12%` or greater (spanning 32 to 210 days), the structural quality of the second peak (`Peak 2`) determines whether the move represents a fatal double top or a healthy bull continuation.

| Overshoot Category | Percentage Band | Historical Frequency | Probabilistic Trajectory | Mandatory System Directive |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Fatal Flat Overshoot (Rule 3 Exhaustion)** | **`+1.0%` to `+6.0%`** (Average `+3%` to `+4%`) | Observed at major cycle tops (e.g., 2021 Peak 2 at `$69,000` was `+6.4%`; 2025 Peak 2 at `$126,199` was `+1.4%` to `+2.4%`) | **`71%`** probability of dropping `> -20%` within 90 days (median `-36.1%`); **`57%`** probability of crashing `> -50%` to `-85%` within 500 days | **Execute Tranche 2 Profit Harvesting:** Sell partial laddered tranches into the `+1%` to `+6%` zone. Arm Rule 1. If daily close drops `<= -10%`, exit 100% of tactical exposure to cash. |
| **Healthy Wide Overshoot (Bull Continuation)** | **`+13.0%` to `+50.0%+`** | `32` out of `33` ATHs across the 2024–2025 secular expansion | Continuation into higher macro valuation tiers; routine structural repricing backed by Spot ETF inflows | **Retain Core Trend Exposure:** Keep tactical runners open. Trail stops behind the 4-hour and 1-day Volume Profile Value Area Lows. |

---

## 4. Systematic Tranche Execution and Capital Sizing

Cycle tops are multi-week distributional processes rather than single-tick price events. To capture maximum upside without risking round-trip drawdowns, capital distribution is executed across structured, laddered tranches rather than an emotional single-block exit.

### The Laddered Distribution Protocol
* **Tranche Sizing Structure:** Execute sales in **four equal tranches of `5%` each** (totaling **`20%`** of core cycle holdings) as price navigates the **`+1.0%` to `+6.0%`** Peak 2 band.
* **The Confirmation Gate:** If the market pushes into a Wide Continuation (`+13.0%+`), the remaining **`80%`** of holdings continues riding the secular trend. If the market triggers Rule 1 (daily close `<= -10.0%` within 3 to 8 days), the distribution thesis is confirmed, triggering an immediate exit of all remaining tactical swing capital into **100% Cash**.
* **Risk Limits:** Tactical swing risk is strictly capped at **`0.75%`** of total portfolio equity, with a maximum leverage of **`1.0x`** (Spot only; zero perpetual leverage permitted at secular cycle ATH clusters).

### Master Cycle Harvest Roadmaps
Based on empirical diminishing returns across cycles (compressing from `3.49x` in 2017–2021 to `1.83x` in 2021–2025), two operational models guide distribution targets when the secular trend reclaims `$126,200`:

* **Conservative Harvest Model:**

* **Circuit Breaker 1 (Daily Closes Over Hourly Noise):** Rule 1 execution is evaluated strictly on the `23:59:59 UTC` daily close. Never trigger a full cycle exit based on an intraday hourly flash wick.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 (Never 100% All-Out on Price Alone):** Never liquidate an entire portfolio purely because price reaches a projected target price. Always utilize laddered tranches combined with Rule 1 confirmation.

### PART III — EXECUTION WALKTHROUGH: PHASE BY PHASE

```
  [Break $126.2k] ────────► SWING 1 (Peak 1)
                                │
                                ▼
                       MID-CYCLE SHAKEOUT

                                │

```

#### Phase 1: Swing 1 Distribution (Peak 1)
* **Price Behavior:** Bitcoin traverses price discovery toward either **$172k–$180k** (Conservative) or **$230k–$250k** (Bull Stretch).
* **Execution Directives:**
  * **Days 0–2:** Track Coinbase Premium Z-scores and US Spot ETF flows. Harvest Stage 1 tactical profits (**50% off the table**) directly into overheated positive funding (≥ +0.025%) and retail FOMO breakouts.
  * **Days 3–8 (Rule 1 Timer):** Monitor daily 23:59:59 UTC closes. A daily close ≤ -10.0% below the Day 0 ATH high triggers the **Rule 1 First-Week Flush**.
  * **Days 4–9 (Liquidation Wick Zone):** Stand down in cash while cascading liquidation wicks clear leverage by **−17% to −44%**. Core Sleeve B sovereign multi-sig cold storage remains completely untouched.

#### Phase 2: The Mid-Cycle Shakeout & Buyback Zone
* **Structural Context:** Following Peak 1, the market enters a **−25% to −40% correction trough** spanning 32 to 210 days.
* **Trough Targets:**
  * **Conservative:** $115,000 to $125,000.
  * **Bull Stretch:** $138,000 to $172,000.
* **Buyback Authentication (Mutation M4):** Tactical re-accumulation tranches are authorized **only** when all of the following conditions verify:
  1. **On-Chain Reset:** Short-Term Holder MVRV compresses into deep value (STH-MVRV ≤ 0.85 per MOD 13D).
  2. **Cohort Flow:** 100–10,000 BTC shark/whale wallets transition from distribution to net accumulation (MOD 15C).
  3. **Order-Flow Reclaim:** Spot price and Domestic USD Spot CVD decisively reclaim the **200-Day Moving Average** (MOD 17A) and 4H Volume Profile VAH.

#### Phase 3: Swing 2 Distribution (Peak 2 / The Flat +4% Trap)
* **Price Behavior:** The market rallies out of the shakeout trough back up to test or marginally exceed Peak 1.
* **Rule 3 Diagnostic (Flat vs. Wide Overshoot):**
  * **Flat Overshoot (+1.0% to +6.0%, Average +3% to +4%):** Peak 2 clears Peak 1 by only a sliver ($179k–$187k Conservative or $232k–$265k Bull Stretch) with flat or negative Domestic USD Spot CVD.
  * **The Empirical Law:** Historically, a flat overshoot carries a **71% probability of dropping > −20% within 90 days** (median −36.1%) and a **57% probability of crashing −50% to −85% within 500 days**.
* **Execution Directive:**
  * **Laddered Tranches:** Execute partial sales across **four equal tranches of 5% each** (20% total harvest) throughout the +1% to +6% zone. **Never dump 100% on a price target alone** (CB-02), ensuring upside participation remains if it mutates into a wide continuation (+13%+).
  * **Arm Rule 1 Countdown:** Arm the 10-day daily close countdown timer immediately on Day 0 of Peak 2.

#### Phase 4: Final Bear Cycle Confirmation & Defense
* **Execution Trigger (Rule 1 Confirmation):** Within Days 3 to 8 of the Peak 2 high, if a daily UTC close finishes **≤ -10.0% below the ATH**, the fatal flat double top is confirmed (Mutation M2).
* **Mandatory Action:**
  * Immediately lock **100% Cash** on all remaining tactical swing capital (Sleeve A).
  * Stand down on all dip-buying during the subsequent multi-year bear decline toward the secular floor targets ($50k–$85k Conservative or $98k–$106k Bull Stretch).
  * Deploy deep-value sovereign vault tranches into 2-of-3 multi-sig cold storage (Sleeve B) only when price touches the **200-Week SMA / Realized Price** or drops **16% to 22% below the prior cycle's $126,200 ATH**.

### PART IV — KEY OPERATIONAL CIRCUIT BREAKERS

1. **Daily Closes Over Hourly Noise (CB-01):** Rule 1 execution is evaluated strictly at **23:59:59 UTC daily close**—never panic-exit or lock cash based on an intraday hourly liquidation wick.
2. **Never 100% All-Out on Price Alone (CB-02):** Always sell in laddered 5% tranches at Peak 2; only the confirmed **Rule 1 daily close (≤ -10%)** authorizes a 100% tactical cash lock.
3. **Temporal Alignment to Block 1,050,000 (MOD 46):** Secular cycle tops historically lag the quadrennial halving by **12 to 18 months**. For the Epoch 5 cycle following the April 2028 halving, this roadmap targets the primary distribution harvest window between **April 2029 and October 2029**.

They must be authenticated by order-flow and rough-path filters to distinguish institutional execution from retail traps.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Scenario in Module 42 can authorize a spot decision, all four circuit breakers must pass:

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Diagonal Fallacy (`CB-01`):** Diagonal trendlines and geometric shapes have no causal force. Institutional algorithms execute based on horizontal liquidity pools (Volume Profile VAH/VAL/POC) and order-book depth. Treat classical shapes strictly as *maps* of where retail liquidity is resting, and wait for order-flow authentication.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Bar-Count Discipline (`CB-04`):** A neckline or wedge break requires **1–3 completed 1H bars** (or **1–2 completed 4H bars**) for a sweep-and-reclaim. **4+ consecutive 1H closes** (or **2+ consecutive 4H closes**) below support confirms a true structural breakdown.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Liquidation Contagion (`CB-03`):** Never attempt to trade a Bull Flag or Double Bottom during an active liquidation cascade (`MOD 03`). Wait for OI contraction to decelerate and 15m footprint absorption to print.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pillar 1 Macro Gate:** Verify secular macro permissions before trading bullish reversal or continuation shapes.

---

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-42A: Double Top / Head & Shoulders Breakdown ("Retail Exhaustion & Distribution")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price forms equal highs or a marginal higher high (`+1%` to `+6%` flat exhaustion per `MOD 41`), followed by a drop below the **Horizontal Neckline / 4H VAL**.
  2. **Authentication:** Breakdown is accompanied by **aligned Spot + Perp outflow (`Quad 4` / `MOD 05D`)** and negative Rough Path curvature (`A_P,V <= -1.5 Z`).
  3. Bar-count discipline confirms **4+ consecutive 1H closes** below the neckline.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional sellers used the initial peaks to distribute inventory into retail FOMO; the neckline breakdown confirms exhaustion of bid liquidity.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Sustained markdown toward the next major lower-timeframe Volume Profile POC or Liquidation Cluster.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **ENFORCE 100% CASH LOCK.** If holding an active Spot Swing Long, execute immediate Stage 2 Full Profit Exit.

### CSU-42B: Double Bottom / Inverse H&S / Wyckoff Spring ("Liquidity Sweep Reversal")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price sweeps equal lows or the left shoulder trough, triggering retail stop-losses (`Wyckoff Spring` / `MOD 11A` VAL sweep).
  2. **Authentication:** 15m/1H footprint shows **passive limit-bid absorption (`MOD 06A`)**, and **Domestic USD Spot CVD** turns sharply positive. Price reclaims the **15m/1H Neckline / VAH**.
  3. `MOD 03A` confirms a post-flush OI reset.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Institutional buyers engineered a liquidity sweep to fill large spot bids at a discount, trapping late breakout shorts.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High-probability V-shaped or U-shaped mean-reversion rally.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** upon verified 15m/1H Neckline / VAH reclaim with positive USD Spot CVD.
  * **Position Sizing Formula:** Size strictly via the **0.75% Risk Ceiling** with hard stop beneath the Spring / Double Bottom wick low.

### CSU-42C: Rising Wedge Breakdown ("Leveraged Long Crowding Trap")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price grinds higher within converging upward trendlines into overhead supply (e.g., 4H/1D VAH or 200D MA).
  2. **Authentication:** Volume contracts (`A_P,V` rolling negative), **Domestic USD Spot CVD flattens/falls**, and **offshore Perp OI + funding climb (`MOD 02B` / `Quad 3`)**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The rally is driven entirely by fragile paper leverage rather than genuine spot demand; institutional sellers are capping the advance.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** High vulnerability to a cascading long-liquidation flush once momentum stalls.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **BLOCK NEW LONG ENTRIES. LOCK 100% CASH.** Treat Rising Wedges strictly as top warnings. Exit active tactical longs immediately.

### CSU-42D: Bull Flag / Falling Wedge Breakout ("Volatility Squeeze Continuation")
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price consolidates in a parallel downward channel (Flag) or converging downward lines (Wedge) following a strong bullish impulse.
  2. **Authentication:** **Garman-Klass volatility squeezes (`MOD 12A`)** and leverage voluntarily bleeds (`MOD 03D`: OI drifting down while funding resets), while **Domestic USD Spot CVD holds steady**.
  3. Price breaks above the 15m/1H Flag VAH with **`A_P,V >= +1.5 Z` (`MOD 08A`)**.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** Orderly digestion of a prior impulse; weak hands are shaking out while institutions quietly re-accumulate.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Explosive upside continuation once the volatility squeeze releases.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** on the first 15m/1H pullback retest of the reclaimed **Flag VAH** with positive USD Spot CVD. Size strictly to the **0.75% Risk Ceiling**.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

Before any Fibonacci or Harmonic Impulse signal can authorize a spot decision, all four circuit breakers must pass:

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — No Subjective Anchors (`CB-01`):** Python exclusively dictates the Fib pulls. It queries the 30D or 90D high/low and calculates the sequence. Manual, human-drawn levels adjusting to fit a narrative are blocked.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — The Isolation Ban (`CB-02`):** A `.618` Golden Ratio means nothing if the order book is empty. The calculated Fib level must intersect within `1%` of a dense Volume Profile node (`MOD 11`) AND exhibit live passive absorption (`MOD 06A`) on the 15m footprint.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — ATR Wave Replacement (`CB-03`):** Ban "Elliott Wave" guessing. An impulse is only recognized if the ZigZag algorithm records a move `>= 2.0 ATR` without a single daily/4H close retracing `> 1.0 ATR`.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Macro Trend Filter (`CB-04`):** Deep retracements (`.786`) are for bear-market loading; shallow retracements (`.382` / `.500`) are for secular bull continuations. Apply the exact level appropriate to the active `MOD 33` Radar regime.

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-43A: Golden Confluence Retracement (The "0.618 + POC" Strike)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Python computes the tactical 30D or structural 90D Absolute High-to-Low Fibonacci grid.
  2. Price retraces to the `.500` or `.618` ratio.
  3. **Authentication:** The `.618` level sits within `1%` of the **Volume Profile POC or VAL**.
  4. **Order Flow:** 15m Domestic USD Spot CVD prints positive divergence (`MOD 05A`) and Rough Path `A_P,V >= +1.5 Z`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** High-frequency algorithms and institutional TWAP engines are front-running the Golden Ratio because it perfectly overlaps with a historical heavy-volume liquidity pool.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Asymmetric mean-reversion bounce in the direction of the primary macro trend.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Entry Trigger:** Execute **Unleveraged Spot Long** precisely at the confluence band.
  * **Risk:** Cap at `0.75%`, with a hard stop `1.0 ATR` below the `.618` support.

### CSU-43B: Harmonic Impulse Measurement (The "Wave 3" ATR Thrust)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Following a consolidation or CSU-43A bounce, price initiates a unidirectional thrust.
  2. **Authentication:** The thrust exceeds **2.0 ATR** (14-period) over consecutive 4H/1D candles without a counter-close exceeding `1.0 ATR`.
  3. Spot CVD and Perp CVD are aligned positive (`Quad 1`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** A true, algorithmic "Wave 3" equivalent. Pure momentum expansion fueled by spot accumulation and short-covering.
  * **Probable Trajectory:** Continued upward price discovery until momentum curvature (`A_P,V`) flattens and funding overheats.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** DO NOT sell into the thrust. Trail stop-losses tightly (`1.5 ATR` behind price) and wait for the `MOD 12A` Garman-Klass volatility expansion to peak. Execute Stage 1 profit taking (50%) when the thrust hits the `1.272` Fibonacci Extension.

### CSU-43C: Deep Discount Liquidation Trap (The "0.786 / 0.886" Sweep)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Price slices through the `.618` support, triggering mass retail stop-losses.
  2. Price wicks into the **.786 or .886 Fibonacci Retracement**.
  3. **Authentication:** Massive OI wipeout (`MOD 03A`), negative funding, and heavy 15m limit-bid absorption at the `.786`.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** The "Wave 2" or deep mid-cycle shakeout (equivalent to the `-30%` to `-55%` drop mapped in `MOD 41`). Institutions swept the `.618` liquidity pool to fill orders at the deeper `.786` discount.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** Deploy sovereign multi-sig accumulation tranches (`MOD 18D`) or maximum `0.75%` Spot Swing allocation on the V-shaped reclaim of the `.786`.

Never catch falling knives on listing day.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Physical Flow Over Headlines (`CB-02`):** Do not freeze trading over routine `$1B–$5B` IPOs. `MOD 44` activates **only** when a single equity offering (or stacked cluster) exceeds **`$25B+`** (or an index deletion forces **`$5B+`** in passive crypto-proxy turnover) **AND** `MOD 07` confirms `>= 3` consecutive days of US Spot BTC ETF net redemptions (`<= -$750M` 10-day cumulative).

Keep tactical swing capital in **100% Cash Lock** until the final calendar deadline clears.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Horizon & Barbell Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Time Horizon Is Not a License to Catch Falling Knives (`CB-01`):** Anchoring to April 2028 (`Block 1,050,000`) eliminates short-term emotional panic, **but** Tactical Swing capital (`Sleeve A`) must still obey `MOD 44` and `MOD 45` (`100% Cash Lock` during active ETF outflow streaks and forced deleveraging). Long-term conviction never overrides short-term risk gates.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Supply Cut vs. Peak Window Separation (`CB-02`):** Do not expect a secular cycle top in April 2028 itself. Block `1,050,000` cuts daily new supply from `~450 BTC` to `~225 BTC`; the structural supply deficit compounds over the following **12 to 18 months**, placing the primary `MOD 41` harvest window in **April 2029 – October 2029**.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Diminishing Return Realism (`CB-03`):** As Bitcoin's market capitalization scales past `$1.3T–$2.5T+`, percentage gains per halving epoch compress (`+684%` in `2020–2021` $\rightarrow$ `+97%` in `2024–2025`). Enforce `MOD 41` laddered profit-taking (`4 x 5%` tranches) inside realistic diminishing-return bands rather than holding through a `-54%` to `-77%` post-peak bear market.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Strict Barbell Firewall (`CB-04`):** Keep **Sleeve A (Active Exchange USDT/BTC Swing)** and **Sleeve B (`2-of-3` Dual-Jurisdiction Multi-Sig Cold Storage per `MOD 32`)** completely air-gapped. Sovereign UTXOs acquired at or below the prior-halving price floor (`~$64,000`) remain untouched until `MOD 41` Swing 1 / Swing 2 distribution zones in `2029`.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Treasury-Flywheel Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Discount `mNAV` Is Not Forced BTC Liquidation (`CB-01`):** When a treasury stock trades below `1.0x mNAV` (such as Metaplanet at `~0.49x` or Strategy near `0.93x–1.08x`), issuing equity becomes dilutive to BTC-per-share, **freezing new corporate BTC purchases** and shifting management toward stock buybacks. However, unless unsecured debt maturities or preferred dividend shortfalls force asset sales, discounted `mNAV` removes a *buyer* rather than creating a *forced BTC seller*.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — OTC/TWAP Execution Reality (`CB-02`):** Corporate treasury purchases rarely chase spot order books at market; they execute via institutional OTC desks and algorithmic TWAP slices (`MOD 21`). Never buy a headline treasury purchase announcement (`Form 8-K`) unless price simultaneously holds above `4H VAH` with positive `Domestic USD Spot CVD`.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Remove Phantom Corporate Bid During Compression (`CB-03`):** Whenever leading treasury `mNAV` averages `< 1.15x`, treat the corporate ATM flywheel as **OFFLINE (`0 BTC` marginal demand)**. Require US Spot ETF inflows (`MOD 07`) and `Coinbase USD Spot CVD` to carry 100% of the bullish burden.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Pure `1.0x` Spot BTC Execution (`CB-04`):** Proxy equities carry double-beta risk (`BTC` price beta $\times$ `mNAV` multiple compression + index deletion risk). Execute exclusively in `1.0x` Spot BTC and USDT.

### CSU-47A: Accretive `mNAV` Flywheel Expansion (`mNAV >= 1.35x` + Rising Velocity)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Benchmark treasury `mNAV` (`Strategy` and top-5 corporate holders) holds `>= 1.35x` with positive 14-day slope.
  2. Net 30-day corporate + sovereign BTC treasury additions exceed **`+15,000 BTC`** (`>= ~1.1x` monthly new miner issuance of `13,500 BTC`).
  3. `Pillar 1` is `GATE_GREEN` and `Domestic USD Spot CVD` confirms upward absorption (`MOD 05A`).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

  * **Probable Trajectory:** Persistent structural floor beneath `4H/1D POC` and shallow pullbacks (`-8%` to `-15%`) during bull expansions.
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**

### CSU-47B: `mNAV` Compression & Buyback Pivot (`mNAV <= 1.10x` — Corporate Bid Frozen)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Benchmark corporate treasury `mNAV` compresses to `<= 1.10x` (or `< 1.00x` discount) and 30-day corporate net BTC additions drop below **`+2,500 BTC`**.
  2. Treasury managements pivot capital allocation from buying spot BTC to **repurchasing their own discounted shares** (e.g., Strategy `$1.0B` buyback authorization; Metaplanet `¥60B` buyback program).
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
  * **Sovereign Macro Lens:** A major pillar of 2024–2025 marginal spot demand has stepped off the bid; BTC becomes more sensitive to US Spot ETF flows (`MOD 07`) and macro liquidity (`MOD 22`).
* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
  * **Action:** **STRIP CORPORATE BID ASSUMPTION FROM MODELS (`CB-03`).** Cap tactical breakout risk at **`0.375%` (`GATE_AMBER`)** unless US Spot ETFs print `>= +$400M/day` net inflows and `Coinbase USD Spot CVD` leads independently.

### CSU-47C: Corporate Treasury Balance-Sheet Stress / Restructuring Alert (`mNAV <= 0.65x` + Debt Wall)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. A top-5 corporate BTC holder (`>= 25,000 BTC`) trades below **`0.65x mNAV`** while facing an imminent convertible put date, covenant breach, or unhedged cash-interest shortfall, **AND** on-chain attribution (`MOD 39`) detects corporate custody outflows toward exchange/OTC desks.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**

### CSU-47D: Sovereign Strategic Reserve Adoption Catalyst (`G20` / Central Bank / SWF Allocation)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
  1. Official confirmation (central bank balance sheet, sovereign wealth fund filing, or enacted national treasury execution) of direct sovereign BTC spot accumulation (`>= 10,000 BTC` mandate), validated by multi-day `Coinbase USD Spot CVD` surge (`MOD 05A`) and `4H VAH` breakout.
* **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

* **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**

# PROJECT CORETEX BTC BRAIN — MODULE 48: STABLECOIN SUPPLY RATIO (SSR), USDT.D & NET MINT/BURN VELOCITY
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 1 (Macro Liquidity & Crypto-Native Dry Powder) & Pillar 5 (Settlement-Rail Flows & Exchange Buying Power) — operates directly alongside `MOD 05` (Spot vs. Perp CVD), `MOD 07` (Domestic USD Gateways vs. Offshore), `MOD 16` (Macro Liquidity & Stablecoin Dry Powder), `MOD 22` (Net Fed Liquidity), and `MOD 39` (Exchange Supply & Stablecoin Inflows).
**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:**
1. **November 2026 Redemption Drain Benchmark:** Following a May 2026 peak (`~$312.85B–$322.4B`), total USD-pegged stablecoin supply contracted by **`-$14.2B` to `-$14.9B`** over the 30 days through November 9–12, 2026 (down to `~$289.37B`, the fastest 30-day contraction since June 2022). **`USDT` (`-$8.31B` to `$175.64B`)** and **`USDC` (`-$3.20B` to `$69.66B`)** accounted for `77%` of the outflow while maintaining `$1.00` parity (`USDT ~$0.9992`), confirming an active capital redemption drain rather than a solvency de-peg.
2. **SSR Denominator Distortion & Non-Trading Rails (2026 Regime):** During the November 2026 drawdown, stablecoin supply fell `-4.6%` while BTC fell `> -20%`, pushing stablecoins to `~19.5%` of BTC market cap and compressing the **Stablecoin Supply Ratio (`SSR = BTC_Market_Cap / Stablecoin_Market_Cap`)** toward its `-2σ` lower Bollinger band. Simultaneously, post-GENIUS Act / MiCA corporate payment rails (`Stripe`, `PayPal`, `Visa`) and yield-bearing tokens (`sUSDS`, `USDe`) absorb significant non-trading supply, requiring exchange-level inflow confirmation before treating low `SSR` as active spot buying power.

## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Stablecoin-Liquidity Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Denominator Fallacy (`CB-01`):** `SSR` (`BTC Market Cap / Stablecoin Market Cap`) drops automatically whenever Bitcoin sells off `-20%+`, even while billions in `USDT` and `USDC` are being redeemed out of crypto (as seen in November 2026). Never buy a `-2σ` `SSR` reading until **7-day `USDT + USDC` net supply stops shrinking (`>= $0`)**.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Non-Trading Payment & Yield Noise (`CB-02`):** Corporate B2B settlement (`Stripe`/`Visa`), AI-agent micropayments, and yield-bearing wrappers (`sUSDS`, `USDe`) inflate total stablecoin supply without adding exchange order-book bids. Track **`USDT + USDC` 7D/30D net change** plus **exchange stablecoin netflows** (`MOD 39 M7`), validated by `Domestic USD Spot CVD` (`MOD 05`).
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Domestic `USDC` vs. Offshore `USDT` Quality Gate (`CB-03`):** Institutional US spot accumulation flows through `USD` and `USDC` rails (`Coinbase`/`CME`/`ETFs`), whereas `USDT` dominates offshore leverage. When `USDC` contracts while `USDT` expands offshore, cap tactical risk at **`0.375%` (`GATE_AMBER`)**.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — De-Peg Contagion Lock (`CB-04`):** Any sustained deviation of `USDT` or `USDC` below **`$0.992`** (`> 2 hours`) triggers an immediate **`100% Cash Lock`** (`MOD 16 M7` / `MOD 28 BS-02`).

### CSU-48C: Synchronized `USDT + USDC` Mint Expansion (`7D >= +$1.0B` & `30D >= +$4.0B`)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**

### CSU-48D: High-`SSR` Thin Dry-Powder Exhaustion (`SSR >= +2.00 Z` + Stalled Mints)
* **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**

# PROJECT CORETEX BTC BRAIN — MODULE 52: ROLLING 30-TRADE EXPECTANCY AUDIT & PARAMETER DRIFT GOVERNOR
**Waterfall Tier:** Tier D (Continuous Governance, Statistical Calibration & Anti-Curve-Fitting Control-Plane) — operates directly alongside `MOD 18` (`0.75%` Risk & Two-Stage Exit), `MOD 28` (Live Autopsy & Black Swan Engine), `MOD 30` (`PCF3` Configs `A–J`), `MOD 36` (Determinism Attestation), and `MOD 37` (`Net R >= 3.0` & Equity-Curve Governor).
**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:**
1. **Live Operations Launch Baseline (November 2026):** Ahead of deploying Project CoreTex BTC Brain onto a 24/7 Ubuntu VPS + Hermes Agent + GitHub Actions stack, `MOD 52` establishes the deterministic statistical audit ledger (`trade_audit_ledger.json`) required to verify that live execution matches theoretical **`Net R >= 3.0`** expectancy without silent slippage erosion, regime drift, or emotional parameter tampering.
2. **Expectancy Law (`E[R]`):** Across a rolling window of `N = 30` closed spot trades, true system edge is governed by:
   $$E[R] = (\text{WinRate} \times \text{AvgWin}_R) - (\text{LossRate} \times \text{AvgLoss}_R)$$
   With strict `1.0x` spot-only `0.75%` risk capping every losing trade at `-1.0R` (`-0.75%` equity) and `MOD 37` requiring `>= +3.0R` projected reward, even a `35%` win rate yields `+0.40R` positive expectancy per trade (`+12.0R` / `+9.0%` compounded equity per 30 trades).
