## 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Governance Invariants)

* **Circuit Breaker 1 — Anti-Curve-Fitting Law (`CB-01`):** Changing Z-score thresholds or lookback windows after two or three frustrating losses destroys out-of-sample robustness. Parameter adjustments require **`N >= 30` closed trades** in the audit ledger (`CB-01`), except when a structural Black Swan (`MOD 28`) exposes a verified market-plumbing blind spot.
* **Circuit Breaker 2 — Execution Slippage & Fee Drift Guard (`CB-02`):** If real-world fills on USD spot venues slip `>= 0.25R` worse than `MOD 37` pre-trade L2 projections over 10 consecutive trades, `MOD 52` automatically throttles sizing to **`0.375%` (`GATE_AMBER`)** and enforces passive maker limit execution.
* **Circuit Breaker 3 — Granular Config Quarantine (`CB-03`):** Rather than shutting down the entire brain when one setup struggles, `MOD 52` isolates performance by `MOD 30` Config (`A–J`) and `MOD 33` Regime (`1–5`), quarantining any specific Config that hits **4 consecutive `-1.0R` stop-outs** for a `MOD 28` post-mortem.
* **Circuit Breaker 4 — Immutable Sovereign Risk Ceiling (`CB-04`):** Even if rolling 30-trade expectancy exceeds `+0.80R`, maximum equity risk per trade stays permanently locked at **`0.75%`** (`1.0x` spot only).

## 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (`Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C`)

### CSU-52A: Healthy Positive Expectancy Regime (`Rolling 30-Trade E[R] >= +0.35R`)
* **Trigger Condition A:** Across the last `30` closed spot trades, realized `E[R] >= +0.35R`, average losing trade stays `<= 1.05R` (confirming disciplined stop adherence), and slippage drift vs. `MOD 37` is `< 0.25R`.
* **Observed Outcome B:** The 5-Pillar Waterfall and `MOD 30` configurations are aligned with live market microstructure.
* **Execution Rule C:** **MAINTAIN FULL `0.75%` SPOT RISK AUTHORIZATION** (when `Pillar 1 == GATE_GREEN`) and prohibit discretionary parameter edits (`CB-01`).

### CSU-52B: Slippage / Execution Friction Drift Alert (`Realized R - Modeled R <= -0.25R`)
* **Trigger Condition A:** Over the last `10+` executed spot trades, actual entry/exit slippage + taker fees reduce realized reward by `>= 0.25R` compared to `MOD 37` pre-trade calculations.
* **Observed Outcome B:** Exchange L2 depth or order routing is experiencing higher friction than modeled (e.g., chasing market orders during volatile `1H` candles).
* **Execution Rule C:** **THROTTLE MAX RISK TO `0.375%` (`GATE_AMBER`)** and enforce **100% passive limit-order entry (`MOD 51 CB-03`)** until 10-trade slippage drift drops back below `0.15R`.

### CSU-52C: Single-Config Regime Mismatch (`4 Consecutive -1.0R Losses in One Config`)
* **Trigger Condition A:** A specific `MOD 30` configuration (e.g., `CONFIG_G` breakout or `CONFIG_F` range fade) records **4 consecutive stop-outs** inside the current `MOD 33` regime, while total portfolio drawdown remains within `MOD 37` bounds.
* **Observed Outcome B:** That specific tactical pattern is temporarily mismatched with current order-book behavior.
* **Execution Rule C:** **QUARANTINE ONLY THAT CONFIG (`STATUS = QUARANTINED`)** and generate a `MOD 28` diagnostic entry; keep unaffected `MOD 30` Configs active at normal sizing.

### CSU-52D: Rolling 30-Trade Negative Expectancy Governor (`Rolling 30-Trade E[R] < 0.00R`)
* **Trigger Condition A:** Rolling `30`-trade realized expectancy drops below **`0.00R`** (or cumulative drawdown hits `MOD 37` `-3.00%` weekly lock).
* **Observed Outcome B:** Structural regime shift has degraded edge across multiple waterfall modules.
* **Execution Rule C:** **ENFORCE `0.375%` HALF-SIZE RISK CAP (`GATE_AMBER`)** across all signals, run a full `MOD 28` 30-trade attribution autopsy via Hermes + Gemini, and require `10` consecutive trades at `E[R] >= +0.20R` before restoring `0.75%` sizing.

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 53: HAWKES POINT PROCESSES, JUMP-DIFFUSION DE-NOISING & TRANSFER ENTROPY CAPITAL ROTATION ENGINE
**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 1 (Macro Liquidity & Cross-Asset Rotation) and Pillar 2 (Microstructure, Path Geometry & Order Flow).  
**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Codifies the mathematical separation of endogenous derivatives leverage cascades from exogenous spot accumulation. Solves standard chart blindness during high-volatility liquidity shocks (e.g., flash deleveraging flushes) and external capital suction events (e.g., mega-equity IPOs and index rebalances). Replaces discretionary chart interpretation with Hawkes self-exciting point processes, jump-diffusion drift separation, and cross-asset transfer entropy.

####  **2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)**
Before any trade signal can be authorized under this engine, all four circuit breakers must evaluate clean:
*   **Circuit Breaker 1 — The Hawkes Aftershock Cooling Lock (**   **CB-01**   **):**  In crypto derivatives markets, initial price breaks trigger chain-reaction forced liquidations (self-exciting aftershocks). When a single 4H candle exceeds  **2.0x 14-period ATR**  or prints an absolute move  **> 2.5%** , the market is in an endogenous shock state. All breakout market orders are frozen for  **2 candles (8 hours)** .
*   **Circuit Breaker 2 — Spot CVD Confirmation Mandate (**   **CB-02**   **):**  If price moves violently while aggregate Open Interest drops  **> 3.0%**  and Domestic USD Spot CVD diverges, the move is driven by leveraged stop-harvesting, not institutional accumulation. Chasing moves with a rejection wick  **> 55%**  of the total candle range is strictly prohibited.
*   **Circuit Breaker 3 — Two-Session ETF Inflow Rule (**   **CB-03**   **):**  During external capital drains (e.g., mega-IPOs or equity rebalancing windows), single-day green ETF prints are frequently noise. Re-entry requires  **2 consecutive trading days**  of US Spot ETF net inflows  **> +$100M/day** .
*   **Circuit Breaker 4 — Strict Spot-Only Execution Gate (**   **CB-04**   **):**  Leveraged positioning is barred. If capital suction is active and microstructure indicates whipsaw, portfolio posture must remain  **100% Cash / Invariant Patience** , with execution limited exclusively to passive deep-discount limit bids ("stink-bids").

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

####  **3. Core Conditional Signal Units (Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C)**
#####  **CSU-53A: Hawkes Liquidation Shock & Whipsaw Invalidation (Filter A)**
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    *   **Shock Detection:**  A 4H candle range exceeds  **2.0x 14-period ATR**  or executes an absolute price thrust  **> 2.5%** .
    *   **Whipsaw Invalidation Criteria (At least 2 of 3 met):**
    *  Aggregate Open Interest drops  **> 3.0%**  within the 4H window while Spot CVD fails to reach a new local extreme (MOD 05).
    *  The candle closes with an upper or lower rejection wick accounting for  **> 55%**  of the total candle range.
    *  Price retraces  **> 60%**  of the shock candle within the 8-hour cooling window on contracting volume.
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Microstructure Lens:**  An endogenous liquidation cascade (Hawkes aftershock cluster) where market makers harvest thin stop liquidity. The move lacks organic spot backing and mean-reverts once liquidations exhaust.
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Classify as WHIPSAW (Fakeout).**  Block all directional breakout buying. Maintain core spot inventory untouched. Rest deep-value limit bids below support to capture liquidation wicks (MOD 51).
#####  **CSU-53B: Transfer Entropy Macro Capital Rotation Re-Entry (Filter B)**
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**  Following an external capital vacuum event (e.g., mega-IPO window under MOD 44), monitor the 3 core plumbing gauges:
    *   **US Spot ETF Net Inflows:**  Record  **>= 2 consecutive US trading sessions**  of net inflows  **> +$100M/day**  (composite IBIT/FBTC/ARKB).
    *   **Stablecoin Supply Expansion:**  7-day net issuance of USDT + USDC expands by  **> +0.5%** , with exchange stablecoin reserves showing a net 72-hour increase (MOD 48).
    *   **Cross-Asset Relative Strength:**  The daily  **BTC/QQQ ratio**  prints a confirmed higher low and closes above its  **10-day EMA** .
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Macro Plumbing Lens:**  Transfer entropy flips positive. External liquidity suction has peaked; institutional allocators and retail liquidity are rotating capital out of fiat/equities and back into Bitcoin spot rails.
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **If 0 or 1 Trigger Met (Suction Active):**  Capital preservation protocol active. Treat upward price thrusts as low-liquidity relief rallies. Restrict deployment to defensive tranches on extreme wicks.
    *   **If >= 2 Triggers Met (Rotation Confirmed):**  Green-light transition to active spot accumulation. Authorize full 0.75% portfolio risk deployment when aligned with microstructure gates.
#####  **CSU-53C: Full Breakout Alignment (Dual-Gate Macro-Micro Confluence)**
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    *   **Filter A Status = REAL SPOT TREND:**  Spot CVD trends positively across  **>= 2 consecutive 4H closes** , OI expands steadily without funding overheating (8h funding between  **-0.01% and +0.025%** ), and wick ratio remains  **< 45%** .
    *   **Filter B Status = ROTATION CONFIRMED:**  At least 2 of 3 transfer entropy liquidity conditions are verified positive.
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Confluence Lens:**  High-conviction structural expansion. Exogenous spot capital inflows absorb passive overhead resistance, while derivatives positioning provides healthy fuel without excessive leverage fragility.
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Execute Full Spot Swing Deployment:**  Authorize standard  **0.75% Portfolio Risk**  (MOD 18). Place initial hard stop-loss  **1.0 ATR**  below the breakout base. Trail stops behind 4H Volume Profile VAL/POC levels (MOD 11).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#### 2. Empirical Cycle Audit: Separating Verified History from Speculation

Popular retail narratives compress Bitcoin's history into a 3-stage loop (*Crash -> Sideways Accumulation -> Euphoric Rally*). While the historical price coordinates are factually accurate, conflating past symmetry with future certainty introduces severe asymmetric risk.

| Cycle Window | Peak to Trough | Drawdown Depth | Trough to Next Peak | Upside Multiple | Structural Context & Regime Shift |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **Cycle 1 (2011–2013)** | $31.90 -> $2.00 | **-93.7%** | $2.00 -> ~$1,150 | **575.0x** | Nascent price discovery; thin retail order books; zero institutional participation. |
| **Cycle 2 (2013–2017)** | ~$1,150 -> $150 | **-87.0%** | $150 -> ~$19,800 | **132.0x** | Mt. Gox bankruptcy (Feb 2014) triggered a 12-month bear market bottoming in Jan 2015. |
| **Cycle 3 (2017–2021)** | ~$19,800 -> $3,120 | **-84.2%** | $3,120 -> $69,000 | **22.1x** | ICO bust followed by global zero-rate liquidity expansion (2020–2021). |
| **Cycle 4 (2021–2025)** | $69,000 -> $15,475 | **-77.6%** | $15,475 -> ~$126,000 | **8.14x** | FTX/credit contagion bottom (Nov 2022) followed by US Spot ETF institutional inflows. |

##### Core Analytical Findings (Non-Biased Synthesis)
1. **Historical Phase Anatomy Is Real, Not Guaranteed:** Across four completed cycles, Bitcoin experienced deep deleveraging drawdowns (-77.6% to -93.7%), multi-month base-building ranges, and trend expansions. However, $N = 4$ is a statistically small sample; treating a 15-year streak as an immutable physical law ignores regime maturation.
2. **The -50% Mid-Drawdown Fallacy:** Claiming that a >50% drop from an all-time high automatically signals the "boring accumulation phase" contradicts every prior cycle. Historically, a -50% decline marked the **midpoint** of the bear market—leaving investors exposed to an additional **50% to 85% drop from that -50% level** before the true trough formed.
3. **Compressing Volatility & Upside Decay:** Each successive bull run has produced a materially smaller multiple (`575x -> 132x -> 22.1x -> 8.14x`) as Bitcoin's market capitalization expanded and institutional capital (Spot ETFs, sovereign/corporate treasuries, macro funds) replaced pure retail speculation. Lower structural multiples require stricter entry discipline because forward reward-to-risk is narrower than in 2011–2017.
4. **Macro Liquidity Over Simple Calendar Memes:** While supply halvings reduce programmatic issuance, major expansions and contractions have increasingly aligned with global net liquidity (MOD 22), real yields/DXY (MOD 16), bond volatility (MOD 23), and institutional ETF flow regimes (MOD 38).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

#### 3. Deterministic Condition-State-Action Rules (CSU-56A to CSU-56C)

##### CSU-56A: Mid-Drawdown Narrative Trap (-50% to -65% Off ATH Without Reset Telemetry)
* **Trigger Condition A:** Price is down >= 50% from secular ATH, social/retail commentary declares the "boring accumulation phase" has begun, **AND** MOD 33 remains in `REGIME_3` or `REGIME_4` (negative Coinbase Premium, un-reset OI, or STH-SOPR still bleeding without capitulation flush).
* **Mechanical Reality B:** Historically, -50% drawdowns without full derivatives deleveraging (MOD 03) and on-chain cost-basis reset (MOD 13/14) represent mid-cycle distribution or continuation risk rather than a confirmed floor.
* **Execution Rule C:** **ENFORCE 100% CASH LOCK (`0.00%` New Risk)** or retain existing MOD 34 AEGIS-3 freeze. Reject narrative-driven lump-sum entries until objective telemetry verifies exhaustion.

##### CSU-56B: Verified Post-Capitulation Accumulation Base (True "Boring Bit" Transition)
* **Trigger Condition A:** Deep drawdown accompanied by verified deleveraging flush (MOD 03 OI reset -8% to -17%), STH-SOPR <= 0.96 capitulation print (MOD 14), Garman-Klass volatility compression <= 15th percentile (MOD 12), and positive USD Spot CVD / Coinbase Premium stabilization (MOD 05/19), transitioning MOD 33 into `REGIME_5` or `REGIME_2`.
* **Mechanical Reality B:** Leveraged weak hands have exited, spot supply is transferring from short-term speculators to high-conviction cohorts (MOD 15), and downside asymmetry is structurally compressed.
* **Execution Rule C:** **AUTHORIZE `0.375%` SPOT-ONLY PROBE (`1.0x` Leverage)** under MOD 30 `CONFIG_F` or `CONFIG_J`, scaling to `0.75%` only after MOD 16 Macro Gate confirms `GATE_GREEN` and price reclaims key secular structure (MOD 17).

##### CSU-56C: Diminishing-Return Asymmetry Calibration on Secular Expansions
* **Trigger Condition A:** MOD 33 enters `REGIME_1` (Secular Liquidity Expansion) following a verified accumulation base.
* **Mechanical Reality B:** Because Bitcoin's market cap and institutional ownership compress cycle multiples relative to early retail eras (`8.1x` in Cycle 4 vs. `575x` in Cycle 1), assuming 50x–100x targets distorts position sizing and profit-taking discipline.
* **Execution Rule C:** **ANCHOR EXITS TO TELEMETRY, NOT HYPE TARGETS.** Enforce MOD 18 two-stage exits (`>= 3.0R` minimum net expectancy per MOD 37) and MOD 41 secular ATH cluster rules rather than holding blindly for historical percentage multiples.

#### 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Anti-Overfitting & Tail-Risk Guardrails)

*   **Circuit Breaker 1 — Appendix B & OOS False-Edge Quarantine (CB-01):** Never execute trades based on unadjusted day-hour directional claims, raw taker-buy ratios (`lag-1` corr `0.04`), yesterday's return (`lag-1` autocorr `-0.05` daily / `-0.01` hourly), turn-of-month Day-1 drift, Thursday drift (`-19 bps` OOS, `p=0.414`), or unauthenticated 2–5 bps absorption candles (below taker fees). Green-Monday -> red-Tuesday (`32.7%` win in 2025–2026 vs `47.1%` win / `+0.05%` in 2017–2024 OOS) failed out-of-sample replication and is downgraded to non-binding background trivia.
*   **Circuit Breaker 2 — Tail-Sized Stop Discipline & Notional Compression (CB-02):** Because `1%` of hours carry `28%` of total return variance (`99th` percentile hourly move = `176 bps`, `9x` the `19.5 bps` median), stops sized to median hours are systematically stopped out on Fridays, Mondays, and during the US equity open window (`13:00–18:00 UTC`). Widen structural stops during high-vol windows (`-21.3%` noise stop-out reduction verified across `16,300` bars) and compress BTC notional size proportionally (`MOD 18 M4`) so max risk never exceeds `0.75%` (`0.375%` Amber).
*   **Circuit Breaker 3 — ETF-Era Volume-Surge Day-1 Bounce Ban (CB-03):** While pre-ETF retail cycles (`2017–2024`, `n=127`) averaged `+0.74%` (`57.5%` win) the day after a `> 2.0x` 20D volume surge, the institutional ETF era (`2025–2026`) inverted to `-0.29%` (`39.3%` win; `8.33%` range) due to multi-day AP redemption and liquidation cascades. Classify any `> 2.0x` volume surge as a multi-day shock rather than a Day-1 V-bottom and enforce `MOD 03` / `MOD 34` stand-down.
*   **Circuit Breaker 4 — 00:00 UTC Opening Range Protection (CB-04):** Because `00:00 UTC` (`08:00 MYT`) prints the daily high (`12.3%`) or daily low (`14.5%`) roughly `3x` more often than random (`4.2%`), and **`77.5%` of upward breaks and `75.1%` of downward breaks snap back inside the `00:00 UTC` range before UTC close**, never chase a raw `00:00 UTC` break or fade it without `MOD 11` / `MOD 05` Domestic USD Spot CVD confirmation.
*   **Circuit Breaker 5 — Timezone & Sample-Window Honesty (CB-05):** All daily and monthly aggregations must strictly use **UTC** boundaries (converting to **MYT/SGT** only for display) to prevent cross-month distortion (e.g., Feb 2026 `-14.94%` UTC vs. `-20.25%` SGT).

#### 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C)

##### CSU-57A: US Equity Open DST Peak Window & Same-Day Range Expansion
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    1. Clock enters the active US cash-equity open block: **`13:00–17:00 UTC` (`21:00–01:00 MYT`)** during US Daylight Time (Mar–Nov) or **`14:00–18:00 UTC` (`22:00–02:00 MYT`)** during US Standard Time (Nov–Mar).
    2. **Same-Day Regime Confirmation:** The `14:00–17:00 UTC` block prints wide hourly ranges (`> 1.0%`/hr; `0.61–0.66` correlation with full-day range).
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Microstructure Lens:** Institutional USD spot, ETF Authorized Participant flow, and macro desks dominate order flow (`41.3%` of total daily range occurs inside `13:00–20:00 UTC`), creating fat-tailed price discovery.
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Action:** Apply `1.25x–1.50x` volatility stop buffer on any `MOD 18` authorized spot entry, scale BTC notional size down proportionally to keep equity risk `<= 0.75%`, and prohibit tight scalp stops inside the peak window.

##### CSU-57B: High-Volatility Clustering Carryover (Post-2% Hour / Post-6% Day)
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    1. Prior `1H` candle range exceeds **`2.0%`** (`lag-1` autocorr `0.57`), **OR** prior full UTC day range exceeds **`6.0%`** (`lag-1` autocorr `0.39` IS / `0.53` OOS).
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Microstructure Lens:** Volatility clusters strongly (`1.69%` expected next-hour range vs. `0.62%` baseline; `5.41%` IS / `7.66%` OOS expected next-day range). Aftershocks and liquidity sweeps persist across subsequent bars.
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Action:** Maintain elevated Garman-Klass (`MOD 12`) stop-distance floors for at least `4` subsequent `1H` bars (or `24H` after a `> 6.0%` day). Do not prematurely tighten trailing stops into noise.

##### CSU-57C: 00:00 UTC Opening Range Anchor & Sunday 22:00 UTC Mini-Open
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    1. Price tests or breaks the **`00:00 UTC` (`08:00 MYT`)** first-hour high or low during intraday trading, **OR** clock reaches **Sunday `22:00 UTC` (`06:00 MYT` Monday)** where average hourly range jumps to `0.94%` (`~2x` Sunday's `0.48%` mean).
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Microstructure Lens:** `00:00 UTC` sets the session's structural boundary `26.8%` of all days (`12.3%` high + `14.5%` low) with `>75%` mean-reversion on initial breaks, while Sunday `22:00 UTC` marks institutional pre-open positioning (`MOD 50`).
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Action:** Treat the `00:00 UTC` high/low as a hard horizontal structural reference (`MOD 11` / `MOD 54`). Require `4+` consecutive `1H` closes (`00C`) + `MOD 05/07` Domestic USD Spot CVD confirmation before treating a `00:00 UTC` breach as a structural breakout, and enforce `MOD 50` pre-settlement caution at Sunday `22:00 UTC`.

##### CSU-57D: Saturday Illiquidity vs. High-Vol Storm Override
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    1. Calendar day is **Saturday UTC** (`1.83%` IS / `3.97%` OOS mean daily range — #7 quietest in both eras; `46%` weekday volume, `$540` higher avg institutional block size).
    2. **Regime Split:** Trailing 24H/7D volatility is either bottom-quartile calm (`14.2 bp`/hr baseline) OR top-quartile storm (`40.0 bp`/hr, matching Friday's `43.0 bp`).
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Microstructure Lens:** Calm Saturdays mean-revert inside narrow ranges with wider effective spreads, whereas high-volatility Saturdays behave like full weekday cascades due to thin order-book depth (e.g., 31 Jan 2026 `-6.55%` / `10.15%` range).
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Action:** During calm Saturdays, avoid breakout chasing and enforce passive limit-only orders (`MOD 50 CB-03`). During high-vol regimes (`ICDM >= 60` or `MOD 33 R4`), cancel any Saturday volatility discount and enforce full `100% CASH LOCK`.

| **M4: Below-200D SMA Negative-Drift Regime** | Spot < 200D SMA | Price trades below 200D SMA (`-0.15%`/day OOS vs `+0.41%`/day above, `p=0.0001`; `-0.22%`/day IS vs `+0.15%`/day above, `p=0.0327`). | Proven 9-year secular regime divider: positive drift above 200D SMA, negative drift below 200D SMA. | **Enforce `MOD 17` Risk Cap (`<= 0.375%` Amber or `0.00%` Cash Lock)** below 200D SMA and widen structural stops in ETF-era drawdowns (`3.60%` daily range). |

| **3-Red-Day Streak Mild Bounce** | `n = 252` streaks: **`+0.33%`** next-day mean, **`56.0%`** win | `n = 89` streaks: **`+0.42%`** next-day mean, **`56.2%`** win | **SURVIVES (Soft Context Only):** Reproduces OOS, but remains below-threshold for standalone triggers. |

| **Volume Surge (`>2.0x` 20D MA)** | `n = 127`: **`+0.74%`** next-day mean, **`57.5%`** win (pre-ETF V-bottoms) | `n = 35`: **`-0.37%`** next-day mean, **`40.0%`** win (ETF cascade regime) | **REGIME SHIFT (`CB-03`):** Post-ETF volume surges trigger multi-day unwinds, not Day-1 bounces. |

**Waterfall Tier:** Pillar 1 (Macro Liquidity Plumbing, Sovereign Rate Gates & Scheduled Event Risk Control-Plane) — expands Historical Registry `00C` Entry #33 (*72-Hour Macro Trinity Gate*) into a deterministic execution state machine operating directly alongside `MOD 16` (Macro Liquidity & Yields), `MOD 17` (Session Handoffs), `MOD 22` (Net Fed Liquidity), `MOD 23` (MOVE Index & Credit Spreads), `MOD 34` (AEGIS-3 Impulse Defense), `MOD 44` (Calendar Liquidity Vacuums), and `MOD 57` (DST Session Clock & Volatility Calibration).
**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** November–December 2026 Federal Reserve & US Macro Release Cycle — Hot October CPI (`Nov 10, 2026`, `08:30 ET`) driving US 10Y yields to `5.129%` and `MOVE` to `92.44`, followed by the `Nov 18` FOMC Minutes (`19:00 UTC`), `Nov 25` Core PCE (`13:30 UTC`), `Dec 4` NFP (`13:30 UTC`), and the back-to-back **`Dec 8–9` FOMC + SEP Dot Plot (`Dec 9, 19:00 UTC`) & `Dec 10` November CPI (`13:30 UTC`)** Double-Trinity cluster.
**Governance Posture:** Scheduled macro event gate (`ZERO_STANDALONE_DIRECTIONAL_SIGNALS`). Controls pre-event entry freezes, `T-0` algorithmic whip blackouts, and post-print fixed-income authentication before `MOD 18` can authorize spot capital.

#### 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Mandatory Pre-Execution Filters)

*   **Circuit Breaker 1 — The `T-0` First-Candle Whip Ban (CB-01):** Never buy or sell the initial `15m` or `1H` candle following an FOMC statement, SEP dot-plot release, CPI, Core PCE, or NFP print. Algorithmic headline readers routinely trigger `1.5%–4.0%` stop-hunt wicks in both directions—and FOMC press conferences (`T+30m` to `T+90m`) frequently reverse the `14:00 ET` statement spike completely. Enforce a non-negotiable `T-0` to `T+4h` execution blackout.
*   **Circuit Breaker 2 — Fixed-Income Lead Supremacy (CB-02):** In institutional macro regimes, US Treasury yields (`US10Y`), bond volatility (`MOVE` per `MOD 23`), and the US Dollar Index (`DXY` per `MOD 16`) dictate the true direction of liquidity `1` to `6` hours before crypto derivatives settle. If BTC spikes higher after a macro print while `US10Y` jumps `> +10 bps` and `DXY` surges `> +0.8%`, classify the BTC spike as a synthetic offshore perp short-squeeze (`MOD 03C`) and **veto all long entries**.
*   **Circuit Breaker 3 — Compound / Back-to-Back Macro Cluster Lockout (CB-03):** Whenever two Tier-1 macro releases occur within `36 hours` of each other (e.g., **Wednesday, Dec 9, 2026 FOMC + SEP Dot Plot at `19:00 UTC`** followed `18.5 hours` later by **Thursday, Dec 10, 2026 November CPI at `13:30 UTC`**), merge both Trinity gates into a single continuous **96-Hour Compound Lockout**. Do not deploy fresh risk between the two prints.
*   **Circuit Breaker 4 — SEP Dot-Plot & Terminal-Rate Override (CB-04):** On quarterly FOMC meetings (`March`, `June`, `September`, `December`), an "unchanged" rate decision is **not** neutral if the Summary of Economic Projections (`SEP`) median dot plot shifts the forward terminal rate higher by `>= +25 bps` or removes expected cuts. Treat a hawkish dot-plot shift identically to a surprise rate hike (`GATE_RED_100_CASH_LOCK`).
*   **Circuit Breaker 5 — Upstream Structural Vacuum Veto (CB-05):** Even if a macro release prints decisively dovish (`US10Y` drops `>= -15 bps`, `DXY` falls), `MOD 58` cannot authorize `0.75%` full risk if an active `MOD 44` liquidity drain (such as the Nov 19 Anthropic IPO / Nov 30 MSCI rebalance window), `ICDM >= 60` (`MOD 31`), or `MOD 36` telemetry staleness remains active.

#### 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C)

#### 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Arbitration & Anti-Paralysis Guardrails)

*   **Circuit Breaker 1 — Strict `min(risk_cap)` Composition (`CB-01`):** Whenever two or more gating modules authorize different maximum risk percentages (e.g., `MOD 16` permits `0.75%`, `MOD 17` `< 200D SMA` caps at `0.375%`, and `MOD 58` `TRINITY_LOCK` enforces `0.00%`), the compiler MUST take the mathematical minimum (`min(0.75%, 0.375%, 0.00%) = 0.00%`). No lower-ranked bullish signal can average up or dilute a higher-ranked veto.
*   **Circuit Breaker 2 — No Stop-Compression To Fake `Net R >= 3.0` (`CB-02`):** When `MOD 12` (Garman-Klass volatility) and `MOD 57` (calendar/session multiplier) widen the required structural stop distance, causing `MOD 37`'s post-slippage `Net R` to fall below `3.0`, **the trade MUST be aborted (`0.00%` risk)**. Never artificially tighten a stop-loss inside volatility noise to force `Net R >= 3.0` on paper.
*   **Circuit Breaker 3 — Domestic Fiat Spot Over Offshore Perp Supremacy (`CB-03`):** Inside Pillar 2 and Pillar 3, whenever an offshore `USDT` perp metric (rising OI, negative funding squeeze, or Binance-only breakout) conflicts with **Domestic USD Spot CVD (`MOD 05`/`MOD 07`)**, **Coinbase Premium (`MOD 19`)**, or **US Spot ETF primary flows (`MOD 38`)**, the domestic institutional USD spot signal unconditionally wins.
*   **Circuit Breaker 4 — Anti-Capitulation `CIRCUIT_LOCK` Exception (`CB-04`):** `MOD 34` (`AEGIS-3` `CSU-34B` `CIRCUIT_LOCK`) is the *only* protective lock designed to **block panic selling** rather than buying. Once forced liquidations reach terminal exhaustion (`> 95th` percentile `4H` flush + `-4%` drawdown in `< 3.5h`), `CIRCUIT_LOCK` vetoes remaining market sell slices so the engine never dumps inventory at the exact cascade trough.
*   **Circuit Breaker 5 — Fail-Closed Deadlock Resolution (`CB-05`):** If two peer modules at the same precedence rank produce an unresolved contradiction not explicitly cleared by positive USD Spot CVD and `MOD 54` bar-close confirmation, `MOD 60` declares `CONFLICT_DEADLOCK` and enforces `100% CASH LOCK` (`0.00%` risk) until the next hourly evaluation cycle.

# PROJECT CORETEX-BTC BRAIN — MODULE 61: DATA HUB INGESTION, HYGIENE GATE & DESK-LITE VERDICT COMPILATION PROTOCOL

**Waterfall Tier:** Tier D (Control-Plane Telemetry Ingestion, Data Hygiene Audit, 5-Function Synthesis & Workstation Verdict Interface) — governs how AI reasoning instances (Gemini, Hermes Agent, or Workstation Co-Pilots) parse consolidated `TAB [D: DATA HUB]` telemetry packets and compile auditable, zero-hallucination verdicts alongside `MOD 36` (Telemetry Trust & Determinism Attestation), `MOD 45` (Executive Bottom-Line HUD), `MOD 54` (500/600/500 Kline Lookback Contract), and `MOD 60` (7-Rank Veto Precedence Ladder).
**Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Codified from the `TAB [D: DATA HUB]` Consolidated Data-Only Telemetry Report schema (`Sections 1–8`, SQLite closed-`4H` trajectory archive, `500`-bar `1D/4H/1H` swing structure & S/R clusters, and closed OHLCV klines) paired with the **CORTEX BTC DESK LITE v1.0** 3-step execution playbook.
**Governance Posture:** Pure analytical & compilation protocol (`ZERO_STANDALONE_STRATEGY_DRIFT`). Enforces strict data hygiene, non-fabrication of external metrics, and deterministic routing into `100% USDT CASH — NO ENTRY` or `ACTIVE SPOT LONG` (`<= 0.75%` max portfolio risk, `1.0x` unleveraged spot only).

#### 2. Protective Circuit Breakers (Workstation Hygiene & Anti-Hallucination Guardrails)

*   **Circuit Breaker 1 — Mandatory Pre-Scoring Data Hygiene Gate (`CB-01`):** Never compute a directional regime or execution posture before auditing the `Coverage` header (`OK / UNAVAILABLE / STALE / PROXY/STATIC`). Every missing feed (`UNAVAILABLE`) or proxy metric (`[PROXY]`) must be explicitly logged in Section 2 of the verdict and treated as non-confirming (`MOD 36` `DATA UNAVAILABLE` honesty).
*   **Circuit Breaker 2 — Headless Snapshot vs. Live Stream Disambiguation (`CB-02`):** Scheduled GitHub Actions or one-shot cron runs execute a single REST warmup (`Warm:YES`) without a persistent WebSocket daemon. When `WebSocket Feeds & Warmup` displays `STALE (no tick)` alongside `Warm:YES` and a current `Closed 4H Anchor` timestamp, classify it as an expected headless snapshot—not a corrupted REST feed (`MOD 36 M2` partial-feed awareness).
*   **Circuit Breaker 3 — Partial-Venue OI & Proxy-ETF Quarantine (`CB-03`):** Whenever `Cross-Exch Perp OI` or Section `7` archive rows display `[... X/3 PARTIAL_VENUE]` (`< 3/3` exchanges), ban any deleveraging or short-squeeze claim derived from OI contraction. Similarly, when primary `US Spot ETF Flow (Farside)` is `UNAVAILABLE` and only `IBIT+FBTC [PROXY]` volume/flow is available, downgrade ETF confirmation to `PROXY/CAUTION` (`MOD 31 M7`) until primary settlement data arrives (`MOD 38`).
*   **Circuit Breaker 4 — Below-`1D SMA 200` & `ADX < 20` Cash Shield (`CB-04`):** Enforce **`100% USDT CASH — NO ENTRY`** whenever `1D` closes below `1D SMA 200` (`MOD 17` / `MOD 57 M4`) while `4H` exhibits an active `DOWNTREND (LH/LL)` or broken structure, when `ADX < 20` / `TRANSITIONAL` chop prevails, or when any `MOD 60` `Rank 0–4` veto (`MOD 44` IPO/MSCI vacuum, `MOD 58` Trinity Lock, `ICDM >= 60`) is active.
*   **Circuit Breaker 5 — Dual-Viewport Rendering Bridge (`CB-05`):** When operating in desktop/workstation mode, output the full **6-Section Workstation Audit Contract** (`Step 3`). When operating on mobile Telegram (`Samsung Galaxy A55`) or generating automated hourly cron alerts, compress the compiled verdict strictly into **`MOD 45` Template `45A` or `45B` HUD** without dropping any `DATA UNAVAILABLE` flags.

#### 3. Core Conditional Signal Units (Condition A -> Outcome B -> Rule C)

##### CSU-61A: Step 1 — Mandatory Data Hygiene Gate (`Coverage` & Feed Integrity Audit)
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    1. Operator or automated pipeline supplies a consolidated `TAB [D: DATA HUB]` telemetry report.
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Control-Plane Lens:** Raw multi-tab telemetry mixes live REST endpoints, daily on-chain/macro prints, proxy volume estimates, and headless WebSocket flags. Scoring without hygiene filtering produces false confidence or false outage alarms.
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Action:** Execute the 5-point Hygiene Check before any market synthesis:
        1. Log exact `OK / UNAVAILABLE / STALE / PROXY/STATIC` counts.
        2. Check `Warm:YES` to separate expected headless `WS STALE (no tick)` from true REST staleness.
        3. Verify `[BN+BYB+OKX 3/3]` venue quorum on Cross-Exch OI (quarantine if `< 3/3`).
        4. Confirm `90D Pct` is read as `0–100` rank, `30D VA Pos%` against Macro 30D VAH/VAL, and `ATR(14)` per matching timeframe (`1D/4H/1H`).
        5. Lock strict zero-hallucination boundary (zero external prices or unlogged metrics permitted).

##### CSU-61B: Step 2 — 5-Function Deterministic Synthesis Pipeline
*   **Trigger Condition A (The Telemetry Scan):**
    1. `CSU-61A` Data Hygiene Gate completes and isolates valid vs. degraded feeds.
*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**
    *   **Architectural Lens:** Sequential evaluation across all five core pillars (`MOD 18` + `MOD 60` precedence ladder) prevents single-indicator bias (such as mistaking a `1H` bounce for a `1D` trend reversal).
*   **Execution Rule C (The Non-Negotiable Action):**
    *   **Action:** Execute the 5 deterministic functions in strict order:
        1. **Function 1 — Multi-Timeframe Regime Classifier:** Integrate Section `1` (`1D SMA 200` & slope, `1D/4H` EMAs, `ADX(14)`, `RSI(14)`), Section `6` (`Hurst H`, `VCR 72H`), Section `7A` (`1D/4H/1H` swing structure & latest `BOS`), and Section `7B` closed OHLCV bars -> classify into `UPTREND`, `DOWNTREND`, `RANGE-BOUND`, or `TRANSITIONAL`.
        2. **Function 2 — Spot Flow & Liquidity Inspector:** Evaluate Section `2` (`15m/1h/4h/24h` Spot vs. Perp CVD, `z_S / z_F / D`, `15m` delta/stacks, `Coinbase Premium $/%/Z`, `L2 Depth ±1%/±2%`, `OBI`, `Spread`, `30D VA Pos%`, `SLSP`) + Section `6` (`4H A_SP` Lévy area) + Section `7A` S/R clusters -> classify true spot accumulation vs. distribution or passive absorption.
        3. **Function 3 — Derivatives & Squeeze Risk Profiler:** Evaluate Section `3` (`BN OI`, `3/3` Cross-Exch OI, `OI Δ%` & Quad, `8H/Ann/90D Pct` funding, `Perp Basis`, `DVOL`, `RV20`, `GK`, `25d Skew`, `Upper/Lower Liq Magnets`, `Pull Ratio`, `LSCE`, `Liquidations`) -> quantify leverage fragility and squeeze asymmetry.
        4. **Function 4 — Macro, On-Chain & ETF Gatekeeper:** Synthesize Section `4` (`MVRV`, `SOPR`, `Exchange Netflow/Reserves`, `USDT+USDC` `7D/30D` net issuance, `Farside` / `IBIT+FBTC` proxy, `MTF MACD`) + Section `5` (`SOFR`, `ON RRP`, `HYG/LQD`, `DXY`, `US10Y`, `TIPS Real`, `SPX/NDX`, `VIX`, `Brent`, `BTC-SPX Corr`, `F&G`, `Manual Overrides`) + active `MOD 44`/`58` calendar gates -> assign macro backdrop (`Tailwind`, `Neutral`, or `Headwind`).
        5. **Function 5 — Spot-Only Risk & Decision Arbiter:** Apply `MOD 60` `Ranks 0–4` vetoes and `CB-04` gates. If `1D < 1D SMA 200` + `4H DOWNTREND`, or `ADX < 20` / `TRANSITIONAL`, or multi-gate conviction fails, enforce **`100% USDT CASH — NO ENTRY`**. Otherwise size `ACTIVE SPOT LONG` strictly at `<= 0.75%` portfolio risk (`1.0x` spot only, `Net R >= 3.0` per `MOD 37`).

##### CSU-61C: Step 3 — 6-Section Workstation Output Contract & `MOD 45` Mobile Bridge

*   **Observed Outcome B (The Mechanical Trajectory):**

        4. **DERIVATIVES, LIQUIDITY & MACRO CONTEXT** (OI venue-integrity check, funding `90D Pct`, options `25d` skew, liquidation magnets, macro/ETF/stablecoin headwind vs. tailwind).

# CORTEX-BTC v2.0
## Integrated Bidirectional BTC/USDT Spot Rotation Rulebook

**Document ID:** CORTEX-BTC-IBSR-001
**Version:** 2.0 — Integrated Architecture (Harmonized into Master Rulebook v9 as `MOD_62`)
**Status:** RESEARCH / DECISION-SUPPORT FRAMEWORK — UNVALIDATED
**Strategy:** Bidirectional BTC/USDT Spot Rotation
**Operational Engine:** DIBSE — Dual-Inventory Bidirectional Swing Engine v2.0
**Trading Instruments:** BTC and USDT only
**Execution Preference:** Limit orders (with `MOD 37` slippage-capped marketable limit allowance on `MOD 08A/11B` LVN breakouts)
**Leverage / Margin / Borrowing:** Prohibited (`1.0x` Unleveraged Spot Only)
**Futures / Perpetual Trading:** Prohibited
**Short Selling:** Prohibited
**Autonomous Order Execution:** Prohibited

---

# PART I — SYSTEM IDENTITY AND GOVERNING AUTHORITY

## Rule 1. Core Objective

CORTEX-BTC is a spot-only BTC/USDT portfolio rotation framework.

Its objective is to manage the relative allocation between BTC and USDT through evidence-based accumulation, distribution and re-accumulation decisions.

The system must be capable of:

1. Accumulating BTC using existing USDT when downside conditions become favourable.
2. Holding BTC when market structure remains constructive.
3. Reducing BTC exposure into USDT when credible distribution or downside-risk conditions emerge.
4. Re-accumulating BTC when a subsequent opportunity satisfies the required conditions.
5. Remaining in either asset when evidence is insufficient.

The objective is not to predict every market movement.

The objective is to improve portfolio management through disciplined conversion decisions, risk control and measurable performance.

## Rule 2. Architecture and Authority

CORTEX-BTC is the governing framework (`MOD 01–61`).

DIBSE (`MOD 62`) is its operational trading engine.

The authority hierarchy is:

1. CORTEX-BTC — overall governance (`MOD 36` & `MOD 60` 7-Rank Veto Precedence).
2. Market Regime and Risk Controller — market conditions and risk restrictions (`MOD 16`, `MOD 31`, `MOD 33`).
3. Bullish Accumulation Module — BTC purchase opportunities (`MOD 01–15`, `MOD 19–27`, `MOD 30`, `MOD 42–43`, `MOD 54`, `MOD 59`).
4. Bearish Distribution Module — BTC-to-USDT conversion opportunities (`MOD 02B`, `MOD 06B`, `MOD 13C`, `MOD 15B`, `MOD 27B`, `MOD 41`).
5. Portfolio Allocation Controller — final exposure and trade eligibility (`MOD 18`, `MOD 37`, `MOD 62`).
6. DIBSE — inventory accounting, conversion-cycle tracking and order lifecycle management.
7. Human operator — final execution authorisation.

**Critical rule:** DIBSE must never override CORTEX-BTC governance.

Neither DIBSE trading loop may independently authorise a trade.

The Portfolio Allocation Controller is the single final decision authority.

## Rule 3. Non-Negotiable Trading Restrictions

The system shall never:

- Borrow BTC or USDT.
- Use margin or leverage.
- Open futures or perpetual positions.
- Create synthetic short exposure.
- Sell BTC that is not owned and available.
- Spend USDT that is not available.
- Trade the protected long-term BTC inventory (`Sleeve B`, `MOD 32/46`).
- Execute orders autonomously.

Selling owned BTC for USDT is a spot inventory conversion, not short selling.

---

# PART II — DIBSE OPERATIONAL INTEGRATION

## Rule 4. Dual-Inventory Architecture

DIBSE maintains two strategic inventories:

- BTC inventory.
- USDT inventory.

Both must be monitored independently.

The system must report:

- Available BTC.
- Reserved BTC in open sell orders.
- Available USDT.
- Reserved USDT in open buy orders.
- Protected long-term BTC (`Sleeve B`).
- Tactical BTC inventory (`Sleeve A`).
- Realised trading gains and losses.
- Unrealised portfolio valuation changes.
- Fees and execution costs.

No asset may be allocated to more than one outstanding order.

## Rule 5. Capital Boundary

Only explicitly authorised tactical trading capital may be used by DIBSE.

The DIBSE source rulebook proposes an initial 20% active sleeve (`Sleeve A`):

- 10% of total portfolio capital in USDT.
- 10% of total portfolio capital in BTC.
- Remaining 80% outside the active sleeve (`Sleeve B` Sovereign Multi-Sig Reserve).

This is a proposed allocation parameter, not a proven optimal allocation or an automatic instruction.

CORTEX must require explicit operator approval before activating or changing the sleeve.

The protected reserve must never be appropriated automatically.

## Rule 6. Inventory Floors

Minimum BTC and USDT inventory floors must be configured before deployment.

Every proposed conversion must be checked against:

- Available inventory.
- Reserved inventory.
- Protected holdings.
- Active sleeve boundaries.
- Maximum permitted exposure.
- Existing open orders.

Neither asset may be assumed to be a perfect substitute for the other.

## Rule 7. Two Independent Research Loops

DIBSE operates two opportunity-detection loops.

**Loop A — USDT → BTC → USDT**

Purpose: Increase realised USDT through favourable BTC accumulation and subsequent distribution.

**Loop B — BTC → USDT → BTC**

Purpose: Increase BTC quantity through favourable BTC distribution and subsequent re-accumulation.

Each loop maintains its own setup, conversion-cycle ledger and performance record.

They may monitor opportunities simultaneously.

However, both loops must submit proposed trades to the same Portfolio Allocation Controller.

The controller must reconcile:

- Aggregate BTC exposure.
- Aggregate USDT exposure.
- Available and reserved inventory.
- Conflicting orders.
- Market regime.
- Risk restrictions.
- Portfolio-level trade intent.

Two loops must never become two independent trading authorities.

---

# PART III — MARKET DATA AND EVIDENCE GOVERNANCE

## Rule 8. Evidence Hierarchy

CORTEX prioritises observable market behaviour over indicator-based assumptions.

The principal evidence categories are:

1. Spot CVD and actual spot-market participation (`MOD 05`, `MOD 07`, `MOD 19`).
2. Spot volume and trade delta (`MOD 06`, `MOD 08`).
3. Order-flow imbalance and order-book liquidity (`MOD 21`, `MOD 37`).
4. Footprint and auction behaviour (`MOD 11`).
5. Higher-timeframe market structure (`MOD 17`, `MOD 54`).
6. Open interest and funding as positioning context (`MOD 01–04`).
7. Confirmed liquidation events (`MOD 03`, `MOD 51`).
8. Futures CVD as contextual evidence (`MOD 05`, `MOD 09`).
9. Macro liquidity and broader market conditions (`MOD 16`, `MOD 22–24`, `MOD 29`, `MOD 44`, `MOD 58`).

No single indicator may independently authorise a trade.

## Rule 9. Data Classification

Every material data input must carry a status (aligned with `MOD 36` & `MOD 61`):

- VERIFIED
- PARTIALLY VERIFIED
- PROXY
- STALE
- UNAVAILABLE
- CONFLICTING

The system must distinguish directly observed exchange data from estimated, model-derived or aggregated values.

Source timestamps, collection timestamps and calculation timestamps must be recorded.

## Rule 10. Data Integrity Gate

Before a trade can become eligible, the system must verify:

- Source freshness (`MOD 36` SLA).
- Timestamp consistency.
- Instrument consistency.
- Correct market type.
- Calculation validity.
- Cross-source reconciliation where required (3-venue quorum per `MOD 36` / `MOD 54`).
- No material unresolved data conflicts.

If critical evidence is stale, unavailable or conflicting:

**Decision: DATA HALT / NO NEW TRADE.**

Existing inventory accounting must continue.

Working orders must be reassessed under the order-risk procedure.

---

# PART IV — MARKET REGIME CONTROLLER

## Rule 11. Regime Classification

CORTEX classifies the BTC environment into four operating regimes mapped directly to `MOD 33` (Unified 5-Regime Master Radar) and `MOD 16` (Macro Permission Gates):

**CONSTRUCTIVE** (`MOD 33 REGIME_1 / REGIME_2` + `GATE_GREEN`)

Higher-timeframe structure is supportive, with credible demand and no confirmed major deterioration.

**MIXED / CAUTIOUS** (`MOD 33 REGIME_3` + `GATE_AMBER`)

Evidence is conflicting, structure is unclear or market participation is inconsistent.

**DEFENSIVE** (`MOD 33 REGIME_5` or `< 200D SMA`)

Meaningful structural weakness, distribution or deterioration in market participation is present.

**DISLOCATION / EXTREME RISK** (`MOD 33 REGIME_4` or `ICDM >= 60` / `MOD 60 Rank 0–3 Veto`)

Severe downside displacement, disorderly liquidity, confirmed deleveraging or major market disruption is present.

## Rule 12. Regime Effects

The regime changes the permissions and requirements of both DIBSE loops.

- CONSTRUCTIVE: Accumulation is permitted when its independent conditions pass (up to `0.75%` portfolio risk cap). Distribution requires credible evidence of weakening.
- MIXED / CAUTIOUS: Reduce trade frequency and require stronger confirmation (`0.375%` portfolio risk cap).
- DEFENSIVE: Tighten accumulation requirements and permit defensive BTC-to-USDT conversion when distribution evidence is confirmed.
- DISLOCATION / EXTREME RISK: Suspend new risk-taking until data and market conditions stabilise (`0.00%` new risk / `100% Cash Lock`). Human review is required.

A regime classification alone does not authorise a trade.

---

# PART V — BULLISH ACCUMULATION MODULE

## Rule 13. Accumulation Objective

Identify opportunities to convert available USDT into BTC when downside selling pressure weakens and the market demonstrates credible recovery or acceptance.

## Rule 14. Required Evidence

The module evaluates:

- Higher-timeframe support or structural reference (`MOD 11`, `MOD 13`, `MOD 17`, `MOD 59`).
- Downside liquidity sweep or meaningful support test (`MOD 35`, `MOD 42`).
- Spot CVD and spot participation (`MOD 05`, `MOD 07`, `MOD 19`).
- Selling exhaustion or absorption (`MOD 06`, `MOD 21`).
- Price displacement relative to negative delta (`MOD 06`, `MOD 08`).
- Order-book and bid-replenishment behaviour (`MOD 06`, `MOD 37`).
- Open-interest and liquidation context (`MOD 01`, `MOD 03`, `MOD 51`).
- Volume-profile and auction recovery (`MOD 11`).
- Reclaim of a relevant structural boundary (`MOD 11A/B`, `MOD 54`).

A lower wick, oversold indicator or liquidation heatmap alone cannot authorise accumulation.

## Rule 15. Accumulation Gate

A proposed buy must pass:

1. Data Integrity Gate (`MOD 36` / `MOD 61`).
2. Market Regime Gate (`MOD 16` / `MOD 33` / `MOD 60`).
3. Structural Context Gate (`MOD 17` / `MOD 54`).
4. Selling-Pressure Assessment (`MOD 03` / `MOD 06`).
5. Spot-Market Confirmation (`MOD 05` / `MOD 07` / `MOD 19`).
6. Reversal or Reclaim Confirmation (`MOD 11` / `00C` bar-count rules).
7. Entry Price and Execution Assessment (`MOD 12` / `MOD 37` `Net R >= 3.0`).
8. Inventory and Capital Boundary Check (`DIBSE` Sleeve A USDT floor & reservations).
9. Portfolio Risk Check (`MOD 18` `<= 0.75%` or `0.375%` equity risk cap).
10. Final Portfolio Allocation Controller approval.

If a mandatory gate fails, no BUY authorisation is issued.

---

# PART VI — BEARISH DISTRIBUTION MODULE

## Rule 16. Distribution Objective

Identify opportunities to convert a permitted quantity of existing tactical BTC into USDT when credible evidence indicates weakening upside progress, distribution or increased downside risk.

This module manages BTC exposure. It does not create short exposure.

## Rule 17. Required Evidence

The module evaluates:

- Higher-timeframe resistance or supply (`MOD 11`, `MOD 13C`, `MOD 41`).
- Failed upside auction or rejection (`MOD 11C`).
- Weakening spot participation (`MOD 05C`, `MOD 07B`).
- Deteriorating Spot CVD (`MOD 05`, `MOD 19`).
- Significant buying volume without sustained upside progress (`MOD 06B` Passive Limit Ask-Wall Capping).
- Evidence of supply absorption or distribution (`MOD 15B`, `MOD 39`).
- Failed reclaim or loss of relevant market structure (`MOD 54`).
- Order-book and liquidity changes (`MOD 21`, `MOD 34`).
- Derivative positioning as supporting context (`MOD 02B`, `MOD 04B`, `MOD 27B`).

A resistance touch, high RSI reading or liquidation heatmap target alone cannot authorise distribution.

## Rule 18. Distribution Classification

The module must distinguish:

**PROFIT-TAKING**

A partial conversion at a favourable price without evidence of major structural deterioration (`MOD 18` Stage 1 50% harvest).

**DEFENSIVE REDUCTION**

A reduction in BTC exposure because market structure or participation has weakened (`MOD 34A` `PRE_TRIM` 25%–40% reduction).

**FULL DEFENSIVE EXIT**

A permitted exit of tactical BTC inventory when severe conditions justify it (`MOD 18` Stage 2 / `MOD 60 M2` upstream veto exit).

A full exit must never automatically include protected long-term BTC holdings (`Sleeve B`).

## Rule 19. Distribution Gate

A proposed sell must pass:

1. Data Integrity Gate (`MOD 36` / `MOD 61`).
2. Market Regime Gate (`MOD 16` / `MOD 33` / `MOD 60`).
3. Structural Context Gate (`MOD 11` / `MOD 54`).
4. Upside Weakness Assessment (`MOD 02B` / `MOD 06B` / `MOD 13C`).
5. Spot-Market Confirmation (`MOD 05C` / `MOD 19`).
6. Distribution or Breakdown Confirmation (`MOD 11C` / `MOD 41`).
7. Sell Price and Execution Assessment (`MOD 37` slippage audit).
8. BTC Inventory Floor Check (`DIBSE` Sleeve A tactical BTC floor & protected Sleeve B isolation).
9. Portfolio Risk Check (`MOD 18` / `MOD 34` anti-capitulation sell-freeze check).
10. Final Portfolio Allocation Controller approval.

If a mandatory gate fails, no SELL authorisation is issued.

---

# PART VII — PORTFOLIO ALLOCATION CONTROLLER

## Rule 20. Single Final Decision Authority

The Portfolio Allocation Controller reconciles both trading modules and determines the permitted BTC/USDT exposure within the authorised tactical sleeve (`Sleeve A`).

It must consider:

- Current portfolio allocation.
- Market regime.
- Accumulation evidence.
- Distribution evidence.
- Data integrity.
- Risk restrictions (`MOD 18`, `MOD 31`, `MOD 60`).
- Existing orders.
- Inventory availability.
- Protected capital (`Sleeve B`).
- Expected execution costs (`MOD 37`).

No module may bypass this controller.

## Rule 21. Exposure Research Bands (Tactical Sleeve A Scope)

The following are research allocation bands within the authorised tactical sleeve (`Sleeve A`), subject to `MOD 18` stop-risk sizing (`<= 0.75%` total portfolio equity risk):

| Tactical BTC | Tactical USDT | Classification |
|---:|---:|---|
| 0% | 100% | Fully defensive (`100% Cash Lock` within Sleeve A) |
| 25% | 75% | Defensive |
| 50% | 50% | Balanced (Baseline 10% BTC / 10% USDT split) |
| 75% | 25% | BTC-heavy |
| 100% | 0% | Fully allocated to BTC (within Sleeve A cap) |

The controller may also maintain intermediate allocations when explicitly supported by the approved policy.

These bands apply strictly to the authorised tactical sleeve scope, never automatically to protected holdings (`Sleeve B`).

## Rule 22. Permitted Decisions

The controller may issue:

- ACCUMULATE BTC
- HOLD BTC
- REDUCE BTC
- DEFENSIVE EXIT
- RE-ACCUMULATE BTC
- REMAIN USDT
- NO TRADE
- DATA HALT
- HUMAN REVIEW

A decision must state its permitted size, rationale, evidence, invalidation conditions and expiry.

## Rule 23. Conflict Resolution

If accumulation and distribution modules produce conflicting signals:

1. Reconcile the evidence via `MOD 60` (Global 7-Rank Veto Precedence Ladder & Intra-Pillar Tie-Breakers).
2. Check whether the apparent conflict is caused by different timeframes (`MOD 10` / `MOD 54`).
3. Check data quality and timestamps (`MOD 36` / `MOD 61`).
4. Reassess the market regime (`MOD 33`).
5. Evaluate existing exposure and open orders.

If the conflict remains unresolved:

**Decision: NO TRADE / HOLD CURRENT INVENTORY (`MOD 60 M1 CONFLICT_DEADLOCK`).**

The system must not average contradictory signals into an artificial trade recommendation.

---

# PART VIII — POSITION SIZING AND RISK

## Rule 24. Conversion Limits

Every conversion must have a maximum permitted quantity or notional value.

Sizing must respect:

- Available asset inventory.
- Inventory floors.
- Active sleeve limits (`Sleeve A` 20% portfolio boundary).
- Existing reservations.
- Market regime.
- Portfolio risk policy (`MOD 18` `0.75%` / `0.375%` / `0.00%` stop-loss risk ceiling).
- Expected fees and slippage (`MOD 37`).

Negative inventory and borrowed balances are prohibited.

## Rule 25. Risk Budget

Risk-budget parameters are governed by `MOD 18` and `MOD 37`:

- `GATE_GREEN / CONSTRUCTIVE`: Maximum `0.75%` total portfolio equity risk per trade.
- `GATE_AMBER / MIXED`: Maximum `0.375%` total portfolio equity risk per trade.
- `GATE_RED / DISLOCATION`: `0.00%` new risk (`100% Cash Lock`).

Position size must not be derived from conviction scores alone.

The system must distinguish the amount of capital converted (`notional sleeve allocation`) from the amount of portfolio risk accepted (`stop-distance equity risk`).

## Rule 26. No Automatic Averaging Down

A continuing breakdown is not an accumulation signal.

Every additional buy requires a separately valid setup or a predefined, validated scale-in plan (`MOD 51B` staggered absorption ladder, with combined risk strictly `<= 0.75%`).

## Rule 27. No Automatic Rebuying Higher

After BTC has been converted into USDT, the system must not chase progressively higher BTC prices simply to restore exposure.

USDT may remain unconverted until the next qualified re-accumulation opportunity.

Opportunity cost must be reported.

## Rule 28. Inventory Protection

Long-term core BTC (`Sleeve B`) and tactical trading inventory (`Sleeve A`) must be separately identified.

Only authorised tactical inventory may be used by DIBSE.

No conversion cycle may silently consume protected holdings.

---

# PART IX — DIBSE EXECUTION AND ORDER MANAGEMENT

## Rule 29. Limit-Order Mandate

Limit orders are the preferred execution method (with `MOD 37` slippage-capped marketable limit orders permitted only when `MOD 08A` / `MOD 11B` confirms an LVN fast-traverse breakout).

Every proposed order must specify:

- Instrument (`BTC/USDT` or `BTC/USD`).
- Side (`BUY` or `SELL`).
- Limit price.
- Quantity.
- Notional value.
- Expected fees.
- Inventory impact.
- Setup reference (`MOD` & `CSU` identifier).
- Expiry.
- Cancellation conditions.

Unconstrained market orders are prohibited.

## Rule 30. Order Lifecycle

DIBSE must track:

`PROPOSED → APPROVED → SUBMITTED → WORKING → PARTIALLY FILLED → FILLED`

Alternative terminal states:

`CANCELLED / EXPIRED / REJECTED`.

An order must not be treated as filled or cancelled without confirmed exchange status.

## Rule 31. Partial Fills and Reservations

Only executed quantities are recognised as converted inventory.

Unfilled quantities remain reserved until confirmed cancellation, expiry or other verified terminal status.

The same asset must never be allocated twice.

## Rule 32. Order Reassessment

Working orders must be reassessed when:

- The original setup is invalidated.
- Market structure materially changes.
- The reference price becomes obsolete.
- Data integrity fails (`MOD 36`).
- Inventory constraints change.
- The order expires.

Critical data failure requires immediate order review.

---

# PART X — CONVERSION-CYCLE ACCOUNTING

## Rule 33. Loop A Accounting (`USDT → BTC → USDT`)

For `USDT → BTC → USDT`, record:

- Original USDT committed.
- BTC acquired.
- BTC subsequently sold.
- Gross and net USDT proceeds.
- Fees.
- Net realised USDT gain or loss.
- Cycle duration.

## Rule 34. Loop B Accounting (`BTC → USDT → BTC`)

For `BTC → USDT → BTC`, record:

- Original BTC sold.
- Gross and net USDT proceeds.
- BTC repurchased.
- Fees.
- Net BTC quantity gained or lost.
- Cycle duration.

The principal Loop B metric is:

$$\Delta\text{BTC} = \text{BTC}_{\text{repurchased}} - \text{BTC}_{\text{sold}}$$

A positive result indicates an increase in BTC quantity after accounting for the cycle's costs.

## Rule 35. Whole-Portfolio Performance

A profitable conversion cycle does not automatically mean the overall strategy is successful.

CORTEX must report:

1. Total portfolio value in USDT.
2. Total BTC quantity.
3. Realised and unrealised performance.
4. Trading fees and slippage.
5. Performance of the tactical sleeve (`Sleeve A`).
6. Performance of protected holdings (`Sleeve B`).
7. Opportunity cost.
8. Performance against relevant benchmarks.

---

# PART XI — FAILURE HANDLING AND OPERATIONAL STATES

## Rule 36. Data Failure

When critical data is unavailable, stale or conflicting (`MOD 36` / `MOD 61`):

- Suspend new trade approvals.
- Reassess working orders.
- Preserve inventory accounting.
- Reconcile exchange balances.
- Require verified recovery before resumption (`2 consecutive clean cycles` per `MOD 36`).

## Rule 37. Exchange Failure

Monitor:

- Connectivity.
- Order submission.
- Order status.
- Partial fills.
- Cancellation acknowledgement.
- Balance reconciliation.
- Fee deductions.
- Unexpected rejections.

No unverified execution state may be assumed.

## Rule 38. System States

CORTEX must support:

- `NORMAL`
- `WATCH`
- `SETUP`
- `CONFIRMED`
- `ELIGIBLE`
- `ORDER WORKING`
- `PARTIALLY FILLED`
- `FILLED`
- `RECONVERSION WATCH`
- `CYCLE COMPLETED`
- `INVALIDATED`
- `DATA HALT`
- `HUMAN REVIEW`

Each DIBSE loop may maintain its own operational state, but the Portfolio Allocation Controller governs the final portfolio decision.

---

# PART XII — VALIDATION AND PERFORMANCE REQUIREMENTS

## Rule 39. No Edge Claims Without Evidence

Neither CORTEX nor DIBSE may be described as profitable or possessing a verified trading edge until validated (`MOD 52` Rolling 30-Trade Expectancy Audit).

The integrated architecture is a strategy specification, not proof of profitability.

## Rule 40. Separate Module Testing

Test independently:

1. Bullish Accumulation Module.
2. Bearish Distribution Module.
3. DIBSE Loop A.
4. DIBSE Loop B.
5. Portfolio Allocation Controller.
6. Combined CORTEX-DIBSE strategy.

This identifies which component contributes value and which creates losses or unnecessary turnover.

## Rule 41. Benchmark Requirements

At minimum, compare against:

- 100% BTC buy-and-hold.
- 100% USDT.
- 50% BTC / 50% USDT.
- The proposed integrated rotation strategy.

Include realistic fees, slippage, order non-fills and execution delays.

## Rule 42. Validation Method

Testing must include:

- Historical backtesting.
- Multiple market regimes.
- Out-of-sample testing.
- Walk-forward validation.
- Parameter sensitivity.
- Drawdown analysis.
- Trade-frequency and turnover analysis.
- Missed-upside analysis.
- Avoided-downside analysis.
- BTC-denominated and USDT-denominated performance.

Prevent look-ahead bias, survivorship bias and overfitting.

## Rule 43. Required Performance Metrics

Report:

- Net return.
- CAGR.
- Maximum drawdown.
- Profit factor.
- Expectancy (`MOD 52`).
- Win rate.
- Average gain and loss.
- Trading costs.
- Turnover.
- Time in BTC and USDT.
- BTC quantity gained or lost ($\Delta\text{BTC}$).
- USDT balance gained or lost.
- Benchmark-relative performance.

A strategy must not be declared successful based only on its win rate or the number of completed conversion cycles.

---

# PART XIII — FINAL GOVERNING DOCTRINE

1. CORTEX-BTC is the sole governing framework.
2. DIBSE (`MOD 62`) is the operational dual-inventory engine.
3. BTC and USDT are both strategic inventories.
4. All trading is spot-only (`1.0x`).
5. No leverage, borrowing, margin or short selling.
6. Accumulation and distribution require independent evidence.
7. One Portfolio Allocation Controller governs all trade permissions.
8. Inventory reservations must be reconciled across both loops.
9. Protected BTC holdings (`Sleeve B`) cannot be silently used.
10. Data integrity failures prevent new trade authorisation (`MOD 36`).
11. No trade is compulsory.
12. Limit orders require explicit prices, quantities and expiry rules.
13. Every conversion cycle must be independently accounted for.
14. All portfolio performance must include costs and opportunity costs.
15. No profitability or edge claim without independent validation (`MOD 52`).
16. Human authorisation remains mandatory.

---

# FINAL SYSTEM DIRECTIVE

CORTEX-BTC governs the complete BTC/USDT rotation process.

DIBSE (`MOD 62`) supplies the operational machinery to manage BTC and USDT inventories, identify and track two-way conversion cycles, and control the order lifecycle.

The accumulation and distribution loops may operate independently as research and opportunity-detection modules, but all proposed trades must pass through one central Portfolio Allocation Controller.

The system must be willing to accumulate BTC, reduce BTC, hold either asset or abstain entirely.

Its objective is not maximum trading activity.

Its objective is disciplined portfolio rotation, preservation of capital, measurable improvement in BTC and USDT holdings, and demonstrable performance after all costs.

**CORTEX governs. DIBSE operates. The Portfolio Allocation Controller decides. The human operator authorises execution.**

### PART I — EPISTEMOLOGY & SYSTEM PURPOSE
#### Rule 1. Core Mandate
Spot price levels are non-stationary across macro cycles, whereas derivatives leverage mechanics, carry stress, volatility clustering, and order-book liquidity exhibit recurring structural behavior.
**MOD 63 (CORTEX-RHYME)** answers one empirical question before tactical capital is deployed:
> *"When Bitcoin's derivatives positioning, carry cost, volatility, and order-book flow looked statistically identical to the present hour, how did forward return distributions over +4h, +24h, and +72h deviate from unconditional background noise (`baseline_all_hours`)?"*

#### Rule 2. Strict Non-Prediction Doctrine
1. MOD 63 is **not** a directional price oracle and **never** issues standalone `ACCUMULATE` or `REDUCE` commands.
2. MOD 63 functions exclusively as a **Layer 1 Base-Rate Prior** within the 3-Layer Decision Hierarchy:
   - **Layer 1 — Base Rate Prior (MOD 63):** Establishes conditional return expectancy, directional win-rate lift ($\Delta\text{Up-Share}$), and tail-risk asymmetry.
   - **Layer 2 — Live Mechanism (MOD 01–30 / PCF3 / AEGIS-3):** Confirms active forcing functions (Spot CVD absorption, passive limit walls, liquidation cascades).
   - **Layer 3 — Hard Structure & Sizing (MOD 11 / MOD 18 / MOD 37 / MOD 62):** Defines structural invalidation, Net $R \ge 3.0$ geometry, and Sleeve A inventory allocation.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### PART II — MATHEMATICAL & STATISTICAL SAFEGUARDS
#### Rule 3. Elimination of the Five Analogue Pathologies
To prevent false statistical confidence, any telemetry ingested from `cortex-rhyme` must enforce five mandatory constraints:
1. **Causal Rolling Standardization (Zero Lookahead):** Feature Z-scores must be calculated strictly over trailing $[t-168, t]$ windows. Future data must never enter mean or standard deviation denominators.
2. **Mahalanobis Covariance Whitening:** Because perpetual funding and perp-to-spot basis are strongly collinear, distances must be computed using the regularized inverse covariance matrix $\Sigma_{\text{causal}}^{-1}$ estimated strictly prior to the 72h embargo window, preventing carry stress from being double-counted.
3. **24-Hour De-Clustering:** Candidate matches are ranked by ascending Mahalanobis distance $D_M$. A candidate at hour $i$ is admitted only if $|i - j| \ge 24\text{ hours}$ for every already-selected analogue $j$, ensuring $k$ independent historical regimes rather than autocorrelated clones of a single event.
4. **72-Hour Forward Embargo:** All observations within 72 hours of `as_of_utc` are excluded from the candidate pool so every analogue carries a complete, finalized +4h, +24h, and +72h forward return vector.
5. **Baseline-Relative Conditioning:** Every conditional statistic (`after_similar_setups`) must be evaluated directly against the unconditional full-archive distribution (`baseline_all_hours`). Raw win rates without baseline lift (`up_share_lift_pts`) are invalid for decision support.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### PART III — INTEGRATION WITH MOD 18 SIZING & MOD 62 (DIBSE v2.0)
#### Rule 4. Sample Quality Gating
- **`THIN` Grade ($N < 10$ or $\bar{D}_M > 2.35$):** Indicates an unprecedented structural outlier or macro shock lacking historical precedent. MOD 63 assigns **0.0x sizing modifier** (neutral/no-opinion). Neither Loop A nor Loop B may cite a `THIN` readout to justify a trade.
- **`MODERATE` Grade:** May serve as supporting context only when aligned with Pillar 2/3 live order-flow confirmation.
- **`SOLID` Grade ($N \ge 18$, $\bar{D}_M \le 1.65$, $\text{history\_days} \ge 90$):** Eligible to modulate sizing conviction within MOD 18 and MOD 62 risk ceilings.

#### Rule 5. Tail-Asymmetry & Net-3.0R Veto Check
MOD 63 reports `median_up_pct` (median gain when $R > 0$) separately from `median_down_pct` (median loss when $R < 0$) and computes `tail_skew_ratio = |median_up_pct / median_down_pct|`.
- **Asymmetric Cliff Alert:** Even when `up_share` sits near 50%, if `tail_skew_ratio < 0.40` on the 24h or 72h horizon (indicating severe historical downside tail risk relative to modest upside), MOD 62's Portfolio Allocation Controller must tighten Loop A accumulation requirements and verify that hard structural stops (MOD 11/54) strictly preserve **Net $R \ge 3.0$** after MOD 37 slippage.
- **DIBSE Loop Alignment:**
  - **Loop A (`USDT → BTC → USDT`):** Favored when `sample_quality == SOLID`, 24h/72h `up_share_lift_pts >= +10.0 pts`, and `tail_skew_ratio >= 1.0` alongside confirmed spot absorption (MOD 05/06).
  - **Loop B (`BTC → USDT → BTC`):** Favored when `sample_quality == SOLID`, 24h/72h `up_share_lift_pts <= -10.0 pts` (or severe downside tail skew), and Pillar 2/3 distribution signals (MOD 02B/06B/13C) are active.

#### Rule 6. Data Integrity & Staleness SLA (MOD 36 Bridge)
- If `meta.status != "live"`, `as_of_utc` exceeds **2.5 hours** of staleness, or the endpoint is unreachable, MOD 63 transitions to **`UNAVAILABLE / STALE`** and contributes zero weight. Per MOD 36, stale advisory telemetry does not halt valid primary venue feeds unless primary hub quorum fails, but fabricated or estimated analogue numbers are strictly prohibited.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

### FINAL DIRECTIVE
**MOD 63 measures how loaded the historical spring is—never whether the trigger has been pulled. Base rates inform sizing and asymmetry; live order flow and hard structure govern execution.**

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 65: MEDIA DE-NOISING, NARRATIVE TRIAGE & SIGNAL HYGIENE PROTOCOL

**Waterfall Tier:** Tier D (Meta-Governance, Pre-Ingestion Filter & Signal Hygiene Gateway) — cross-verified with MOD 36 (Telemetry Trust & Staleness SLA), MOD 60 (Precedence Ladder), and MOD 61 (Data Hub Ingestion & Hygiene Gate).
**Provenance & Empirical Benchmark Anchor:** Codified from market cycle down-trend field studies (*"Bitcoin in a Downtrend: How to Read Liquidity, ETF Flows, and Sentiment Without Getting Fooled"*).
**Governance Posture:** Mandatory Pre-Ingestion Firewall. Audits external media, influencer commentary, and vendor models. Has zero standalone trade authorization authority (cannot buy or sell); prevents unvetted narratives from triggering premature capital deployment or panic selling.

---

## 0. Machine-Readable State & Routing Block

```yaml
module_id: "MOD_65"
entry_type: "SIGNAL_HYGIENE_GATEWAY"
waterfall_tier: "TIER_D_META_GOVERNANCE"
title: "Media De-Noising, Narrative Triage & Signal Hygiene Protocol"

mod_60_registration:
  precedence_rank: "PRE_INGESTION_FILTER_TO_MOD_61"
  subordination: "Yields unconditionally to all MOD 60 Rank 0-4 vetoes. Cannot authorize standalone trade execution."

audit_criteria:
  timeframe_isolation: true
  closed_candle_confirmation: true
  primary_source_traceability: true
  falsifiability_defined: true
  risk_and_sizing_bounded: true

quarantine_threshold: ">= 2 FAILED_QUESTIONS -> ASSIGN 0.00% EXECUTION WEIGHT"
```

---

### PART I — THE 5-QUESTION MEDIA AUDIT GATE

Before any external analysis, YouTube/X narrative, or vendor model is ingested into operational planning, it must answer:

1. **Timeframe Separation:** Are higher-timeframe trends (1D/1W), tactical swings (4H/1H), and multi-year DCA horizons strictly isolated, or is the author shifting goalposts?
2. **Deterministic Trigger:** Does the analysis specify an exact candle-close confirmation and a hard invalidation price, or is it relying on unclosed intraday wicks?
3. **Primary-Source Verification:** Are ETF flows, derivatives, and sentiment numbers traceable to primary feeds (Farside, CoinGlass, Glassnode, FRED), or are they unverified estimates?
4. **Falsifiability Condition:** Does the analyst state an explicit *"I am wrong if..."* invalidation level alongside an alternative scenario?
5. **Risk & Sizing Completeness:** Does the thesis account for position sizing (capped at ≤ 0.75% portfolio risk), execution friction, and liquidity depth, or is it merely entertainment?

*Rule:* If an external thesis fails **two or more** questions, it is classified as entertainment, assigned **0.00% execution weight**, and quarantined.

---

### PART II — ANTI-TRAP CIRCUIT BREAKERS

* **CB-01 (Model-vs.-Order-Book Translation Filter):** Forbids treating liquidation heatmaps as actual resting limit orders. Long-lookback heatmaps (1Y–2Y) contain ghost bands from closed positions rather than active liquidity. True liquidity must be verified via Volume Profile (MOD 11) and Limit Order Book depth (MOD 06).
* **CB-02 (Single-Candle CHoCH & Moving-Goalpost Ban):** Prohibits declaring a trend reversal off a single candle close above a lower high. A valid trend reversal mandates a strict two-step sequence: **CHoCH close → Higher Low → Higher High close** on the governing timeframe.
* **CB-03 (Oversold RSI Extension vs. Timing Filter):** Treats daily RSI < 30 as a measure of downside momentum and extension, **never as a calendar bottom date or a standalone buy trigger**.
* **CB-04 (Backward-Looking Sentiment & Reflexive ETF Filter):** Treats Extreme Fear readings (≤ 15) and single-day ETF inflow pauses as historical lag and T+1 feedback loops rather than forward capital turnarounds.
* **CB-05 (Multi-Horizon Frame Separation Lock):** Strictly decouples long-term sovereign accumulation (Sleeve B) from intraday tactical execution (Sleeve A). Long-term "generational value" narratives are never permitted to override active daily cash locks.

---

### FINAL DIRECTIVE
**MOD 65 separates market mechanics from market theater. Models estimate, oscillators measure extension, sentiment reflects the past, and only multi-step candle-close structure backed by verified spot flow authorizes capital.**

# PROJECT CORTEX-BTC — MODULE 65.1: LIVE YOUTUBE TRANSCRIPT TRIAGE AUDIT (2026-11-19)

**Waterfall Tier:** Tier D Operational Case Study & Runbook (Companion to MOD 65).
**Empirical Benchmark:** Live media audit of market commentary during the 19 November 2026 market downturn.
**Core Objective:** Operationalize the 5-Question Media Audit Gate against live retail influencer commentary to demonstrate how to de-noise media narratives during active liquidation regimes.

---

## 1. Influencer Claim Audit & Telemetry Cross-Examination

### Claim 1: "Supply in Profit at ~50% marks a guaranteed macro cycle bottom."
* **MOD 65 Triage:** Fails Question 3 (Primary-Source Verification) and Question 4 (Falsifiability).
* **Telemetry Cross-Check:** Per MOD 56 CB-02, during secular bear transitions, Supply in Profit routinely compresses from 50% down to 35%–42% before true generational capitulation prints.
* **Verdict:** Entertainment / narrative bias. 0.00% execution weight.

### Claim 2: "The 3% drop in Open Interest proves leverage has completely reset."
* **MOD 65 Triage:** Fails Question 2 (Deterministic Trigger) and Question 5 (Risk Completeness).
* **Telemetry Cross-Check:** Per MOD 03 CSU-03B, a -3% OI contraction is an intra-trend partial shakeout, not a full deleveraging flush (which requires -8% to -17% OI contraction).
* **Verdict:** Premature knife-catching risk. Stand down in cash.

### Claim 3: "Liquidation heatmaps show massive resting buy orders down at $51,000."
* **MOD 65 Triage:** Trips MOD 65 CB-01 (Model-vs.-Order-Book Translation Filter).
* **Telemetry Cross-Check:** Heatmaps are derivative models subject to vendor lookback adjustments, not real resting limit orders on exchange books. True structural support sits at the $58,000 Volume Profile HVN (MOD 11).
* **Verdict:** Invalid execution thesis.

---

## 2. Desk Directives & Operational Conclusion
* External commentary failing the MOD 65 filter must never trigger tactical swing allocation.
* When macro gates (MOD 16 / MOD 64) enforce GATE_RED, all retail commentary proposing dip-buys is quarantined.
